#+title: Ekko -- one org file, one project
#+author: generated from the Ekko tree; the tree is generated from this file
#+description: The whole Ekko terminal multiplexer as a single literate org file.
#+options: toc:2 num:nil

* Overview
Ekko is a terminal multiplexer written in Common Lisp with a small C platform
shim.  This file is the entire project: every source, example, script and
packaging file appears exactly once, as one src block with an explicit
`:tangle` target.  Tangling writes the tree back in place, at the repo root.

    sh scripts/tangle.sh

is the bootstrap (`scripts/tangle.sh` is itself plain, not tangled -- a block
for it would be circular).  `scripts/check-tangle.sh` tangles into a temp dir
and compares, and `nix flake check` builds `checks.<system>.tangle`, which
copies the tree twice, re-tangles one copy, and compares it file by file
against the other: a generated file edited by hand behind org's back fails the
check.

What is deliberately *not* here: `flake.lock` (nix writes it), `.gitignore`,
`.gitattributes`, the `GOAL.md` / `PROGRESS.md` / `README.md` prose, and
`docs/` with its captured binary evidence.

A block line that org would otherwise read as structure -- a line starting with
`*`, or a literal `#+end_src` -- carries one leading comma; org-babel-tangle
strips exactly that comma again on the way out, so the emitted bytes equal the
original bytes.

* The build system
Defined first, because everything else is reached through them.

** ekko.asd
The ASDF system graph: `ekko` depends on `ekko/core` and `ekko/builtins`; the
component lists below are also the load order used by this file.

#+begin_src lisp :tangle ekko.asd :mkdirp yes
(asdf:defsystem "ekko"
  :description "Ekko terminal multiplexer build spine"
  :version "0.1.0"
  :depends-on ("ekko/core" "ekko/builtins"))

(asdf:defsystem "ekko/core"
  :depends-on ("sb-posix" "ekko/runtime")
  :serial t
  :components ((:file "src/package")
               (:file "src/cli")))

(asdf:defsystem "ekko/runtime"
  :depends-on ("sb-posix" "ekko/scene" "ekko/client" "ekko/text" "ekko/extensions" "ekko/layout")
  :serial t
  :components ((:file "src/platform") (:file "src/assets") (:file "src/vt") (:file "src/history")
               (:file "src/graphics")
               (:file "src/wire") (:file "src/worker") (:file "src/layout-policy")
               (:file "src/store") (:file "src/views") (:file "src/server")
               (:file "src/commands") (:file "src/menus") (:file "src/animations")
               (:file "src/windows") (:file "src/selection") (:file "src/daemon")
               (:file "src/client")))

(asdf:defsystem "ekko/extensions"
  :depends-on ("ekko/text")
  :components ((:file "src/extensions")))
(asdf:defsystem "ekko/text"
  :components ((:file "src/text-width")))
(asdf:defsystem "ekko/layout"
  :description "Public pure split-tree geometry helpers"
  :components ((:file "src/layout")))
(asdf:defsystem "ekko/builtins"
  :depends-on ("ekko/extensions" "ekko/layout")
  :serial t
  :components ((:file "examples/profiles/zellij-pane")
               (:file "examples/profiles/zellij-bindings")
               (:file "examples/profiles/desktop-style")
               (:file "examples/profiles/layouts") (:file "src/builtins")))

(asdf:defsystem "ekko/scene"
  :description "Pure clipping and rational source transforms"
  :components ((:file "src/geometry")))

(asdf:defsystem "ekko/client"
  :description "Attachment ownership and bounded presentation transactions"
  :components ((:file "src/presentation")))

(asdf:defsystem "ekko/graphics-demo"
  :description "Synthetic Kitty graphics experiment; not a multiplexer"
  :depends-on ("ekko/scene" "ekko/client")
  :components ((:file "src/graphics-demo")))
#+end_src

** flake.nix
The whole nix surface: the packages (default plus the performance, workspace,
benchmark, kitty-oracle and zellij-visual helpers), the apps, the checks --
including the `tangle` check that fails when a generated file was hand-edited --
and the dev shell.

#+begin_src nix :tangle flake.nix :mkdirp yes
{
  description = "Ekko v2 terminal multiplexer";
  inputs.nixpkgs.url = "github:NixOS/nixpkgs/nixos-25.05";
  inputs.terminal-browser = {
    url = "github:zenbu-labs/terminal-browser/cce10b6131d15bf46a3e4b8dc827e0544ff7fc65";
    flake = false;
  };
  outputs = { self, nixpkgs, terminal-browser }:
    let
      systems = [ "x86_64-linux" ];
      forEachSystem = f: nixpkgs.lib.genAttrs systems (system: f nixpkgs.legacyPackages.${system});
    in {
      packages = forEachSystem (pkgs: {
        browser-source = pkgs.stdenvNoCC.mkDerivation {
          name = "ekko-terminal-browser-source";
          src = terminal-browser;
          dontConfigure = true;
          dontBuild = true;
          installPhase = "cp -R . $out";
          dontFixup = true;
        };
        performance = pkgs.writeShellScriptBin "ekko-performance" ''
          exec ${pkgs.python3}/bin/python ${./scripts/performance.py} ${self.packages.${pkgs.system}.default}/bin/ekko "$@"
        '';
        workspace = pkgs.writeShellScriptBin "ekko-workspace" ''
          export EKKO_WORKSPACE_MODE=shell-browser
          exec ${self.packages.${pkgs.system}.benchmark}/bin/ekko-benchmark "$@"
        '';
        benchmark = pkgs.writeShellApplication {
          name = "ekko-benchmark";
          runtimeInputs = [ pkgs.nix pkgs.coreutils ];
          text = ''
            export EKKO_BINARY=${self.packages.${pkgs.system}.default}/bin/ekko
            export EKKO_BROWSER_SOURCE=${self.packages.${pkgs.system}.browser-source}
            export EKKO_KITTY=${pkgs.kitty}/bin/kitty
            export EKKO_FONTCONFIG=${pkgs.makeFontsConf { fontDirectories = [ pkgs.dejavu_fonts ]; }}
            exec ${pkgs.bash}/bin/bash ${./scripts/benchmark.sh} "$@"
          '';
        };
        kitty-oracle = import ./nix/kitty-oracle.nix { inherit pkgs; };
        zellij-visual = import ./nix/zellij-visual.nix { inherit pkgs; };
        default = pkgs.stdenv.mkDerivation {
          pname = "ekko";
          version = "0.1.0";
          src = pkgs.lib.fileset.toSource {
            root = ./.;
            fileset = pkgs.lib.fileset.unions [
              ./ekko.asd
              (pkgs.lib.fileset.fileFilter (file: file.hasExt "lisp") ./examples/profiles)
              (pkgs.lib.fileset.fileFilter (file: file.hasExt "lisp" || file.hasExt "c") ./src)
              ./scripts/build.sh ./scripts/build.lisp ./scripts/build-demo.lisp
              ./scripts/smoke.sh
              ./scripts/generate-text-width.py ./scripts/text-width-oracle.rs
            ];
          };
          nativeBuildInputs = [ pkgs.sbcl ];
          buildInputs = [ pkgs.zlib ];
          dontConfigure = true;
          # A saved SBCL core is appended to the ELF runtime. Stripping loses it.
          dontStrip = true;
          dontPatchELF = true;
          doCheck = true;
          buildPhase = ''
            export XDG_CACHE_HOME=$TMPDIR/ekko-build-cache
            export EKKO_SOURCE_DIR=$PWD
            export EKKO_OUTPUT=$PWD/ekko
            export EKKO_PLATFORM_LIBRARY=$out/lib/libekko-platform.so
            mkdir -p $out/lib
            cc -O2 -Wall -Wextra -Werror -fPIC -shared src/platform.c -o $out/lib/libekko-platform.so -lutil -lz
            sh scripts/build.sh
            EKKO_BUILD_SYSTEM=ekko/core EKKO_OUTPUT=$PWD/ekko-bare sh scripts/build.sh
            EKKO_OUTPUT=$PWD/ekko-graphics-demo sbcl --no-userinit --no-sysinit \
              --non-interactive --load scripts/build-demo.lisp
          '';
          checkPhase = ''
            sh scripts/smoke.sh "$PWD/ekko"
          '';
          installPhase = ''
            install -Dm755 ekko $out/bin/ekko
            install -Dm755 ekko-bare $out/bin/ekko-bare
            install -Dm755 ekko-graphics-demo $out/bin/ekko-graphics-demo
          '';
        };
      });
      apps = forEachSystem (pkgs: {
        performance = {
          type = "app";
          meta.description = "Measure deterministic PTY workloads and emit JSON";
          program = "${self.packages.${pkgs.system}.performance}/bin/ekko-performance";
        };
        workspace = {
          type = "app";
          meta.description = "Open an interactive shell beside terminal-browser in Ekko panes";
          program = "${self.packages.${pkgs.system}.workspace}/bin/ekko-workspace";
        };
        benchmark = {
          type = "app";
          meta.description = "Open terminal-browser and local terminal-slack in two Ekko panes";
          program = "${self.packages.${pkgs.system}.benchmark}/bin/ekko-benchmark";
        };
        demo-graphics = {
          type = "app";
          meta.description = "Write the synthetic two-pane graphics fixture to a file";
          program = "${self.packages.${pkgs.system}.default}/bin/ekko-graphics-demo";
        };
        test-kitty = {
          type = "app";
          meta.description = "Isolated real Kitty red-pixel precursor (not P0 acceptance)";
          program = "${self.packages.${pkgs.system}.kitty-oracle}/bin/ekko-kitty-red-pixel";
        };
        zellij-visual = {
          type = "app";
          meta.description = "Private Wayland Kitty visual capture precursor";
          program = "${self.packages.${pkgs.system}.zellij-visual}/bin/ekko-zellij-visual";
        };
        default = { type = "app"; meta.description = "Ekko terminal multiplexer CLI"; program = "${self.packages.${pkgs.system}.default}/bin/ekko"; };
      });
      checks = forEachSystem (pkgs: {
        # Enforced byte identity: ekko.org is the source of every file it
        # tangles, so a generated file that was hand-edited behind org's back
        # must fail this check.  It copies the tree twice, re-tangles one copy
        # from ekko.org, and compares the result against the untouched copy.
        tangle = pkgs.runCommand "ekko-tangle" {
          nativeBuildInputs = [ pkgs.emacs-nox ];
          input = pkgs.lib.fileset.toSource {
            root = ./.;
            fileset = pkgs.lib.fileset.unions [
              ./ekko.org ./ekko.asd ./flake.nix ./src ./examples ./scripts ./nix
            ];
          };
        } ''
          cp -r $input expected
          cp -r $input work
          chmod -R u+w expected work
          export HOME=$TMPDIR
          cd work
          emacs --batch --eval '(progn (require (quote org)) (org-babel-tangle-file "ekko.org"))'
          cd ..
          status=0
          for rel in ekko.asd flake.nix $(cd expected && find src examples scripts nix -type f | sort); do
            if cmp -s "expected/$rel" "work/$rel"; then
              echo "same     $rel"
            else
              echo "CHANGED  $rel -- the committed file is not what ekko.org tangles to" >&2
              status=1
            fi
          done
          if [ "$status" -ne 0 ]; then
            echo "ekko.org is the source; re-tangle it (scripts/tangle.sh) rather than editing generated files" >&2
            exit 1
          fi
          touch $out
        '';
        text-width = pkgs.runCommand "ekko-text-width" {
          nativeBuildInputs = [ pkgs.python3 pkgs.rustc pkgs.sbcl pkgs.gnutar pkgs.stdenv.cc ];
          unicodeWidthCrate = pkgs.fetchurl {
            url = "https://static.crates.io/crates/unicode-width/unicode-width-0.1.10.crate";
            sha256 = "c0edd1e5b14653f783770bce4a4dabb4a5108a5370a5f5d8cfe8710c361f6c8b";
          };
        } ''
          mkdir crate
          tar -xf $unicodeWidthCrate -C crate
          python ${./scripts/generate-text-width.py} \
            crate/unicode-width-0.1.10 --rustc rustc --output generated.lisp
          cmp generated.lisp ${./src/text-width.lisp}
          rustc --crate-name unicode_width --crate-type lib --edition=2018 \
            crate/unicode-width-0.1.10/src/lib.rs -o libunicode_width.rlib
          rustc ${./scripts/text-width-oracle.rs} \
            --extern unicode_width=$PWD/libunicode_width.rlib -o rust-oracle
          ./rust-oracle > rust-widths
          sbcl --noinform --non-interactive --load ${./src/text-width.lisp} \
            --eval '(loop for cp from 0 to #x10ffff do
                      (unless (<= #xd800 cp #xdfff)
                        (format t "~D~%" (ekko/text:display-width (code-char cp)))))' \
            > lisp-widths
          cmp rust-widths lisp-widths
          printf 'exhaustive Unicode scalar widths match unicode-width 0.1.10\n' > $out
        '';
        build = self.packages.${pkgs.system}.default;
        packaged-smoke = pkgs.runCommand "ekko-packaged-smoke" {} ''
          sh ${./scripts/smoke.sh} ${self.packages.${pkgs.system}.default}/bin/ekko
          touch $out
        '';
      });
      devShells = forEachSystem (pkgs: {
        default = pkgs.mkShell { packages = [ pkgs.sbcl pkgs.zlib pkgs.python3 ]; };
      });
    };
}
#+end_src

* src/ -- the runtime, from package to the graphics experiment
Rearranged by hand into a reading order: package, platform, screen model, then daemon,
then attachment, then the pure geometry and the extension API.

** src/package.lisp
The `ekko` package: the only exported entry points.

#+begin_src lisp :tangle src/package.lisp :mkdirp yes
(defpackage #:ekko
  (:use #:cl)
  (:export #:main #:version))
#+end_src

** src/platform.lisp
Foreign-function layer over the C platform shim (spawn, poll, pty, signals).

#+begin_src lisp :tangle src/platform.lisp :mkdirp yes
(defpackage #:ekko/platform
  (:use #:cl #:sb-alien)
  (:export #:initialize #:spawn #:read-fd #:write-fd #:poll-fds #:terminal-size
           #:resize #:raw #:restore #:close-fd #:reap #:signal-group #:server-signals
           #:listen-local #:connect-local #:accept-local #:lock-file #:compress-bytes
           #:decompress-bytes #:octets #:text-bytes #:bytes-text #:now #:checked #:*asset-bytes* #:nonblock))
(in-package #:ekko/platform)

(defparameter *library* (or (uiop:getenv "EKKO_PLATFORM_LIBRARY")
                            (namestring (merge-pathnames "libekko-platform.so"
                                         (asdf:system-source-directory "ekko")))) )
(defvar *loaded* nil)
(defun initialize ()
  (unless *loaded* (load-shared-object *library*) (setf *loaded* t))
  (%ignore-pipe))
(defun checked (value &optional (operation "OS operation"))
  (when (minusp value) (error "~A failed (errno ~D)" operation (- value))) value)
(defun octets (size) (make-array size :element-type '(unsigned-byte 8)))
(defun text-bytes (text) (sb-ext:string-to-octets text :external-format ':utf-8))
(defun bytes-text (bytes) (sb-ext:octets-to-string bytes :external-format ':utf-8))
(defun now () (/ (get-internal-real-time) internal-time-units-per-second))

(define-alien-routine ("ek_close" close-fd) int (fd int))
(define-alien-routine ("ek_nonblock" nonblock) int (fd int))
(define-alien-routine ("ek_raw" raw) int (fd int))
(define-alien-routine ("ek_restore" restore) int)
(define-alien-routine ("ek_reap" reap) int (pid int))
(define-alien-routine ("ek_signal_group" signal-group) int (pid int) (sig int))
(define-alien-routine ("ek_server_signals" server-signals) void)
(define-alien-routine ("ek_ignore_pipe" %ignore-pipe) void)
(define-alien-routine ("ek_lock" lock-file) int (path c-string))
(define-alien-routine ("ek_listen" listen-local) int (path c-string))
(define-alien-routine ("ek_connect" connect-local) int (path c-string))
(define-alien-routine ("ek_accept" accept-local) int (fd int))
(define-alien-routine ("ek_resize" resize) int (fd int) (cols int) (rows int) (x int) (y int))
(define-alien-routine ("ek_size" %size) int (fd int) (values (* int)))
(define-alien-routine ("ek_poll" %poll) int (fds (* int)) (events (* int)) (count int) (timeout int))
(define-alien-routine ("ek_spawn" %spawn) int
  (argv (* (* char))) (cols int) (rows int) (x int) (y int)
  (directory c-string) (environment (* (* char))) (pid (* int)))
(define-alien-routine ("ek_read" %read) int (fd int) (data system-area-pointer) (size int))
(define-alien-routine ("ek_write" %write) int (fd int) (data system-area-pointer) (size int))
(define-alien-routine ("ek_inflate" %inflate) int (data system-area-pointer) (size int) (out system-area-pointer) (capacity int))
(define-alien-routine ("ek_deflate" %deflate) int (data system-area-pointer) (size int) (out system-area-pointer) (capacity int))
(define-alien-routine ("ek_snapshot_shm" snapshot-shm) int (name c-string) (path c-string) (size int))

(defun spawn (argv cols rows cw ch &key directory (environment nil environment-p))
  "Spawn ARGV on a PTY. DIRECTORY is a nonempty path string or NIL to inherit.
ENVIRONMENT is a full list of NAME=VALUE strings. Omit it to inherit; pass NIL
explicitly for an empty environment. Neither option changes the parent."
  (dolist (strings (list argv environment))
    (unless (handler-case (list-length strings) (type-error () nil))
      (error "Spawn argv and environment must be proper, finite lists"))
    (unless (every (lambda (value) (and (stringp value) (not (find #\Null value)))) strings)
      (error "Spawn argv and environment must contain strings without NUL")))
  (unless (and argv (plusp (length (first argv))))
    (error "Spawn argv must name a program"))
  (unless (or (null directory)
              (and (stringp directory) (plusp (length directory)) (not (find #\Null directory))))
    (error "Spawn directory must be a nonempty string without NUL, or NIL"))
  (dolist (entry environment)
    (let ((separator (position #\= entry)))
      (unless (and separator (plusp separator))
        (error "Invalid environment entry ~S: expected NAME=VALUE" entry))))
  ;; One pointer block holds both null-terminated vectors. Each encoded string
  ;; has explicit foreign ownership until the exec-error pipe closes.
  (let* ((entries (append argv (list nil) (when environment-p (append environment (list nil)))))
         (args (make-alien (* char) (length entries)))
         (initialized 0))
    (unwind-protect
         (progn
           (loop for entry in entries for i from 0 do
             (setf (deref args i) (if entry (make-alien-string entry :external-format ':utf-8)
                                          (sb-sys:int-sap 0)))
             (incf initialized))
           (with-alien ((pid int))
             (let ((fd (checked (%spawn args cols rows (* cols cw) (* rows ch) directory
                                       (if environment-p (addr (deref args (1+ (length argv))))
                                           (sb-sys:int-sap 0))
                                       (addr pid))
                                (format nil "exec ~A" (first argv)))))
               (values fd pid))))
      (dotimes (i initialized) (free-alien (deref args i)))
      (free-alien args))))
(defun terminal-size (fd)
  (with-alien ((values (array int 4)))
    (checked (%size fd (cast (addr values) (* int))) "terminal size")
    (loop for i below 4 collect (deref values i))))
(defun poll-fds (items timeout)
  (let ((count (handler-case (list-length items) (type-error () nil))))
    ;; The C ABI accepts an int count; there is no separate peer/pane limit.
    (check-type count (unsigned-byte 31))
    (let ((storage (make-alien int (* 2 count))))
      (unwind-protect
           (let ((events (addr (deref storage count))))
             (loop for (fd . event) in items for i from 0 do
               (setf (deref storage i) fd (deref events i) event))
             (let ((n (%poll storage events count timeout)))
               (when (= n -4) (return-from poll-fds nil))
               (checked n "poll")
               (loop for (fd . event) in items for i from 0
                     unless (zerop (deref events i)) collect (cons fd (deref events i)))))
        (free-alien storage)))))
(defun read-fd (fd buffer)
  (sb-sys:with-pinned-objects (buffer)
    (%read fd (sb-sys:vector-sap buffer) (length buffer))))
(defun write-fd (fd buffer &optional (start 0) (count (- (length buffer) start)))
  (sb-sys:with-pinned-objects (buffer)
    (%write fd (sb-sys:sap+ (sb-sys:vector-sap buffer) start) count)))
(defun decompress-bytes (input capacity)
  (let ((output (octets capacity)))
    (sb-sys:with-pinned-objects (input output)
      (unless (= capacity (%inflate (sb-sys:vector-sap input) (length input)
                                    (sb-sys:vector-sap output) capacity))
        (error "Invalid compressed image size or data"))) output))
(defun compress-bytes (input)
  (let ((output (octets (+ (length input) (ceiling (length input) 1000) 64))))
    (sb-sys:with-pinned-objects (input output)
      (subseq output 0 (checked (%deflate (sb-sys:vector-sap input) (length input)
                                        (sb-sys:vector-sap output) (length output)) "deflate")))))
#+end_src

** src/platform.c
The C shim itself: pty allocation, poll, terminal modes, signal forwarding.

#+begin_src c :tangle src/platform.c :mkdirp yes
#define _GNU_SOURCE
#include <errno.h>
#include <fcntl.h>
#include <limits.h>
#include <poll.h>
#include <pty.h>
#include <signal.h>
#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <sys/file.h>
#include <sys/ioctl.h>
#include <sys/mman.h>
#include <sys/socket.h>
#include <sys/stat.h>
#include <sys/un.h>
#include <sys/wait.h>
#include <termios.h>
#include <unistd.h>
#include <utmp.h>
#include <zlib.h>

static int result(int n) { return n < 0 ? -errno : n; }
int ek_close(int fd) { return result(close(fd)); }
int ek_nonblock(int fd) {
    int flags = fcntl(fd, F_GETFL);
    if (flags < 0) return -errno;
    return result(fcntl(fd, F_SETFL, flags | O_NONBLOCK));
}
int ek_read(int fd, void *data, int size) { return result(read(fd, data, size)); }
int ek_write(int fd, const void *data, int size) { return result(write(fd, data, size)); }
int ek_poll(int *fds, int *events, int count, int timeout) {
    if (count < 0 || (count && (!fds || !events))) return -EINVAL;
    if (!count) return result(poll(NULL, 0, timeout));
    /* calloc checks the input-sized allocation for multiplication overflow. */
    struct pollfd *items = calloc(count, sizeof(*items));
    if (!items) return -ENOMEM;
    for (int i = 0; i < count; i++) items[i] = (struct pollfd){fds[i], events[i], 0};
    int n = result(poll(items, count, timeout));
    if (n >= 0)
        for (int i = 0; i < count; i++) events[i] = items[i].revents;
    free(items);
    return n;
}
int ek_size(int fd, int *values) {
    struct winsize ws;
    if (ioctl(fd, TIOCGWINSZ, &ws) < 0) return -errno;
    values[0] = ws.ws_col; values[1] = ws.ws_row;
    values[2] = ws.ws_xpixel; values[3] = ws.ws_ypixel;
    return 0;
}
int ek_resize(int fd, int cols, int rows, int x, int y) {
    struct winsize ws = {rows, cols, x, y};
    return result(ioctl(fd, TIOCSWINSZ, &ws));
}
static struct termios saved;
static int saved_fd = -1, saved_flags, saved_output_flags;
int ek_raw(int fd) {
    if (saved_fd >= 0) return -EBUSY;
    if (tcgetattr(fd, &saved) < 0) return -errno;
    saved_flags = fcntl(fd, F_GETFL);
    saved_output_flags = fcntl(STDOUT_FILENO, F_GETFL);
    struct termios raw = saved;
    cfmakeraw(&raw);
    if (tcsetattr(fd, TCSANOW, &raw) < 0) return -errno;
    saved_fd = fd;
    ek_nonblock(STDOUT_FILENO);
    return ek_nonblock(fd);
}
int ek_restore(void) {
    if (saved_fd < 0) return 0;
    int n = tcsetattr(saved_fd, TCSANOW, &saved);
    fcntl(saved_fd, F_SETFL, saved_flags);
    fcntl(STDOUT_FILENO, F_SETFL, saved_output_flags);
    saved_fd = -1;
    return result(n);
}
int ek_spawn(char **argv, int cols, int rows, int x, int y,
             const char *directory, char **environment, int *pid_out) {
    if (!argv || !argv[0] || !pid_out) return -EINVAL;
    int master, slave, errors[2];
    struct winsize ws = {rows, cols, x, y};
    if (openpty(&master, &slave, NULL, NULL, &ws) < 0) return -errno;
    int n = ek_nonblock(master);
    if (n < 0 || fcntl(master, F_SETFD, FD_CLOEXEC) < 0) {
        int e = n < 0 ? -n : errno; close(master); close(slave); return -e;
    }
    if (pipe2(errors, O_CLOEXEC) < 0) {
        int e = errno; close(master); close(slave); return -e;
    }
    pid_t pid = fork();
    if (pid == 0) {
        close(errors[0]); close(master);
        if (login_tty(slave) < 0) goto failed;
        if (errors[1] != 3) {
            if (dup3(errors[1], 3, O_CLOEXEC) < 0) goto failed;
            close(errors[1]); errors[1] = 3;
        }
        if (close_range(4, UINT_MAX, 0) < 0) goto failed;
        sigset_t mask;
        sigemptyset(&mask);
        if (sigprocmask(SIG_SETMASK, &mask, NULL) < 0) goto failed;
        for (int s = 1; s < NSIG; s++) signal(s, SIG_DFL);
        if (directory && chdir(directory) < 0) goto failed;
        /* This fork owns its environment. execvp must search this PATH, not
         * the daemon's; execvpe would still search the calling environment. */
        if (environment) environ = environment;
        execvp(argv[0], argv);
failed:;
        int e = errno;
        ssize_t written;
        do { written = write(errors[1], &e, sizeof(e)); } while (written < 0 && errno == EINTR);
        _exit(127);
    }
    int e = errno;
    close(slave); close(errors[1]);
    if (pid < 0) { close(master); close(errors[0]); return -e; }
    int child_error = 0;
    ssize_t received;
    do { received = read(errors[0], &child_error, sizeof(child_error)); }
    while (received < 0 && errno == EINTR);
    e = received < 0 ? errno : (received == sizeof(child_error) && child_error > 0 ? child_error : EIO);
    close(errors[0]);
    if (received != 0) {
        close(master);
        /* A pipe read failure must not leave a running child or wait forever. */
        kill(pid, SIGKILL);
        while (waitpid(pid, NULL, 0) < 0 && errno == EINTR) {}
        return -e;
    }
    *pid_out = pid;
    return master;
}
int ek_reap(int pid) {
    int status;
    int n = waitpid(pid, &status, WNOHANG);
    if (n < 0) return -errno;
    if (!n) return -EAGAIN;
    return WIFEXITED(status) ? WEXITSTATUS(status) : 128 + WTERMSIG(status);
}
int ek_signal_group(int pid, int sig) { return result(kill(-pid, sig)); }
void ek_server_signals(void) { signal(SIGCHLD, SIG_DFL); signal(SIGPIPE, SIG_IGN); }
void ek_ignore_pipe(void) { signal(SIGPIPE, SIG_IGN); }
int ek_lock(const char *path) {
    int fd = open(path, O_CREAT | O_RDWR | O_CLOEXEC | O_NOFOLLOW, 0600);
    if (fd < 0) return -errno;
    if (flock(fd, LOCK_EX | LOCK_NB) < 0) { int e = errno; close(fd); return -e; }
    return fd;
}
static int address(struct sockaddr_un *addr, const char *path) {
    if (strlen(path) >= sizeof(addr->sun_path)) return -ENAMETOOLONG;
    memset(addr, 0, sizeof(*addr)); addr->sun_family = AF_UNIX;
    strcpy(addr->sun_path, path);
    return 0;
}
int ek_listen(const char *path) {
    struct sockaddr_un addr;
    int n = address(&addr, path);
    if (n < 0) return n;
    int fd = socket(AF_UNIX, SOCK_STREAM | SOCK_CLOEXEC | SOCK_NONBLOCK, 0);
    if (fd < 0) return -errno;
    if (bind(fd, (struct sockaddr *)&addr, sizeof(addr)) < 0 || listen(fd, SOMAXCONN) < 0) {
        int e = errno; close(fd); return -e;
    }
    chmod(path, 0600);
    return fd;
}
int ek_connect(const char *path) {
    struct sockaddr_un addr;
    int n = address(&addr, path);
    if (n < 0) return n;
    int fd = socket(AF_UNIX, SOCK_STREAM | SOCK_CLOEXEC, 0);
    if (fd < 0) return -errno;
    if (connect(fd, (struct sockaddr *)&addr, sizeof(addr)) < 0) {
        int e = errno; close(fd); return -e;
    }
    ek_nonblock(fd);
    return fd;
}
int ek_accept(int listener) {
    int fd = accept4(listener, NULL, NULL, SOCK_NONBLOCK | SOCK_CLOEXEC);
    if (fd < 0) return -errno;
    struct ucred peer;
    socklen_t size = sizeof(peer);
    if (getsockopt(fd, SOL_SOCKET, SO_PEERCRED, &peer, &size) < 0 || peer.uid != getuid()) {
        close(fd); return -EACCES;
    }
    return fd;
}
int ek_inflate(const void *input, int size, void *output, int capacity) {
    uLongf length = capacity;
    uLong consumed = size;
    int n = uncompress2(output, &length, input, &consumed);
    return n == Z_OK && consumed == (uLong)size ? (int)length : -EINVAL;
}
int ek_deflate(const void *input, int size, void *output, int capacity) {
    uLongf length = capacity;
    return compress2(output, &length, input, size, 1) == Z_OK ? (int)length : -EINVAL;
}

/* Take ownership of a POSIX graphics transfer, never keep a producer's
 * reusable buffer as retained scene state. Copy through a bounded stack buffer
 * into an exclusively created, daemon-owned regular file. */
int ek_snapshot_shm(const char *name, const char *path, int size) {
    if (name[0] != '/' || !name[1] || strchr(name + 1, '/') ||
        strlen(name) > 255 || size <= 0 || size > 32 * 1024 * 1024) return -EINVAL;
    int in = shm_open(name, O_RDONLY | O_CLOEXEC | O_NONBLOCK | O_NOFOLLOW, 0);
    if (in < 0) return -errno;
    struct stat st;
    int err = 0, out = -1;
    if (fstat(in, &st) < 0) err = errno;
    else if (!S_ISREG(st.st_mode) || st.st_uid != getuid() || st.st_size != size) err = EINVAL;
    if (err) { close(in); return -err; }
    /* Unlink before copying: a producer can immediately reuse the name without
     * changing this open object's identity. Never unlink a failed validation. */
    if (shm_unlink(name) < 0) { err = errno; close(in); return -err; }
    out = open(path, O_WRONLY | O_CREAT | O_EXCL | O_CLOEXEC | O_NOFOLLOW, 0600);
    if (out < 0) { err = errno; close(in); return -err; }
    char buffer[65536];
    int remaining = size;
    while (remaining > 0 && !err) {
        ssize_t n = read(in, buffer, remaining < (int)sizeof(buffer) ? remaining : (int)sizeof(buffer));
        if (n < 0 && errno == EINTR) continue;
        if (n <= 0) { err = n < 0 ? errno : EIO; break; }
        ssize_t done = 0;
        while (done < n) {
            ssize_t written = write(out, buffer + done, n - done);
            if (written < 0 && errno == EINTR) continue;
            if (written <= 0) { err = written < 0 ? errno : EIO; break; }
            done += written;
        }
        remaining -= n;
    }
    close(in);
    if (close(out) < 0 && !err) err = errno;
    if (err) unlink(path);
    return -err;
}
#+end_src

** src/assets.lisp
Reference-counted immutable snapshots shared by daemon, store and attachments.

#+begin_src lisp :tangle src/assets.lisp :mkdirp yes
(in-package #:ekko/platform)

;; The daemon owns immutable snapshots. Store entries and in-flight scenes each
;; own one reference; presentation acknowledgements release the latter.
(defstruct local-asset path size (references 1))
(defvar *asset-directory* nil)
(defvar *asset-sequence* 0)
(defvar *asset-bytes* 0)
(defconstant +asset-limit+ (* 256 1024 1024))

(defun asset-size (data)
  (if (local-asset-p data) (local-asset-size data) (length data)))
(defun retain-asset (data)
  (when (local-asset-p data) (incf (local-asset-references data))) data)
(defun release-asset (data)
  (when (and (local-asset-p data) (zerop (decf (local-asset-references data))))
    (delete-file (local-asset-path data))
    (decf *asset-bytes* (local-asset-size data))))
(defun snapshot-asset (name size)
  (unless (and *asset-directory* (<= (+ *asset-bytes* size) +asset-limit+)
               (<= 2 (length name) 255) (char= (char name 0) #\/)
               (not (find #\/ name :start 1)) (not (find #\Null name)))
    (error "Invalid shared memory transfer or snapshot quota exceeded"))
  (let ((path (format nil "~Aframe-~D" *asset-directory* (incf *asset-sequence*))))
    (checked (snapshot-shm name path size) "shared memory snapshot")
    (incf *asset-bytes* size)
    (make-local-asset :path path :size size)))

(defun initialize-assets (directory)
  ;; Called only while holding this daemon's exclusive lock. Reclaim files
  ;; left by a daemon crash before accepting producers or attachments.
  (ensure-directories-exist directory)
  (let ((st (sb-posix:lstat (string-right-trim "/" directory))))
    (unless (and (sb-posix:s-isdir (sb-posix:stat-mode st))
                 (= (sb-posix:stat-uid st) (sb-posix:getuid)))
      (error "Unsafe asset directory")))
  (sb-posix:chmod directory #o700)
  (dolist (file (directory (concatenate 'string directory "frame-*"))) (delete-file file))
  (setf *asset-directory* directory *asset-sequence* 0 *asset-bytes* 0))

(export '(local-asset local-asset-p local-asset-path local-asset-size make-local-asset
          asset-size retain-asset release-asset snapshot-asset initialize-assets))
#+end_src

** src/vt.lisp
The VT parser and screen model, plus its public accessors.

#+begin_src lisp :tangle src/vt.lisp :mkdirp yes
(defpackage #:ekko/vt
  (:use #:cl)
  (:export #:make-terminal #:feed #:resize-terminal #:terminal-cols #:terminal-rows
           #:terminal-cw #:terminal-ch #:terminal-x #:terminal-y #:terminal-cells
           #:terminal-modes #:terminal-keyboards #:terminal-revision #:terminal-screen
           #:terminal-parser #:terminal-buffer #:terminal-visible #:terminal-title
           #:terminal-history #:history-count #:history-text
           #:terminal-materialized-rows #:terminal-erase-display-history
           #:parameters #:row-text #:character-width #:update-rendition))
(in-package #:ekko/vt)
(declaim (inline ensure-materialized-rows))


(defstruct (terminal (:constructor %make-terminal))
  (cols 80 :type fixnum) (rows 24 :type fixnum) (cw 8) (ch 16) (x 0 :type fixnum) (y 0 :type fixnum) (saved '(0 0))
  (cells #() :type simple-vector) main-cells history (screen :main) (top 0 :type fixnum) (bottom 23 :type fixnum) (visible t)
  ;; Zellij's Grid starts with one materialized row and grows rows as output
  ;; reaches them. Keep this separate from the fixed-size cell array so ED2
  ;; can transfer exactly the rows that exist, including blank rows.
  (materialized-rows 1) (main-materialized-rows 1)
  (erase-display-history nil)
  (rendition '(0)) (modes (make-hash-table)) (keyboards '(0))
  (parser :ground) (buffer (make-array 0 :element-type '(unsigned-byte 8)
                                     :adjustable t :fill-pointer 0))
  (utf-code 0) (utf-left 0) (utf-min 0) (revision 0) (title nil)
  (renditions (make-hash-table :test 'equal)) (pen (list nil)))
(defun blank-cells (cols rows)
  (let ((cells (make-array rows)))
    (dotimes (y rows cells) (setf (svref cells y) (make-array cols :initial-element '(" " (0)))))))
(defun make-terminal (&key (cols 80) (rows 24) (cw 8) (ch 16)
                           (erase-display-history nil))
  (let ((vt (%make-terminal :cols cols :rows rows :cw cw :ch ch :bottom (1- rows)
                            :erase-display-history erase-display-history
                            :cells (blank-cells cols rows))))
    (setf (terminal-rendition vt) (canonical-rendition vt (terminal-rendition vt)))
    vt))
(defun canonical-rendition (vt rendition)
  "The one RENDITION list VT's cells share for these attributes."
  (let ((table (terminal-renditions vt)))
    (car (or (gethash rendition table)
             (progn (when (>= (hash-table-count table) 64) (clrhash table))
                    (setf (gethash rendition table) (list rendition)))))))
(defparameter *ascii-text*
  (let ((texts (make-array 128))) (dotimes (code 128 texts) (setf (svref texts code) (string (code-char code))))))
(defun ascii-cell (vt code)
  "The shared, immutable cell for ASCII CODE in VT's current rendition."
  (let ((rendition (terminal-rendition vt)) (pen (terminal-pen vt)))
    (unless (eq (car pen) rendition)
      (setf pen (or (gethash rendition (terminal-renditions vt)) (list rendition)) (terminal-pen vt) pen))
    (let ((cells (or (cdr pen) (setf (cdr pen) (make-array 128 :initial-element nil)))))
      (or (svref cells code) (setf (svref cells code) (list (svref *ascii-text* code) rendition))))))
(defun ensure-materialized-rows (vt count)
  (when (eq (terminal-screen vt) :main)
    (setf (terminal-materialized-rows vt)
          (min (terminal-rows vt)
               (max (terminal-materialized-rows vt) count)))))
(defun resize-terminal (vt cols rows cw ch)
  (labels ((resized (old)
             (when old
               (let ((cells (blank-cells cols rows)))
                 (loop for y below (min rows (terminal-rows vt)) do
                   (loop for x below (min cols (terminal-cols vt)) do
                     (let ((cell (svref (svref old y) x)))
                       ;; Do not leave half a wide glyph at the new right edge.
                       (unless (and (= x (1- cols)) (plusp (length (first cell)))
                                    (= 2 (character-width (char (first cell) 0))))
                         (setf (svref (svref cells y) x) cell))))) cells))))
    (setf (terminal-cells vt) (resized (terminal-cells vt))
          (terminal-main-cells vt) (resized (terminal-main-cells vt))
          (terminal-cols vt) cols (terminal-rows vt) rows (terminal-cw vt) cw (terminal-ch vt) ch
          (terminal-materialized-rows vt) (min rows (terminal-materialized-rows vt))
          (terminal-main-materialized-rows vt) (min rows (terminal-main-materialized-rows vt))
          (terminal-top vt) 0 (terminal-bottom vt) (1- rows)
          (terminal-x vt) (min (terminal-x vt) (1- cols))
          (terminal-y vt) (min (terminal-y vt) (1- rows)))
    (incf (terminal-revision vt))))
(defun parameters (text &optional (separator #\;))
  (loop for start = 0 then (1+ end) for end = (position separator text :start start)
        for item = (subseq text start end)
        collect (if (zerop (length item)) 0 (or (parse-integer item :junk-allowed t) 0))
        while end))
(defun clear-range-with-rendition (vt start end rendition)
  (let ((cell (list " " rendition)) (cols (terminal-cols vt)) (cells (terminal-cells vt)))
    (loop for y from (floor start cols) while (< (* y cols) end)
          do (fill (svref cells y) cell :start (max 0 (- start (* y cols))) :end (min cols (- end (* y cols))))))
  (incf (terminal-revision vt)))
(defun clear-range (vt start end)
  (clear-range-with-rendition vt start end (terminal-rendition vt)))
(defun erase-rendition (vt)
  ;; Zellij's ED2 replacement character has default attributes and inherits
  ;; only the pending background color, if one is set.
  (let ((codes (rest (terminal-rendition vt)))
        (background nil))
    (labels ((consume-color (mode)
               (case mode
                 (5 (when codes (list mode (pop codes))))
                 (2 (when (>= (length codes) 3)
                      (list mode (pop codes) (pop codes) (pop codes)))))))
      ;; RENDITION is the canonical sequence emitted by UPDATE-RENDITION:
      ;; standard foreground/background values are one-element groups, while
      ;; 38/48/58 consume a mode and its color values. Consume those groups as
      ;; we scan so a foreground RGB component such as 44 or 48 cannot be read
      ;; as a standard background or an extended-background introducer.
      (loop while codes
            for code = (pop codes) do
              (cond
                ((= code 48)
                 (when codes
                   (let ((color (consume-color (pop codes))))
                     (when color (setf background (cons 48 color))))))
                ((member code '(38 58))
                 (when codes (consume-color (pop codes))))
                ((or (<= 40 code 47) (<= 100 code 107))
                 (setf background (list code)))))
      (if background (cons 0 background) '(0)))))
(defun erase-display (vt)
  "Implement ED2, optionally preserving materialized main-screen rows.

Zellij's full erase transfers the rows currently present in its viewport to
scrollback, including rows containing only blanks. Its viewport is sparse
until output reaches a row, while Ekko's cell array is always rectangular;
MATERIALIZED-ROWS carries that distinction."
  (when (and (terminal-erase-display-history vt)
             (eq (terminal-screen vt) :main))
    (let ((cells (terminal-cells vt)))
      (dotimes (row (terminal-materialized-rows vt))
        (remember-row vt (svref cells row))
        (setf (svref cells row) (make-array (terminal-cols vt))))))
  (clear-range-with-rendition vt 0 (* (terminal-cols vt) (terminal-rows vt))
                              (if (terminal-erase-display-history vt)
                                  (erase-rendition vt)
                                  (terminal-rendition vt)))
  (setf (terminal-materialized-rows vt) (terminal-rows vt))
  (when (terminal-erase-display-history vt)
    (setf (terminal-top vt) 0
          (terminal-bottom vt) (1- (terminal-rows vt)))))
(defun scroll-lines (vt amount emit)
  (let* ((cols (terminal-cols vt)) (cells (terminal-cells vt))
         (top (terminal-top vt)) (end (1+ (terminal-bottom vt)))
         (n (min (abs amount) (- end top)))
         (history (and (plusp amount) (eq (terminal-screen vt) :main)
                       (zerop top) (= end (terminal-rows vt)))))
    (if (plusp amount)
        (let ((gone (subseq cells top (+ top n))))
          (replace cells cells :start1 top :start2 (+ top n) :end2 end)
          (loop for row across gone for y from (- end n)
                do (setf (svref cells y) (if history (progn (remember-row vt row) (make-array cols)) row)))
          (clear-range vt (* cols (- end n)) (* cols end)))
        (let ((gone (subseq cells (- end n) end)))
          (replace cells cells :start1 (+ top n) :end1 end :start2 top :end2 (- end n))
          (replace cells gone :start1 top)
          (clear-range vt (* cols top) (* cols (+ top n))))))
  (when (and (plusp amount) (eq (terminal-screen vt) :main)
             (zerop (terminal-top vt))
             (= (terminal-bottom vt) (1- (terminal-rows vt))))
    (ensure-materialized-rows vt (+ (terminal-materialized-rows vt) amount)))
  (funcall emit :scroll (list (terminal-top vt) (terminal-bottom vt) amount)))
(defun newline (vt emit)
  (if (= (terminal-y vt) (terminal-bottom vt)) (scroll-lines vt 1 emit)
      (progn
        (setf (terminal-y vt) (min (1- (terminal-rows vt)) (1+ (terminal-y vt))))
        (ensure-materialized-rows vt (1+ (terminal-y vt))))))
(defun character-width (char)
  (ekko/text:display-width char))
(defun put-character (vt char emit)
  (let* ((width (character-width char))
         (cols (terminal-cols vt)))
    (ensure-materialized-rows vt (1+ (terminal-y vt)))
    (when (zerop width)
      (when (plusp (terminal-x vt))
        (let* ((row (svref (terminal-cells vt) (terminal-y vt))) (index (min (1- cols) (1- (terminal-x vt))))
               (cell (svref row index)))
          (setf (svref row index)
                (list (concatenate 'string (first cell) (string char)) (second cell)))
          (incf (terminal-revision vt))))
      (return-from put-character))
    (when (> (+ (terminal-x vt) width) cols)
      (setf (terminal-x vt) 0) (newline vt emit))
    (when (<= width cols)
      (ensure-materialized-rows vt (1+ (terminal-y vt)))
      (let ((row (svref (terminal-cells vt) (terminal-y vt))) (index (terminal-x vt)))
        (setf (svref row index) (if (< (char-code char) 128) (ascii-cell vt (char-code char))
                                    (list (string char) (terminal-rendition vt))))
        (when (= width 2) (setf (svref row (1+ index)) (list "" (terminal-rendition vt)))))
      (incf (terminal-x vt) width) (incf (terminal-revision vt)))))
(defun text-byte (vt byte emit)
  (cond
    ((plusp (terminal-utf-left vt))
     (if (<= #x80 byte #xbf)
         (progn
           (setf (terminal-utf-code vt) (+ (ash (terminal-utf-code vt) 6) (logand byte 63)))
           (when (zerop (decf (terminal-utf-left vt)))
             (let ((code (terminal-utf-code vt)))
               (put-character vt (if (and (<= (terminal-utf-min vt) code #x10ffff)
                                         (not (<= #xd800 code #xdfff)))
                                    (code-char code) #\Replacement_Character) emit))))
         (progn (setf (terminal-utf-left vt) 0)
                (put-character vt #\Replacement_Character emit) (text-byte vt byte emit))))
    ((< byte 128) (put-character vt (code-char byte) emit))
    ((<= #xc2 byte #xdf) (setf (terminal-utf-left vt) 1 (terminal-utf-code vt) (logand byte 31) (terminal-utf-min vt) #x80))
    ((<= #xe0 byte #xef) (setf (terminal-utf-left vt) 2 (terminal-utf-code vt) (logand byte 15) (terminal-utf-min vt) #x800))
    ((<= #xf0 byte #xf4) (setf (terminal-utf-left vt) 3 (terminal-utf-code vt) (logand byte 7) (terminal-utf-min vt) #x10000))
    (t (put-character vt #\Replacement_Character emit))))
(defun reply (emit control &rest args)
  (funcall emit :reply (apply #'format nil control args)))
(defun update-rendition (current args)
  (let ((state (make-array 13 :initial-element nil)))
    (declare (dynamic-extent state))
    (flet ((slot (key) (position key #(1 2 3 4 5 7 8 9 21 38 48 53 58))))
      (dolist (codes (list current args))
        (loop while codes for code = (pop codes) do
          (cond
            ((zerop code) (fill state nil))
            ((member code '(38 48 58))
             (let* ((mode (pop codes)) (count (case mode (2 3) (5 1))))
               (when (and count (>= (length codes) count))
                 (let ((values (loop repeat count collect (pop codes))))
                   (when (every (lambda (n) (<= 0 n 255)) values)
                     (setf (svref state (slot code)) (list* code mode values)))))))
            ((or (<= 30 code 37) (<= 90 code 97)) (setf (svref state (slot 38)) (list code)))
            ((or (<= 40 code 47) (<= 100 code 107)) (setf (svref state (slot 48)) (list code)))
            ((member code '(39 49 59)) (setf (svref state (slot (1- code))) nil))
            ((member code '(22 23 24 25 27 28 29 55))
             (dolist (key (case code (22 '(1 2)) (23 '(3)) (24 '(4 21)) (25 '(5))
                                    (27 '(7)) (28 '(8)) (29 '(9)) (55 '(53))))
               (setf (svref state (slot key)) nil)))
            ((member code '(1 2 3 4 5 7 8 9 21 53)) (setf (svref state (slot code)) (list code))))))
      (cons 0 (loop for group across state append group)))))
(defun csi-parameters (buffer start end)
  "The ; separated integers of BUFFER between START and END, read as PARAMETERS reads text."
  (loop for from = start then (1+ to)
        for to = (or (position 59 buffer :start from :end end) end)
        collect (let ((i from) (sign 1) (n nil))
                  (loop while (and (< i to) (= (aref buffer i) 32)) do (incf i))
                  (when (and (< i to) (member (aref buffer i) '(43 45)))
                    (when (= (aref buffer i) 45) (setf sign -1))
                    (incf i))
                  (loop while (and (< i to) (<= 48 (aref buffer i) 57))
                        do (setf n (+ (* 10 (or n 0)) (- (aref buffer i) 48))) (incf i))
                  (if n (* sign n) 0))
        while (< to end)))
(defun csi (vt buffer emit)
  (let* ((end (1- (length buffer))) (final (code-char (aref buffer end)))
         (private (and (plusp end) (find (code-char (aref buffer 0)) "?<>=!")))
         (args (csi-parameters buffer (if private 1 0) end))
         (n (max 1 (first args))) (cols (terminal-cols vt)) (rows (terminal-rows vt))
         (cursor (+ (* cols (terminal-y vt)) (min (terminal-x vt) (1- cols))))
         (esc (code-char 27)))
    (case final
      ((#\H #\f) (setf (terminal-y vt) (min (1- rows) (1- n))
                          (terminal-x vt) (min (1- cols) (max 0 (1- (or (second args) 1)))))
                      (ensure-materialized-rows vt (1+ (terminal-y vt))))
      (#\A (setf (terminal-y vt) (max 0 (- (terminal-y vt) n))))
      ((#\B #\e) (setf (terminal-y vt) (min (1- rows) (+ (terminal-y vt) n)))
                         (ensure-materialized-rows vt (1+ (terminal-y vt))))
      ;; CPL and CNL move by lines and return to the first column. Live regions
      ;; (nix-output-monitor) repaint with these, so dropping them appends every
      ;; frame below the previous one instead of overwriting it.
      (#\F (setf (terminal-y vt) (max 0 (- (terminal-y vt) n)) (terminal-x vt) 0))
      (#\E (setf (terminal-y vt) (min (1- rows) (+ (terminal-y vt) n)) (terminal-x vt) 0)
             (ensure-materialized-rows vt (1+ (terminal-y vt))))
      ((#\C #\a) (setf (terminal-x vt) (min (1- cols) (+ (terminal-x vt) n))))
      (#\D (setf (terminal-x vt) (max 0 (- (terminal-x vt) n))))
      (#\G (setf (terminal-x vt) (min (1- cols) (1- n))))
      (#\d (setf (terminal-y vt) (min (1- rows) (1- n)))
             (ensure-materialized-rows vt (1+ (terminal-y vt))))
      (#\J (case (first args)
              (0 (clear-range vt cursor (* cols rows)))
              (1 (clear-range vt 0 (1+ cursor)))
              (2 (erase-display vt))
              (3 (clear-history vt))))
      (#\K (let ((start (* cols (terminal-y vt))))
              (case (first args) (0 (clear-range vt cursor (+ start cols)))
                    (1 (clear-range vt start (1+ cursor))) (2 (clear-range vt start (+ start cols))))))
      (#\X (clear-range vt cursor (min (+ cursor n) (* cols (1+ (terminal-y vt))))))
      ((#\P #\@)
       (let* ((end (* cols (1+ (terminal-y vt)))) (row (svref (terminal-cells vt) (terminal-y vt)))
              (x (- cursor (* cols (terminal-y vt)))))
         (setf n (min n (- end cursor)))
         (if (char= final #\P)
             (progn (replace row row :start1 x :start2 (+ x n))
                    (clear-range vt (- end n) end))
             (progn (replace row row :start1 (+ x n) :start2 x :end2 (- cols n))
                    (clear-range vt cursor (+ cursor n))))))
      ((#\S #\T) (scroll-lines vt (if (char= final #\S) n (- n)) emit))
      ((#\L #\M)
       (when (<= (terminal-top vt) (terminal-y vt) (terminal-bottom vt))
         (let ((old (terminal-top vt)))
           (setf (terminal-top vt) (terminal-y vt))
           (scroll-lines vt (if (char= final #\M) n (- n)) emit)
           (setf (terminal-top vt) old))))
      (#\r (let ((bottom (if (plusp (or (second args) 0)) (second args) rows)))
              (when (< n bottom (1+ rows))
                (setf (terminal-top vt) (1- n) (terminal-bottom vt) (1- bottom)
                      (terminal-x vt) 0 (terminal-y vt) 0))))
      (#\m (unless private
              (setf (terminal-rendition vt)
                    (canonical-rendition vt (update-rendition (terminal-rendition vt) args)))))
      ((#\h #\l)
       (when (eql private #\?)
         (dolist (mode args)
           (let ((on (char= final #\h)) (was (gethash mode (terminal-modes vt))))
             (setf (gethash mode (terminal-modes vt)) on)
             ;; Applications batch one redraw between BSU and ESU so it presents
             ;; atomically; the server holds scene publication for that window.
             (when (and (= mode 2026) (not (eql was on))) (funcall emit :sync on))
             (case mode
               (25 (setf (terminal-visible vt) on))
               ((47 1047 1049)
                (cond ((and on (eq (terminal-screen vt) :main))
                       (setf (terminal-main-cells vt) (terminal-cells vt)
                             (terminal-saved vt) (list (terminal-x vt) (terminal-y vt))
                             (terminal-main-materialized-rows vt) (terminal-materialized-rows vt)
                             (terminal-cells vt) (blank-cells cols rows) (terminal-screen vt) :alternate
                             (terminal-materialized-rows vt) rows
                             (terminal-x vt) 0 (terminal-y vt) 0))
                      ((and (not on) (eq (terminal-screen vt) :alternate))
                       (setf (terminal-cells vt) (or (terminal-main-cells vt) (blank-cells cols rows))
                             (terminal-main-cells vt) nil (terminal-screen vt) :main
                             (terminal-materialized-rows vt) (terminal-main-materialized-rows vt)
                             (terminal-x vt) (min (1- cols) (first (terminal-saved vt)))
                             (terminal-y vt) (min (1- rows) (second (terminal-saved vt))))))
                (funcall emit :screen (terminal-screen vt)) (incf (terminal-revision vt))))))))
      (#\p (when (and (eql private #\?) (find 36 buffer :end end))
              (reply emit "~C[?~D;~D$y" esc (first args)
                     (cond ((member (first args) '(1000 1002 1003 1004 1006 1016 2004 2026))
                            (if (gethash (first args) (terminal-modes vt)) 1 2)) (t 0)))))
      (#\u (case private
              (#\? (reply emit "~C[?~Du" esc (first (terminal-keyboards vt))))
              (#\> (when (< (length (terminal-keyboards vt)) 16) (push (first args) (terminal-keyboards vt))))
              (#\< (dotimes (i (min n (1- (length (terminal-keyboards vt))))) (pop (terminal-keyboards vt))))
              (#\= (setf (first (terminal-keyboards vt)) (first args)))
              (otherwise (setf (terminal-x vt) (first (terminal-saved vt)) (terminal-y vt) (second (terminal-saved vt))))))
      (#\s (setf (terminal-saved vt) (list (terminal-x vt) (terminal-y vt))))
      (#\n (case (first args) (5 (reply emit "~C[0n" esc))
                  (6 (reply emit "~C[~D;~DR" esc (1+ (terminal-y vt)) (1+ (min (1- cols) (terminal-x vt)))))))
      (#\c (reply emit (if (eql private #\>) "~C[>0;1;0c" "~C[?62;22c") esc))
      (#\t (case (first args)
              (14 (reply emit "~C[4;~D;~Dt" esc (* rows (terminal-ch vt)) (* cols (terminal-cw vt))))
              (16 (reply emit "~C[6;~D;~Dt" esc (terminal-ch vt) (terminal-cw vt)))
              (18 (reply emit "~C[8;~D;~Dt" esc rows cols)))))))
(defun osc (vt bytes emit)
  (let* ((text (handler-case (ekko/platform:bytes-text bytes) (error () "")))
         (semi (position #\; text)))
    (when semi
      (let ((code (parse-integer text :end semi :junk-allowed t)) (body (subseq text (1+ semi))) (esc (code-char 27)))
        (cond ((and (member code '(10 11)) (string= body "?"))
               (reply emit "~C]~D;rgb:~A~C\\" esc code (if (= code 10) "dddd/dddd/dddd" "1111/1515/1b1b") esc))
              ((and (eql code 4) (search ";?" body))
               (let ((index (parse-integer body :junk-allowed t)))
                 (when (and index (<= 0 index 255)) (reply emit "~C]4;~D;rgb:8888/8888/8888~C\\" esc index esc))))
              ((member code '(0 2))
               (let ((title (coerce (remove-if (lambda (c) (< (char-code c) 32)) body)
                                   'string)))
                 (setf (terminal-title vt) title)
                 (incf (terminal-revision vt))))
              ;; OSC 9 is iTerm2's notification unless it is a numbered ConEmu
              ;; command such as 9;4 progress. OSC 777 is notify;title;body.
              ((eql code 9)
               (unless (let ((end (position #\; body))) (and end (plusp end) (every #'digit-char-p (subseq body 0 end))))
                 (funcall emit :notify (list nil body))))
              ((and (eql code 777) (> (length body) 7) (string= "notify;" body :end2 7))
               (let* ((rest (subseq body 7)) (semi (position #\; rest)))
                 (funcall emit :notify (if semi (list (subseq rest 0 semi) (subseq rest (1+ semi)))
                                           (list rest ""))))))))))
(defun feed (vt bytes emit)
  (declare (optimize (speed 3) (safety 2))
           (type (vector (unsigned-byte 8)) bytes))
  (labels ((reset-parser () (setf (terminal-parser vt) :ground (fill-pointer (terminal-buffer vt)) 0))
           (append-byte (b limit discard)
             (if (< (length (terminal-buffer vt)) limit) (vector-push-extend b (terminal-buffer vt))
                 (setf (terminal-parser vt) discard (fill-pointer (terminal-buffer vt)) 0)))
           (end-string (kind)
             (case kind (:osc (osc vt (terminal-buffer vt) emit))
                   (:apc (funcall emit :graphics (copy-seq (terminal-buffer vt)))))
             (reset-parser)))
    (loop for index from 0 below (length bytes) for byte = (aref bytes index) do
      (case (terminal-parser vt)
        (:ground
         (case byte
           (27 (setf (terminal-parser vt) :escape))
           ((10 11 12) (newline vt emit))
           (7 (funcall emit :notify nil))
           (13 (setf (terminal-x vt) 0))
           (8 (setf (terminal-x vt) (max 0 (1- (terminal-x vt)))))
           (9 (setf (terminal-x vt) (min (1- (terminal-cols vt)) (* 8 (1+ (floor (terminal-x vt) 8))))))
           (otherwise (when (>= byte 32) (unless (= byte 127) (text-byte vt byte emit))))))
        (:escape
         (reset-parser)
         (case byte
           (91 (setf (terminal-parser vt) :csi)) (93 (setf (terminal-parser vt) :osc))
           (95 (setf (terminal-parser vt) :apc)) ((80 94 88) (setf (terminal-parser vt) :discard))
           ((40 41 42 43 35) (setf (terminal-parser vt) :charset))
           (55 (setf (terminal-saved vt) (list (terminal-x vt) (terminal-y vt))))
           (56 (setf (terminal-x vt) (first (terminal-saved vt)) (terminal-y vt) (second (terminal-saved vt))))
           (68 (newline vt emit)) (69 (setf (terminal-x vt) 0) (newline vt emit))
           (77 (if (= (terminal-y vt) (terminal-top vt)) (scroll-lines vt -1 emit) (setf (terminal-y vt) (max 0 (1- (terminal-y vt))))))
           (99 (clear-range vt 0 (* (terminal-cols vt) (terminal-rows vt)))
               (setf (terminal-x vt) 0 (terminal-y vt) 0
                     (terminal-materialized-rows vt) 1
                     (terminal-main-materialized-rows vt) 1)
               (funcall emit :reset nil))))
        (:charset (reset-parser))
        (:csi
         (cond ((= byte 27) (setf (terminal-parser vt) :escape (fill-pointer (terminal-buffer vt)) 0))
               ((<= 64 byte 126)
                (append-byte byte 1024 :discard-csi)
                (unless (eq (terminal-parser vt) :discard-csi)
                  (csi vt (terminal-buffer vt) emit)) (reset-parser))
               ((<= 32 byte 63) (append-byte byte 1024 :discard-csi))))
        (:discard-csi (when (<= 64 byte 126) (reset-parser)))
        (:apc
         ;; Graphics payloads are ASCII runs terminated by ESC. Copy each run
         ;; once, retaining the same control-string limit and split-ST state.
         (let* ((end (or (position 27 bytes :start index) (length bytes)))
                (buffer (terminal-buffer vt))
                (used (length buffer)) (required (+ used (- end index)))
                (overflow (> required 16384)))
           (if overflow
               (setf (fill-pointer buffer) 0 (terminal-parser vt) :discard)
               (progn
                 (when (> required (array-total-size buffer))
                   (setf buffer (adjust-array buffer (min 16384 (max required (* 2 (array-total-size buffer)))))
                         (terminal-buffer vt) buffer))
                 (setf (fill-pointer buffer) required)
                 (replace buffer bytes :start1 used :start2 index :end2 end)))
           (if (< end (length bytes))
               (setf index end (terminal-parser vt) (if overflow :discard-escape :apc-escape))
               (setf index (1- end)))))
        ((:osc :discard)
         (cond ((= byte 27) (setf (terminal-parser vt) (case (terminal-parser vt) (:osc :osc-escape) (:apc :apc-escape) (t :discard-escape))))
               ((and (= byte 7) (eq (terminal-parser vt) :osc)) (end-string :osc))
               ((not (eq (terminal-parser vt) :discard)) (append-byte byte 16384 :discard))))
        ((:osc-escape :apc-escape :discard-escape)
         (if (= byte 92)
             (end-string (case (terminal-parser vt) (:osc-escape :osc) (:apc-escape :apc)))
             (setf (terminal-parser vt) :discard)))))))
#+end_src

** src/history.lisp
Bounded main-screen scrollback rows.

#+begin_src lisp :tangle src/history.lisp :mkdirp yes
(in-package #:ekko/vt)

(defconstant +history-rows+ 10000)
(defconstant +history-bytes+ (* 8 1024 1024))
(defstruct history (rows (make-array +history-rows+ :initial-element nil))
  (start 0) (count 0) (bytes 0))
(defun row-cost (row)
  (loop for cell across row
        sum (the fixnum (+ 24 (* 4 (length (the string (first cell))))
                           (* 8 (length (the list (second cell))))))
        into total fixnum
        finally (return total)))
(defun history-drop (history)
  (let* ((index (history-start history)) (row (aref (history-rows history) index)))
    (decf (history-bytes history) (row-cost row))
    (setf (aref (history-rows history) index) nil
          (history-start history) (mod (1+ index) +history-rows+))
    (decf (history-count history))))
(defun remember-row (vt row)
  (let ((cost (row-cost row)))
    (when (> cost +history-bytes+) (return-from remember-row))
    (let ((history (or (terminal-history vt) (setf (terminal-history vt) (make-history)))))
      (loop while (or (= (history-count history) +history-rows+)
                      (> (+ (history-bytes history) cost) +history-bytes+)) do (history-drop history))
      (setf (aref (history-rows history) (mod (+ (history-start history) (history-count history)) +history-rows+)) row)
      (incf (history-count history)) (incf (history-bytes history) cost))))
(defun clear-history (vt)
  "Discard all rows above the main-screen viewport, as ED3 requires."
  (when (and (terminal-history vt) (eq (terminal-screen vt) :main))
    (setf (history-rows (terminal-history vt))
          (make-array +history-rows+ :initial-element nil)
          (history-start (terminal-history vt)) 0
          (history-count (terminal-history vt)) 0
          (history-bytes (terminal-history vt)) 0)))
(defun history-cells (vt)
  "Frozen rows retain the VT's immutable text/rendition cells."
  (let ((history (terminal-history vt)))
    (coerce
      (append
        (when (and history (eq (terminal-screen vt) :main))
          (loop for i below (history-count history)
                collect (aref (history-rows history) (mod (+ (history-start history) i) +history-rows+))))
        (loop for y below (terminal-rows vt)
              collect (copy-seq (svref (terminal-cells vt) y))))
      'vector)))
(defun history-text (vt)
  (map 'vector #'row-text (history-cells vt)))
(defun row-text (row)
  (string-right-trim " " (with-output-to-string (out)
                           (loop for cell across row do (write-string (first cell) out)))))
(export '(terminal-history history-count history-cells history-text clear-history))
#+end_src

** src/graphics.lisp
Kitty graphics command store: uploads, expiry and frame accounting.

#+begin_src lisp :tangle src/graphics.lisp :mkdirp yes
(defpackage #:ekko/graphics
  (:use #:cl)
  (:export #:make-store #:accept-command #:store-images #:store-pending #:store-revision
           #:store-frames #:store-errors #:store-bytes #:expire-upload #:clear-screen
           #:scroll-images #:image-id #:image-generation #:image-width #:image-height
           #:image-format #:image-data #:image-x #:image-y #:image-cols #:image-rows
           #:image-screen #:image-visible #:base64-encode #:base64-decode #:header #:number-key #:upload-at))
(in-package #:ekko/graphics)

(defparameter *alphabet* "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/")
(defparameter *decode-table*
  (let ((table (make-array 256 :element-type '(signed-byte 8) :initial-element -1)))
    (dotimes (i (length *alphabet*) table)
      (setf (aref table (char-code (char *alphabet* i))) i))))
(defun base64-encode (bytes)
  (declare (optimize (speed 3) (safety 2)))
  (check-type bytes (vector (unsigned-byte 8)))
  (unless (typep bytes '(simple-array (unsigned-byte 8) (*)))
    (return-from base64-encode (base64-encode (copy-seq bytes))))
  (let* ((n (length bytes)) (text (make-string (* 4 (ceiling n 3)) :element-type 'base-char :initial-element #\=)))
    (declare (type (simple-array (unsigned-byte 8) (*)) bytes)
             (type simple-base-string text))
    (loop for i fixnum from 0 below n by 3 for j fixnum from 0 by 4
          for bits = (+ (ash (aref bytes i) 16) (if (< (1+ i) n) (ash (aref bytes (1+ i)) 8) 0)
                        (if (< (+ i 2) n) (aref bytes (+ i 2)) 0)) do
      (setf (char text j) (char *alphabet* (ldb (byte 6 18) bits))
            (char text (1+ j)) (char *alphabet* (ldb (byte 6 12) bits)))
      (when (< (1+ i) n) (setf (char text (+ j 2)) (char *alphabet* (ldb (byte 6 6) bits))))
      (when (< (+ i 2) n) (setf (char text (+ j 3)) (char *alphabet* (ldb (byte 6 0) bits))))) text))
(defun base64-decode (input &key (start 0) (end (length input)))
  (declare (optimize (speed 3) (safety 2)))
  (unless (and (integerp start) (integerp end) (<= 0 start end (length input)))
    (error "Invalid base64 range"))
  ;; Public string callers retain their API. Graphics ingress uses the octet
  ;; branch directly, avoiding a full payload conversion/copy on every chunk.
  (when (stringp input)
    (return-from base64-decode
      (base64-decode (map '(vector (unsigned-byte 8)) #'char-code (subseq input start end)))))
  (check-type input (vector (unsigned-byte 8)))
  ;; Normalize uncommon adjustable/displaced vectors at the boundary. The
  ;; graphics parser already supplies simple octets; its hot loop needs no copy.
  (unless (typep input '(simple-array (unsigned-byte 8) (*)))
    (return-from base64-decode (base64-decode (subseq input start end))))
  (let* ((start (the fixnum start)) (end (the fixnum end))
         (n (- end start))
         (table *decode-table*)
         (padding (cond ((zerop n) 0)
                        ((= (aref input (1- end)) 61)
                         (if (and (> n 1) (= (aref input (- end 2)) 61)) 2 1))
                        (t 0))))
    (declare (type (simple-array (signed-byte 8) (256)) table)
             (type (simple-array (unsigned-byte 8) (*)) input))
    (unless (zerop (mod n 4)) (error "Invalid base64 length"))
    (let ((out (ekko/platform:octets (- (* 3 (floor n 4)) padding))))
      (declare (type (simple-array (unsigned-byte 8) (*)) out))
      (loop for i fixnum from start below end by 4 for target fixnum from 0 by 3 do
        (let ((bits 0))
          (declare (type (unsigned-byte 24) bits))
          (dotimes (k 4)
            (let* ((index (+ i k)) (code (aref input index))
                   (value (if (and (= code 61) (>= index (- end padding))) 0
                              (aref table code))))
              (when (minusp value) (error "Invalid base64 alphabet"))
              (setf bits (logior (ash bits 6) value))))
          ;; Padding replaces low-order groups with zeroes. Reject nonzero
          ;; unused bits in the last real group, without re-encoding the image.
          (when (= (+ i 4) end)
            (when (or (and (= padding 2) (not (zerop (logand bits #xffff))))
                      (and (= padding 1) (not (zerop (logand bits #xff)))))
              (error "Invalid base64 padding")))
          (dotimes (k (min 3 (- (length out) target)))
            (setf (aref out (+ target k)) (ldb (byte 8 (* 8 (- 2 k))) bits)))))
      out)))
(defstruct image id generation width height format data (x 0) (y 0) (cols 0) (rows 0) screen (visible t))
(defstruct upload keys chunks (bytes 0) (at (ekko/platform:now)) x y screen)
(defstruct store (images (make-hash-table)) pending (revision 0) (frames 0) (errors 0) (bytes 0) (next-id 1))
(defun header (text)
  (let ((keys (make-hash-table :test 'eql)))
    (dolist (part (uiop:split-string text :separator ","))
      (unless (and (>= (length part) 3) (char= (char part 1) #\=)
                   (not (gethash (char part 0) keys))) (error "Invalid graphics header"))
      (setf (gethash (char part 0) keys) (subseq part 2))) keys))
(defun number-key (keys key &optional (default 0))
  (let ((value (gethash key keys)))
    (if value (let ((n (parse-integer value)))
                (unless (<= 0 n #xffffffff) (error "Invalid graphics integer")) n) default)))
(defun key-is (keys key value &optional default) (string= (gethash key keys default) value))
(defun acknowledge (keys emit message)
  (let ((id (number-key keys #\i)) (quiet (number-key keys #\q)))
    (when (and (plusp id) (or (zerop quiet) (and (= quiet 1) (not (string= message "OK")))))
      (funcall emit :reply (format nil "~C_Gi=~D~A;~A~C\\" (code-char 27) id
                                  (if (gethash #\p keys) (format nil ",p=~D" (number-key keys #\p)) "")
                                  message (code-char 27))))))
(defun remove-image (store id)
  (let ((image (gethash id (store-images store))))
    (when image (decf (store-bytes store) (ekko/platform:asset-size (image-data image)))
          (ekko/platform:release-asset (image-data image))
          (remhash id (store-images store)) (incf (store-revision store)))))
(defun clear-screen (store screen)
  (let ((ids (loop for image being the hash-values of (store-images store)
                  when (eq (image-screen image) screen) collect (image-id image))))
    (dolist (id ids) (remove-image store id)))
  (setf (store-pending store) nil))
(defun expire-upload (store)
  (when (and (store-pending store) (>= (- (ekko/platform:now) (upload-at (store-pending store))) 10))
    (setf (store-pending store) nil) (incf (store-errors store))))
(defun scroll-images (store screen top bottom amount cw ch)
  (declare (ignore cw))
  (maphash (lambda (id image)
             (declare (ignore id))
             (when (and (eq screen (image-screen image))
                        (<= (* top ch) (image-y image) (* (1+ bottom) ch)))
               (decf (image-y image) (* amount ch)) (incf (store-revision store)))) (store-images store)))
(defun complete-upload (store upload vt emit)
  (let* ((keys (upload-keys upload)) (w (number-key keys #\s)) (h (number-key keys #\v))
         (format (number-key keys #\f 32)) (data (ekko/platform:octets (upload-bytes upload))))
    ;; CONCATENATE on hundreds of chunks takes the generic sequence path.
    ;; The upload already tracks its exact size; copy each byte range once.
    (loop with offset = 0 for chunk in (nreverse (upload-chunks upload))
          do (replace data chunk :start1 offset) (incf offset (length chunk)))
    (unless (and (member format '(24 32)) (<= 1 w 8192) (<= 1 h 8192)
                 (<= (* w h (/ format 8)) (* 32 1024 1024))) (error "Unsupported image format or dimensions"))
    (let* ((size (* w h (/ format 8)))
           (compressed (if (key-is keys #\o "z")
                           (progn (ekko/platform:decompress-bytes data size) data)
                           (progn (unless (= (length data) size) (error "Invalid image length"))
                                  (ekko/platform:compress-bytes data)))))
      (commit-image store upload vt emit compressed w h format))))

(defun commit-image (store upload vt emit compressed w h format)
  (let ((keys (upload-keys upload)))
      (if (key-is keys #\a "q") (acknowledge keys emit "OK")
          (let* ((id (number-key keys #\i)) (old (gethash id (store-images store))))
            (when (zerop id) (setf id (loop for n = (incf (store-next-id store)) unless (gethash n (store-images store)) return n)))
            (when (or (and (null old) (>= (hash-table-count (store-images store)) 64))
                      (> (+ (store-bytes store) (ekko/platform:asset-size compressed) (- (if old (ekko/platform:asset-size (image-data old)) 0))) (* 128 1024 1024)))
              (error "Pane image quota exceeded"))
            (when old
              (decf (store-bytes store) (ekko/platform:asset-size (image-data old)))
              (ekko/platform:release-asset (image-data old)))
            (incf (store-bytes store) (ekko/platform:asset-size compressed))
            (setf (gethash id (store-images store))
                  (make-image :id id :generation (incf (store-revision store)) :width w :height h :format format
                              :data compressed :x (+ (upload-x upload) (number-key keys #\X))
                              :y (+ (upload-y upload) (number-key keys #\Y))
                              :cols (number-key keys #\c) :rows (number-key keys #\r)
                              :screen (upload-screen upload) :visible (key-is keys #\a "T" "t")))
            (incf (store-frames store)) (acknowledge keys emit "OK")
            (when (and (key-is keys #\a "T") (zerop (number-key keys #\C)))
              (setf (ekko/vt:terminal-x vt) (min (1- (ekko/vt:terminal-cols vt))
                                                 (+ (ekko/vt:terminal-x vt) (ceiling w (ekko/vt:terminal-cw vt)))))
              (setf (ekko/vt:terminal-y vt) (min (1- (ekko/vt:terminal-rows vt))
                                                 (+ (ekko/vt:terminal-y vt) (1- (ceiling h (ekko/vt:terminal-ch vt))))))))))
)
(defun accept-command (store bytes vt emit)
  (unless (and (plusp (length bytes)) (= (aref bytes 0) 71)) (return-from accept-command))
  (let ((keys nil))
    (handler-case
        (let* ((semi (position 59 bytes))
               (text (map 'string #'code-char (subseq bytes 1 semi))))
          (setf keys (header text))
          (maphash (lambda (key value) (declare (ignore value))
                     (unless (find key "aftsvipCqmoXYd" :test #'char=) (error "Unsupported graphics key"))) keys)
          (unless (member (gethash #\t keys "d") '("d" "s") :test #'string=)
            (error "Unsupported graphics transfer medium"))
          (when (some (lambda (key) (gethash key keys)) '(#\U #\I #\P #\Q #\x #\y #\w #\h #\z #\c #\r))
            (error "Unsupported graphics placement key"))
          (when (and (gethash #\o keys) (not (key-is keys #\o "z"))) (error "Unsupported compression"))
          (cond
            ((key-is keys #\t "s")
             (unless (and (member (gethash #\a keys "t") '("t" "T" "q") :test #'string=)
                          (zerop (number-key keys #\m)) (not (gethash #\o keys)))
               (error "Shared transfers require one uncompressed RGB/RGBA frame"))
             (setf (store-pending store) nil)
             (let* ((w (number-key keys #\s)) (h (number-key keys #\v))
                    (format (number-key keys #\f 32)) (size (* w h (/ format 8)))
                    (id (number-key keys #\i)) (old (gethash id (store-images store))))
               (unless (and (member format '(24 32)) (<= 1 w 8192) (<= 1 h 8192)
                            (<= 1 size (* 32 1024 1024))
                            (or (key-is keys #\a "q")
                                (and (or old (< (hash-table-count (store-images store)) 64))
                                     (<= (+ (store-bytes store) size
                                            (- (if old (ekko/platform:asset-size (image-data old)) 0)))
                                         (* 128 1024 1024)))))
                 (error "Invalid dimensions or pane quota exceeded"))
               (let ((asset (ekko/platform:snapshot-asset
                              (ekko/platform:bytes-text (base64-decode bytes :start (if semi (1+ semi) (length bytes)))) size)))
                 (unwind-protect
                      (commit-image store
                          (make-upload :keys keys :x (* (ekko/vt:terminal-x vt) (ekko/vt:terminal-cw vt))
                                       :y (* (ekko/vt:terminal-y vt) (ekko/vt:terminal-ch vt))
                                       :screen (ekko/vt:terminal-screen vt))
                          vt emit asset w h format)
                   (unless (loop for image being the hash-values of (store-images store)
                                 thereis (eq asset (image-data image)))
                     (ekko/platform:release-asset asset))))))
            ((key-is keys #\a "d")
             (let ((kind (gethash #\d keys "a")))
               (cond ((member kind '("a" "A") :test #'string=) (clear-screen store (ekko/vt:terminal-screen vt)))
                     ((member kind '("i" "I") :test #'string=) (remove-image store (number-key keys #\i)))
                     (t (error "Unsupported delete")))))
            ((key-is keys #\a "p")
             (let ((image (gethash (number-key keys #\i) (store-images store))))
               (unless image (error "Unknown image"))
               (setf (image-x image) (* (ekko/vt:terminal-x vt) (ekko/vt:terminal-cw vt))
                     (image-y image) (* (ekko/vt:terminal-y vt) (ekko/vt:terminal-ch vt))
                     (image-visible image) t (image-cols image) (number-key keys #\c)
                     (image-rows image) (number-key keys #\r))
               (incf (store-revision store)) (acknowledge keys emit "OK")))
            (t
             (unless (member (gethash #\a keys "t") '("t" "T" "q") :test #'string=) (error "Unsupported graphics action"))
             (when (or (gethash #\a keys) (gethash #\s keys)) (setf (store-pending store) nil))
             (let* ((upload (or (store-pending store)
                                (make-upload :keys keys :x (* (ekko/vt:terminal-x vt) (ekko/vt:terminal-cw vt))
                                             :y (* (ekko/vt:terminal-y vt) (ekko/vt:terminal-ch vt))
                                             :screen (ekko/vt:terminal-screen vt))))
                    (chunk (base64-decode bytes :start (if semi (1+ semi) (length bytes)))))
               (when (> (+ (upload-bytes upload) (length chunk)) (* 32 1024 1024)) (error "Upload quota exceeded"))
               (unless (member (number-key keys #\m) '(0 1)) (error "Invalid continuation flag"))
               (incf (upload-bytes upload) (length chunk)) (push chunk (upload-chunks upload))
               (setf (upload-at upload) (ekko/platform:now) (store-pending store) upload)
               (when (zerop (number-key keys #\m))
                 (setf (store-pending store) nil keys (upload-keys upload))
                 (complete-upload store upload vt emit))))))
      (error ()
        (incf (store-errors store)) (setf (store-pending store) nil)
        (when keys (ignore-errors (acknowledge keys emit "ENOTSUP:unsupported or invalid graphics operation")))))))
#+end_src

** src/wire.lisp
The `ekko/runtime` package and its protocol vocabulary.

#+begin_src lisp :tangle src/wire.lisp :mkdirp yes
(defpackage #:ekko/runtime
  (:use #:cl #:ekko/platform #:ekko/vt #:ekko/graphics)
  (:export #:run #:serve #:attach #:control #:restore-terminal #:extension-worker-main #:initialize #:config-path #:load-worker #:config-source #:install-registry #:make-daemon #:extension-worker-registry #:stop-worker #:*instance* #:checked-name #:note-client-error #:version-mismatch-p #:emergency-restore))
(in-package #:ekko/runtime)

;; Version 16 removes session routing: one daemon hosts one workspace and every
;; peer attaches to it. Input transactions stay bound to connection generations
;; and pane identities remain daemon-global.
(defconstant +wire-version+ 17)
(defvar *instance* "default")
(defconstant +queue-limit+ (* 8 1024 1024))
(define-condition wire-protocol-error (simple-error) ())
(defun reject-protocol (message)
  (error 'wire-protocol-error :format-control message :format-arguments nil))
(defstruct wire fd (version +wire-version+) (packet-limit +queue-limit+) (queue nil) (queue-tail nil) (queued 0) (offset 0) (prefix (octets 4))
  (prefix-used 0) body (body-used 0) (known (make-hash-table :test 'equal))
  greeted selected-view-id view closing
  (binding-generation 0) binding-accepted
  attached revision awaiting-scene (leases nil) (at (now)))
(defun u32 (bytes offset)
  (+ (ash (aref bytes offset) 24) (ash (aref bytes (+ offset 1)) 16)
     (ash (aref bytes (+ offset 2)) 8) (aref bytes (+ offset 3))))
(defun put-u32 (bytes offset value)
  (dotimes (i 4) (setf (aref bytes (+ offset i)) (ldb (byte 8 (* 8 (- 3 i))) value))))
(defun integers (values)
  (let ((out (octets (* 4 (length values)))))
    (loop for value in values for offset from 0 by 4 do (put-u32 out offset value)) out))
(defun queue-bytes (wire data)
  (when (> (+ (wire-queued wire) (length data)) +queue-limit+) (error "Output queue limit exceeded"))
  (let ((cell (list data)))
    (if (wire-queue-tail wire)
