        (setf (rest (wire-queue-tail wire)) cell)
        (setf (wire-queue wire) cell))
    (setf (wire-queue-tail wire) cell))
  (incf (wire-queued wire) (length data)))
(defun send-packet (wire type &optional (data (octets 0)))
  (let ((packet (octets (+ 5 (length data)))))
    (put-u32 packet 0 (1+ (length data))) (setf (aref packet 4) type)
    (replace packet data :start1 5) (queue-bytes wire packet)))
(defun flush-wire (wire)
  (loop repeat 16 while (wire-queue wire) for data = (first (wire-queue wire))
        for n = (write-fd (wire-fd wire) data (wire-offset wire)) do
    (cond ((member n '(-11 -4)) (return)) ((<= n 0) (error "Output disconnected"))
          (t (incf (wire-offset wire) n) (decf (wire-queued wire) n)
             (when (= (wire-offset wire) (length data))
               (pop (wire-queue wire))
               (when (null (wire-queue wire)) (setf (wire-queue-tail wire) nil))
               (setf (wire-offset wire) 0))))))
(defun wire-feed (wire bytes count)
  (let ((offset 0) (packets nil))
    (loop while (< offset count) do
      (if (wire-body wire)
          (let ((n (min (- count offset) (- (length (wire-body wire)) (wire-body-used wire)))))
            (replace (wire-body wire) bytes :start1 (wire-body-used wire) :start2 offset :end2 (+ offset n))
            (incf offset n) (incf (wire-body-used wire) n)
            (when (= (wire-body-used wire) (length (wire-body wire)))
              (push (wire-body wire) packets)
              (setf (wire-body wire) nil (wire-body-used wire) 0 (wire-prefix-used wire) 0)))
          (let ((n (min (- count offset) (- 4 (wire-prefix-used wire)))))
            (replace (wire-prefix wire) bytes :start1 (wire-prefix-used wire) :start2 offset :end2 (+ offset n))
            (incf offset n) (incf (wire-prefix-used wire) n)
            (when (= (wire-prefix-used wire) 4)
              (let ((size (u32 (wire-prefix wire) 0)))
                (unless (<= 1 size (wire-packet-limit wire)) (error "Invalid IPC frame size"))
                (setf (wire-body wire) (octets size)))))))
    (nreverse packets)))
(defun receive-packets (wire buffer)
  (let ((n (read-fd (wire-fd wire) buffer)))
    (cond ((member n '(-11 -4)) nil) ((<= n 0) (error "Input disconnected"))
          (t (setf (wire-at wire) (now)) (wire-feed wire buffer n)))))
(defun wire-events (wire) (logior 1 (if (wire-queue wire) 4 0)))
(defun acknowledge-scene (wire)
  (dolist (asset (wire-leases wire)) (release-asset asset))
  (setf (wire-leases wire) nil (wire-awaiting-scene wire) nil))
(defun close-wire (wire) (close-fd (wire-fd wire)) (setf (wire-fd wire) -1)
  (setf (wire-queue wire) nil (wire-queue-tail wire) nil (wire-body wire) nil)
  (clrhash (wire-known wire)) (acknowledge-scene wire))
(defun checked-name (name &optional (kind "Instance"))
  (unless (and (stringp name) (<= 1 (length name) 40)
               (every (lambda (c) (or (find c "abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyzABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ0123456789")
                                     (find c "_-"))) name))
    (error "~A names must contain 1–40 ASCII letters, digits, underscores or hyphens" kind))
  name)
(defun socket-path ()
  (checked-name *instance* "Instance")
  (let* ((base (uiop:getenv "XDG_RUNTIME_DIR"))
         (dir (if (and base (plusp (length base)))
                  (format nil "~A/ekko/" (string-right-trim "/" base))
                  (format nil "/tmp/ekko-~D/" (sb-posix:getuid)))))
    (ensure-directories-exist dir)
    (let ((st (sb-posix:lstat (string-right-trim "/" dir))))
      (unless (and (= (sb-posix:stat-uid st) (sb-posix:getuid))
                   (sb-posix:s-isdir (sb-posix:stat-mode st))) (error "Unsafe daemon directory")))
    (sb-posix:chmod dir #o700)
    (format nil "~A~A.sock" dir (if (string= *instance* "default") "ekko"
                                   (concatenate 'string "instance-" *instance*)))))
(defun send-route (wire &optional view-id)
  (send-packet wire 0 (encode-scene (list +wire-version+ view-id))))
(defun standard-text (value)
  (with-standard-io-syntax (let ((*print-readably* t) (*print-pretty* nil)) (write-to-string value))))
(defun plain-keyword-p (symbol)
  (let ((name (symbol-name symbol)))
    (and (keywordp symbol) (plusp (length name)) (char<= #\A (char name 0) #\Z)
         (every (lambda (c) (or (char<= #\A c #\Z) (char<= #\0 c #\9) (char= c #\-))) name))))
(defstruct (sink (:constructor make-sink ())) (bytes (octets 4096) :type (simple-array (unsigned-byte 8) (*)))
  (fill 0 :type fixnum))
(declaim (inline sink-byte))
(defun sink-byte (sink byte)
  (let ((bytes (sink-bytes sink)) (fill (sink-fill sink)))
    (when (= fill (length bytes))
      (setf bytes (replace (octets (* 2 (length bytes))) bytes) (sink-bytes sink) bytes))
    (setf (aref bytes fill) byte (sink-fill sink) (1+ fill))))
(defun sink-char (sink char)
  (let ((code (char-code char)))
    (cond ((< code #x80) (sink-byte sink code))
          ((< code #x800) (sink-byte sink (logior #xc0 (ash code -6))) (sink-byte sink (logior #x80 (logand code #x3f))))
          ((<= #xd800 code #xdfff) (error "Unable to encode character ~D as :UTF-8." code))
          ((< code #x10000) (sink-byte sink (logior #xe0 (ash code -12)))
           (sink-byte sink (logior #x80 (logand (ash code -6) #x3f))) (sink-byte sink (logior #x80 (logand code #x3f))))
          (t (sink-byte sink (logior #xf0 (ash code -18))) (sink-byte sink (logior #x80 (logand (ash code -12) #x3f)))
             (sink-byte sink (logior #x80 (logand (ash code -6) #x3f))) (sink-byte sink (logior #x80 (logand code #x3f)))))))
(defun sink-text (sink string)
  (loop for char across string do (sink-char sink char)))
(defun sink-decimal (sink n)
  (declare (type (signed-byte 62) n))
  (when (minusp n) (sink-byte sink 45) (setf n (- n)))
  (if (< n 10) (sink-byte sink (+ 48 n))
      (multiple-value-bind (high low) (floor n 10) (sink-decimal sink high) (sink-byte sink (+ 48 low)))))
(defun sink-octets (sink) (subseq (sink-bytes sink) 0 (sink-fill sink)))
(defvar *scene-sink* (make-sink))
(defun encode-scene (scene)
  "SCENE as the UTF-8 bytes WRITE-TO-STRING prints it with standard, readable syntax."
  (let ((sink *scene-sink*))
    (setf (sink-fill sink) 0)
    (labels ((emit (value)
               (typecase value
                 (null (sink-text sink "NIL"))
                 (cons (sink-byte sink 40)
                  (loop (emit (car value))
                        (setf value (cdr value))
                        (cond ((null value) (return))
                              ((consp value) (sink-byte sink 32))
                              (t (sink-text sink " . ") (emit value) (return))))
                  (sink-byte sink 41))
                 ((signed-byte 62) (sink-decimal sink value))
                 (simple-string (sink-byte sink 34)
                  (loop for char across value
                        do (when (or (char= char #\") (char= char #\\)) (sink-byte sink 92)) (sink-char sink char))
                  (sink-byte sink 34))
                 (symbol (cond ((eq value t) (sink-byte sink 84))
                               ((plain-keyword-p value) (sink-byte sink 58) (sink-text sink (symbol-name value)))
                               (t (sink-text sink (standard-text value)))))
                 (t (sink-text sink (standard-text value))))))
      (emit scene)
      (sink-octets sink))))
(defun decode-scene (bytes)
  "Read the one datum in BYTES as READ-FROM-STRING does with standard syntax and no #. evaluation."
  (when (> (length bytes) (* 1024 1024)) (error "Scene metadata too large"))
  (let ((value (read-plain-datum bytes)))
    (if (eq value :fallback)
        (with-standard-io-syntax (let ((*read-eval* nil)) (read-from-string (bytes-text bytes))))
        value)))
(defun read-plain-datum (bytes)
  "The datum in BYTES when it is only lists, fixnums, strings, NIL, T and plain
keywords over well-formed UTF-8; :FALLBACK for anything else."
  (declare (optimize (speed 3) (safety 1)))
  (let* ((bytes (coerce bytes '(simple-array (unsigned-byte 8) (*)))) (end (length bytes)) (pos 0))
    (declare (type (simple-array (unsigned-byte 8) (*)) bytes) (type fixnum end pos))
    (macrolet ((fail () '(return-from read-plain-datum :fallback)))
      (labels ((space-p (byte) (declare (type (unsigned-byte 8) byte)) (or (= byte 32) (<= 9 byte 10) (<= 12 byte 13)))
               (delimiter-p (byte)
                 (declare (type (unsigned-byte 8) byte))
                 (or (space-p byte) (= byte 40) (= byte 41) (= byte 34) (= byte 59) (= byte 39) (= byte 96) (= byte 44)))
               (skip (at) (declare (type fixnum at))
                 (loop while (and (< at end) (space-p (aref bytes at))) do (incf at)) at)
               (tail-byte (at) (declare (type fixnum at))
                 (if (< at end)
                     (let ((byte (aref bytes at))) (if (= (logand byte #xc0) #x80) (logand byte #x3f) (fail)))
                     (fail)))
               (code-at (at)
                 (declare (type fixnum at))
                 (let ((lead (aref bytes at)))
                   (cond ((< lead #x80) (values lead (+ at 1)))
                         ((<= #xc2 lead #xdf) (values (logior (ash (logand lead #x1f) 6) (tail-byte (+ at 1))) (+ at 2)))
                         ((<= #xe0 lead #xef)
                          (let ((code (logior (ash (logand lead #x0f) 12) (ash (tail-byte (+ at 1)) 6) (tail-byte (+ at 2)))))
                            (when (or (< code #x800) (<= #xd800 code #xdfff)) (fail))
                            (values code (+ at 3))))
                         ((<= #xf0 lead #xf4)
                          (let ((code (logior (ash (logand lead #x07) 18) (ash (tail-byte (+ at 1)) 12)
                                              (ash (tail-byte (+ at 2)) 6) (tail-byte (+ at 3)))))
                            (unless (<= #x10000 code #x10ffff) (fail))
                            (values code (+ at 4))))
                         (t (fail)))))
               (read-string ()
                 (let ((at pos) (count 0) (plain t))
                   (declare (type fixnum at count))
                   (loop (when (>= at end) (fail))
                         (let ((byte (aref bytes at)))
                           (cond ((= byte 34) (return))
                                 ((< byte #x80) (when (= byte 92) (setf plain nil) (incf at) (when (>= at end) (fail)))
                                  (setf at (nth-value 1 (code-at at))))
                                 (t (setf plain nil at (nth-value 1 (code-at at))))))
                         (incf count))
                   (let ((string (make-string count)))
                     (if plain
                         (dotimes (i count) (setf (schar string i) (code-char (aref bytes (+ pos i)))))
                         (let ((from pos))
                           (declare (type fixnum from))
                           (dotimes (i count)
                             (when (= (aref bytes from) 92) (incf from))
                             (multiple-value-bind (code next) (code-at from)
                               (setf (schar string i) (code-char code) from next)))))
                     (setf pos (1+ at))
                     string)))
               (read-token ()
                 (let ((start pos))
                   (declare (type fixnum start))
                   (loop while (and (< pos end) (not (delimiter-p (aref bytes pos)))) do (incf pos))
                   (let* ((sign (if (and (< start pos) (or (= (aref bytes start) 43) (= (aref bytes start) 45))) 1 0))
                          (digits (+ start sign)))
                     (declare (type fixnum digits))
                     (cond ((= start pos) (fail))
                           ((and (< digits pos) (<= (- pos digits) 18)
                                 (loop for i of-type fixnum from digits below pos always (<= 48 (aref bytes i) 57)))
                            (let ((n 0))
                              (declare (type (unsigned-byte 62) n))
                              (loop for i of-type fixnum from digits below pos
                                    do (setf n (+ (* n 10) (- (aref bytes i) 48))))
                              (if (and (= sign 1) (= (aref bytes start) 45)) (- n) n)))
                           ((and (= (- pos start) 3) (= (aref bytes start) 78) (= (aref bytes (+ start 1)) 73)
                                 (= (aref bytes (+ start 2)) 76))
                            nil)
                           ((and (= (- pos start) 1) (= (aref bytes start) 84)) t)
                           ((and (= (aref bytes start) 58) (< (1+ start) pos) (<= 65 (aref bytes (1+ start)) 90)
                                 (loop for i of-type fixnum from (1+ start) below pos
                                       always (let ((byte (aref bytes i))) (or (<= 65 byte 90) (<= 48 byte 57) (= byte 45)))))
                            (let ((name (make-string (- pos start 1) :element-type 'base-char)))
                              (loop for i of-type fixnum from (1+ start) below pos
                                    for j of-type fixnum from 0
                                    do (setf (schar name j) (code-char (aref bytes i))))
                              (values (intern name :keyword))))
                           (t (fail))))))
               (read-list ()
                 (let ((head nil) (tail nil))
                   (loop
                     (setf pos (skip pos))
                     (when (>= pos end) (fail))
                     (let ((byte (aref bytes pos)))
                       (cond ((= byte 41) (incf pos) (return head))
                             ((and (= byte 46) tail (< (1+ pos) end) (delimiter-p (aref bytes (1+ pos))))
                              (incf pos)
                              (setf (cdr tail) (read-datum) pos (skip pos))
                              (unless (and (< pos end) (= (aref bytes pos) 41)) (fail))
                              (incf pos)
                              (return head))
                             (t (let ((cell (list (read-datum))))
                                  (if tail (setf (cdr tail) cell) (setf head cell))
                                  (setf tail cell))))))))
               (read-datum ()
                 (setf pos (skip pos))
                 (when (>= pos end) (fail))
                 (let ((byte (aref bytes pos)))
                   (cond ((= byte 40) (incf pos) (read-list))
                         ((= byte 34) (incf pos) (read-string))
                         ((= byte 41) (fail))
                         (t (read-token))))))
        (let ((datum (read-datum)))
          (unless (= (skip pos) end) (fail))
          datum)))))
#+end_src

** src/worker.lisp
The single extension worker process and its bounded packet protocol.

#+begin_src lisp :tangle src/worker.lisp :mkdirp yes
(in-package #:ekko/runtime)

(defconstant +extension-packet-limit+ 65536)
;; The daemon/extension surface this binary speaks: context keys, actions and
;; decoration fields. A reload may run extension code from a newer ekko binary,
;; so bump this whenever that surface changes; a daemon refuses a worker whose
;; revision differs instead of dispatching data it cannot honour.
(defconstant +extension-revision+ 2)
(defstruct extension-worker process executable input output source path directory environment
  registry request deadline recovery initialization-context initialization-actions)
(defun config-path ()
  (or (uiop:getenv "EKKO_CONFIG")
      (format nil "~A/ekko/init.lisp" (or (uiop:getenv "XDG_CONFIG_HOME")
                                        (namestring (merge-pathnames ".config" (user-homedir-pathname)))))))
(defun config-source (path)
  (if (probe-file path)
      (with-open-file (in path)
        (when (> (file-length in) 32768) (error "Configuration exceeds 32 KiB: ~A" path))
        (let ((source (make-string (file-length in))))
          (subseq source 0 (read-sequence source in))))
      (if (uiop:getenv "EKKO_CONFIG") (error "Configuration does not exist: ~A" path) "")))
(defun extension-data (bytes)
  (when (> (length bytes) +extension-packet-limit+) (error "Extension message too large"))
  (let ((data (decode-scene bytes)) (budget 8192))
    (labels ((check (value depth)
               (when (or (minusp (decf budget)) (> depth 40)) (error "Extension data too complex"))
               (typecase value
                 (cons (check (first value) (1+ depth)) (check (rest value) depth))
                 (string (when (> (length value) 32768) (error "Extension string too long")))
                 (integer (unless (<= (- (expt 2 53)) value (expt 2 53)) (error "Extension integer too large")))
                 (symbol (unless (or (null value) (eq value t) (keywordp value)) (error "Extension symbol is not data")))
                 (t (error "Invalid extension value")))))
      (check data 0)) data))
(defun extension-send (wire value)
  (let ((bytes (encode-scene value)))
    (when (> (length bytes) +extension-packet-limit+) (error "Extension result exceeds 64 KiB"))
    (send-packet wire 30 bytes)))
(defun stop-worker (worker)
  (when worker
    (let ((process (extension-worker-process worker)))
      (when (sb-ext:process-alive-p process) (sb-ext:process-kill process 9))
      ;; The PTY reactor owns SIGCHLD and reaps by PID. SBCL's process-wait
      ;; depends on its own signal handler, which is intentionally disabled here.
      (loop while (= -11 (reap (sb-ext:process-pid process))) do (poll-fds nil 1))
      ;; Process streams own these descriptors; do not close them a second time.
      (sb-ext:process-close process))))
(defun start-worker (source path log &key directory (environment (sb-ext:posix-environ))
                                        (executable "/proc/self/exe"))
  ;; The workspace may supply another cwd and PATH. Launch the running host image,
  ;; not argv[0], which can be only the relative command used to start it, unless
  ;; a reload named another ekko binary to supply the extension code.
  (let* ((process (sb-ext:run-program executable '("--extension-worker")
                                    :directory directory :environment environment
                                    :wait nil :input ':stream :output ':stream :error log :if-error-exists ':append))
         (in (make-wire :fd (sb-sys:fd-stream-fd (sb-ext:process-output process)) :packet-limit (1+ +extension-packet-limit+)))
         (out (make-wire :fd (sb-sys:fd-stream-fd (sb-ext:process-input process))))
         (worker (make-extension-worker :process process :executable executable
                                        :input in :output out :source source :path path
                                        :directory directory :environment (copy-list environment)
                                        :deadline (+ (now) 5) :request ':load)) (ready nil))
    (unwind-protect
         (progn
           (checked (ekko/platform:nonblock (wire-fd in)))
           (checked (ekko/platform:nonblock (wire-fd out)))
           (extension-send out (list :load source path))
           (setf ready t) worker)
      (unless ready (stop-worker worker)))))
(defun worker-events (worker)
  (when worker
    (append (list (cons (wire-fd (extension-worker-input worker)) 1))
            (when (wire-queue (extension-worker-output worker))
              (list (cons (wire-fd (extension-worker-output worker)) 4))))))
(defun poll-worker (worker buffer)
  (unless (sb-ext:process-alive-p (extension-worker-process worker)) (error "Extension worker exited"))
  (flush-wire (extension-worker-output worker))
  (let ((packets (receive-packets (extension-worker-input worker) buffer)))
    (when (> (length packets) 1) (error "Unexpected extension responses"))
    (cond
      (packets
       (unless (= (aref (first packets) 0) 30) (error "Invalid extension packet"))
       (extension-data (subseq (first packets) 1)))
      ;; The reactor may have been descheduled past the deadline while the
      ;; worker completed normally. Consume a ready response before timing out.
      ((and (extension-worker-request worker) (> (now) (extension-worker-deadline worker)))
       ;; Requests retain the actual originating view. Printing the complete
       ;; request would recurse through view -> daemon -> worker -> request.
       (error "Extension callback timed out")))))
(defun ready-registry (response)
  "The registry from a worker's :ready RESPONSE, refusing another extension revision."
  (unless (eq (first response) :ready) (error "~A" (or (second response) "Invalid extension load reply")))
  (unless (eql (third response) +extension-revision+)
    (error "Extension code speaks revision ~A but this daemon speaks ~A; upgrade the daemon (ekko stop, then start)"
           (or (third response) 0) +extension-revision+))
  (second response))
(defun load-worker (source path log &key directory (environment (sb-ext:posix-environ)))
  ;; Startup/check only: no running PTYs are held up by this wait. Reload uses
  ;; the normal reactor and swaps the candidate only after validation.
  (let ((worker (start-worker source path log :directory directory :environment environment))
        (ready nil) (buffer (octets 65536)))
    (unwind-protect
         (loop for response = (poll-worker worker buffer) do
           (when response
             (when (eq (first response) :error) (error "~A: ~A" path (second response)))
             (setf (extension-worker-registry worker) (ready-registry response)
                   (extension-worker-request worker) nil ready t)
             (return worker))
           (poll-fds (worker-events worker) 10))
      (unless ready (stop-worker worker)))))

(defun extension-worker-main ()
  (initialize)
  (checked (ekko/platform:nonblock 0)) (checked (ekko/platform:nonblock 1))
  (let ((input (make-wire :fd 0 :packet-limit (1+ +extension-packet-limit+))) (output (make-wire :fd 1)) (buffer (octets 65536)))
    (loop
      (dolist (packet (receive-packets input buffer))
        (let ((*standard-output* *error-output*) (*trace-output* *error-output*))
          (handler-case
              (let ((request (extension-data (subseq packet 1))))
                (case (first request)
                  (:load
                   (setf ekko/extensions:*components* nil)
                   (let ((package (find-package :ekko/builtins)))
                     (when package (funcall (find-symbol "INSTALL" package))))
                   (let* ((path (pathname (third request)))
                          (*default-pathname-defaults* (uiop:pathname-directory-pathname path)))
                     (load (make-string-input-stream (second request)) :verbose nil :print nil))
                   (extension-send output (list :ready (ekko/extensions:registry) +extension-revision+)))
                  (:initialize
                   (extension-send output
                     (list :initialized
                       (loop for c in (getf (ekko/extensions:registry) :components)
                             when (getf c :initialize) collect
                             (ekko/extensions:dispatch :initialize (getf c :id)
                                                       (second request) (third request))))))
                  (:dispatch
                   (extension-send output
                     (list :result (apply #'ekko/extensions:dispatch (rest request)))))
                  (otherwise (error "Unknown extension request"))))
            (error (e) (extension-send output (list :error (subseq (princ-to-string e) 0 (min 2000 (length (princ-to-string e))))))))))
      (flush-wire output)
      (poll-fds (append (list '(0 . 1)) (when (wire-queue output) (list '(1 . 4)))) 1000))))
#+end_src

** src/layout-policy.lisp
Per-view layout state; mounts and unmounts provider contributions.

#+begin_src lisp :tangle src/layout-policy.lisp :mkdirp yes
(in-package #:ekko/runtime)

;; One state per view. The owner contribution is reconstructible; terminal
;; contents and PTYs are not owned by a mounted layout provider.
(defstruct layout-state owner provider placements (camera '(0 0))
  requested-context requested-token committed-context committed-token error)
(defstruct layout-request owner provider reads snapshot token)
(defconstant +layout-deadline+ 1/2)
(defconstant +layout-coordinate-limit+ 1000000)

(defun layout-provider (registry)
  (let ((name (getf (getf registry :options) :layout-provider)))
    (when name
      (or (find name (reverse (getf registry :layout-providers))
                :key (lambda (entry) (getf entry :name)) :test #'equal)
          (error "Selected layout provider is not registered: ~A" name)))))

(defun policy-plist (value keys required description)
  "Check a proper, unique-key property list once at the worker boundary."
  (unless (and (listp value) (evenp (length value)))
    (error "Malformed ~A" description))
  (let ((seen nil))
    (loop for (key item) on value by #'cddr do
      (unless (and (member key keys) (not (member key seen)))
        (error "Invalid or duplicate ~A key: ~S" description key))
      (push key seen))
    (unless (every (lambda (key) (member key seen)) required)
      (error "Missing ~A field" description)))
  value)

(defun validate-layout-registry (registry)
  (let* ((providers (getf registry :layout-providers))
         (options (getf registry :options))
         (budget (getf options :pane-budget 16)))
    (unless (and (listp providers) (<= (length providers) 64)
                 (typep budget '(integer 1 128)))
      (error "Invalid layout registry or workspace budget"))
    (dolist (provider providers)
      (policy-plist provider '(:name :owner :api-version :reads)
                    '(:name :owner :api-version :reads) "layout registration")
      (let ((reads (getf provider :reads)))
        (unless (and (stringp (getf provider :name))
                     (eql (getf provider :api-version) 1)
                     (find (getf provider :owner) (getf registry :components)
                           :key (lambda (entry) (getf entry :id)) :test #'equal)
                     (listp reads)
                     (= (length reads) (length (remove-duplicates reads)))
                     (every (lambda (key) (member key ekko/extensions:*context-keys*)) reads)
                     (every (lambda (key) (member key reads)) '(:panes :focus :viewport :geometry)))
          (error "Invalid layout provider owner, version or dependencies"))))
    (let ((initial (getf options :initial-layout)))
      (when (and initial (> (length (ekko/extensions:initial-layout-leaves initial)) budget))
        (error "Initial layout exceeds the configured pane budget")))
    (layout-provider registry))
  t)

(defun reset-layout-state (state registry)
  (let* ((provider (layout-provider registry))
         (owner (getf provider :owner)) (name (getf provider :name)))
    (unless (and (equal owner (layout-state-owner state))
                 (equal name (layout-state-provider state)))
      (setf (layout-state-owner state) owner (layout-state-provider state) name
            (layout-state-placements state) nil (layout-state-camera state) '(0 0)
            (layout-state-requested-context state) nil (layout-state-requested-token state) nil
            (layout-state-committed-context state) nil (layout-state-committed-token state) nil
            (layout-state-error state) nil)
      t)))

(defun layout-pane-metadata (pane)
  ;; Do not feed committed projection, PTY arbitration, output counters or
  ;; history length back into the provider's own structural dependency.
  (loop for key in '(:id :session :name :label :argv :launch-kind :creation-position
                    :minimized :floating :activation-order :terminal-title :activity :pid :exit)
        when (member key pane) append (list key (ekko/extensions:copy-data (getf pane key)))))

(defun layout-policy-context (provider context)
  (loop for key in (getf provider :reads)
        for value = (getf context key)
        append (list key
                     (if (member key '(:panes :all-panes))
                         (mapcar #'layout-pane-metadata value)
                         (ekko/extensions:copy-data value)))))

(defun schedule-layout (worker state registry context token)
  "Queue one detached provider dispatch. TOKEN binds it to a live view epoch."
  (reset-layout-state state registry)
  (let* ((provider (layout-provider registry))
         (snapshot (and provider (layout-policy-context provider context))))
    (when (and provider worker (null (extension-worker-request worker))
               (or (not (equal snapshot (layout-state-requested-context state)))
                   (not (equal token (layout-state-requested-token state)))))
      (let ((request (make-layout-request :owner (getf provider :owner)
                                         :provider (getf provider :name)
                                         :reads (copy-list (getf provider :reads))
                                         :snapshot snapshot :token token)))
        ;; Mark a failed input as seen too. A bad callback must not starve
        ;; commands or keep restarting the worker for an unchanged context.
        (setf (layout-state-requested-context state) snapshot
              (layout-state-requested-token state) (copy-list token))
        (handler-case
            (extension-send (extension-worker-output worker)
                            (list :dispatch :layout (getf provider :name) snapshot
                                  (list :type :layout :version 1)))
          (error (condition)
            (setf (layout-state-error state) (princ-to-string condition))
            (error condition)))
        (setf (extension-worker-request worker) request
              (extension-worker-deadline worker) (+ (now) +layout-deadline+))
        request))))

(defun layout-point-p (value)
  (and (listp value) (= (length value) 2)
       (every (lambda (n) (and (integerp n)
                              (<= (- +layout-coordinate-limit+) n +layout-coordinate-limit+))) value)))

(defun validate-layout-actions (actions panes)
  "Return detached placements and camera only after the entire action is valid."
  (unless (and (listp actions) (= (length actions) 1)
               (consp (first actions)) (eq (caar actions) :place-panes))
    (error "A layout callback must return one :PLACE-PANES action"))
  (let* ((args (policy-plist (cdar actions) '(:version :placements :camera)
                             '(:version :placements :camera) "layout action"))
         (placements (getf args :placements)) (camera (getf args :camera))
         (ids (mapcar (lambda (pane) (getf pane :id)) panes)) (seen nil))
    (unless (and (eql (getf args :version) 1) (layout-point-p camera)
                 (listp placements) (<= (length placements) 128))
      (error "Invalid layout version, camera or placement count"))
    (dolist (placement placements)
      (policy-plist placement '(:pane :x :y :cols :rows :outer :visible)
                    '(:pane :x :y :cols :rows :outer) "pane placement")
      (let ((id (getf placement :pane)) (x (getf placement :x)) (y (getf placement :y))
            (cols (getf placement :cols)) (rows (getf placement :rows))
            (outer (getf placement :outer)))
        (unless (and (integerp id) (member id ids) (not (member id seen))
                     (member (getf placement :visible t) '(nil t))
                     (layout-point-p (list x y))
                     (typep cols '(integer 1 500)) (typep rows '(integer 1 300))
                     (listp outer) (= (length outer) 4)
                     (layout-point-p (subseq outer 0 2))
                     (typep (third outer) '(integer 1 532))
                     (typep (fourth outer) '(integer 1 332)))
          (error "Invalid pane identity, dimensions or logical rectangle"))
        (destructuring-bind (ox oy width height) outer
          (unless (and (<= ox x) (<= oy y)
                       (<= (+ x cols) (+ ox width)) (<= (+ y rows) (+ oy height)))
            (error "Pane content must fit its full logical outer rectangle")))
        (push id seen)))
    (values (ekko/extensions:copy-data placements) (copy-list camera))))

(defun layout-request-current-p (request registry context token)
  (let ((provider (layout-provider registry)))
    (and (equal token (layout-request-token request))
         (equal (getf provider :owner) (layout-request-owner request))
         (equal (getf provider :name) (layout-request-provider request))
         (equal (getf provider :reads) (layout-request-reads request))
         (equal (layout-policy-context provider context) (layout-request-snapshot request)))))

(defun complete-layout (state request registry context token response)
  "Validate a worker response and commit its owner contribution, never a VT."
  (unless (layout-request-current-p request registry context token)
    (return-from complete-layout :stale))
  (unless (and (listp response) (= (length response) 2) (eq (first response) :result))
    (error "Invalid layout callback response: ~S" response))
  (let ((result (policy-plist (second response) '(:owner :actions)
                              '(:owner :actions) "layout response")))
    (unless (equal (getf result :owner) (layout-request-owner request))
      (error "Layout callback returned the wrong owner"))
    (multiple-value-bind (placements camera)
        (validate-layout-actions (getf result :actions) (getf context :panes))
      (setf (layout-state-owner state) (layout-request-owner request)
            (layout-state-provider state) (layout-request-provider request)
            (layout-state-placements state) placements (layout-state-camera state) camera
            (layout-state-committed-context state) (layout-request-snapshot request)
            (layout-state-committed-token state) (copy-list token)
            (layout-state-error state) nil)))
  :committed)

(defun layout-state-current-p (state registry context token)
  "A successful logical layout must still match every declared dependency."
  (let ((provider (layout-provider registry)))
    (or (null provider)
        (and (equal (layout-state-owner state) (getf provider :owner))
             (equal (layout-state-provider state) (getf provider :name))
             (equal (layout-state-committed-token state) token)
             (equal (layout-state-committed-context state)
                    (layout-policy-context provider context))))))

(defun fail-layout (state request error)
  (when (and (equal (layout-state-owner state) (layout-request-owner request))
             (equal (layout-state-provider state) (layout-request-provider request)))
    (setf (layout-state-error state) (princ-to-string error)))
  nil)
#+end_src

** src/store.lisp
Durable per-component state: one file per namespace, atomically replaced.

#+begin_src lisp :tangle src/store.lisp :mkdirp yes
;;; Durable per-component state: one file per namespace, atomic replacement.
;;; The directory lives outside the daemon runtime directory so the values
;;; survive daemon restarts. Nothing here trusts file contents: values are
;;; read with *READ-EVAL* disabled and bounded before they enter the workspace.
(defpackage #:ekko/store
  (:use #:cl)
  (:export #:*directory* #:store-directory #:store-file #:store-load #:store-write #:store-namespace))
(in-package #:ekko/store)

(defparameter +namespace-limit+ 256)
(defparameter +file-limit+ (* 64 1024))

(defvar *directory* nil)

(defun store-directory (&optional (instance "default"))
  "Resolve a validated INSTANCE once; EKKO_STORE_DIR always overrides it.
The daemon binds *DIRECTORY* to this result for its entire lifetime."
  (or *directory*
      (let* ((override (uiop:getenv "EKKO_STORE_DIR"))
             (state (uiop:getenv "XDG_STATE_HOME"))
             (base (if override
                       (uiop:ensure-directory-pathname override)
                       (if (and state (plusp (length state)))
                           (merge-pathnames "ekko/store/" (uiop:ensure-directory-pathname state))
                           (merge-pathnames ".local/state/ekko/store/" (user-homedir-pathname))))))
        (if (or override (string= instance "default")) base
            (merge-pathnames (format nil "instances/~A/" instance) base)))))

(defun store-namespace (owner)
  "Namespace string for a component owner, or NIL without one."
  (when owner
    (let ((name (string-downcase (string owner))))
      (when (> (length name) +namespace-limit+) (error "Store namespace is too long"))
      name)))

(defun store-file (namespace)
  ;; Percent-encode everything outside a safe set so a namespace can never
  ;; traverse out of the store directory or collide by case.
  (when (zerop (length namespace)) (error "Store namespace is empty"))
  (let ((name (with-output-to-string (out)
                (loop for c across namespace
                      do (if (or (alphanumericp c) (find c "-_." :test #'char=))
                             (write-char c out)
                             (format out "%~2,'0X" (char-code c)))))))
    (merge-pathnames (concatenate 'string name ".lisp") (store-directory))))

(defun private-directory (path)
  ;; The store is private to the user; refuse to write through a directory
  ;; owned by somebody else, mirroring the runtime and asset directories.
  (ensure-directories-exist path)
  (let* ((name (string-right-trim "/" (namestring path)))
         (st (sb-posix:lstat name)))
    (unless (and (sb-posix:s-isdir (sb-posix:stat-mode st))
                 (= (sb-posix:stat-uid st) (sb-posix:getuid)))
      (error "Unsafe store directory ~A" name))
    (sb-posix:chmod name #o700)
    path))

(defun read-entry (path)
  "Return (namespace . entries) for one store file, or NIL when unusable."
  (handler-case
      (let ((size (with-open-file (in path :element-type '(unsigned-byte 8))
                    (file-length in))))
        (when (> size +file-limit+) (error "Store file exceeds 64 KiB"))
        (let ((data (with-open-file (in path)
                      (with-standard-io-syntax
                        (let ((*read-eval* nil) (*package* (find-package :ekko/store)))
                          (read in nil nil))))))
          (destructuring-bind (marker version namespace entries) data
            (unless (and (eq marker :ekko-store) (eql version 1)
                         (stringp namespace) (<= (length namespace) +namespace-limit+)
                         (listp entries))
              (error "Invalid store file"))
            (unless (loop for entry in entries
                          always (and (consp entry)
                                      (or (stringp (first entry)) (keywordp (first entry)))))
              (error "Invalid store entry"))
            (cons namespace entries))))
    (error (e) (format *error-output* "store: ~A: ~A~%" path e) nil)))

(defun store-load ()
  "Load every readable namespace in the store directory."
  (let ((directory (store-directory)))
    (when (probe-file directory)
      (loop for path in (directory (merge-pathnames "*.lisp" directory))
            for entry = (read-entry path)
            when entry collect entry))))

(defun store-write (namespace entries)
  "Replace one namespace atomically. Signals on any filesystem failure."
  (let* ((path (store-file namespace))
         (temp (concatenate 'string (namestring path) ".tmp")))
    (private-directory (store-directory))
    (with-open-file (out temp :direction ':output :if-exists ':supersede :if-does-not-exist ':create)
      (with-standard-io-syntax
        (let ((*print-readably* nil) (*print-pretty* nil))
          (write (list :ekko-store 1 (coerce namespace 'simple-string) entries) :stream out)))
      (finish-output out))
    (sb-posix:rename temp (namestring path))
    path))
#+end_src

** src/views.lisp
Views over panes: mode, geometry and ownership, with no aliasing.

#+begin_src lisp :tangle src/views.lisp :mkdirp yes
(in-package #:ekko/runtime)

;; Pane content survives every view. Nothing below aliases interaction fields
;; into a pane or temporarily installs a view into shared workspace state.
;; One daemon owns one workspace: every pane, view and component lives there.
(defstruct pane id daemon pid io vt (graphics (make-store)) argv label status floating
  (launch-kind :command) (creation-position 0) name pty-size sync-until
  (output-bytes 0) (application-focused nil) row-cache)
(defstruct store-state entries)
(defstruct pane-view pane-id (x 0) (y 0) (cols 0) (rows 0)
  (outer-x 0) (outer-y 0) (outer-cols 0) (outer-rows 0)
  minimized (activation-order 0) (unseen-output nil)
  copy-cells copy-pointer copy-anchor copy-end copy-flash-until copy-lines
  (copy-cursor 0) (copy-top 0) copy-mark search-input (search-text ""))
(defstruct view daemon wire id (epoch 0) focus-id
  (cols 120) (rows 36) (cw 8) (ch 16) reported-cw reported-ch
  pty-reported-cw pty-reported-ch
  (host-focused t) (revision 0) (activation-sequence 0)
  (pane-states (make-hash-table)) mode zoom prefix
  drag window-drag window-hover chrome-press popup transition paste-target last-click
  paste-fallback paste-buffer paste-overflow key-fragment key-fragment-mode
  input-read-framed input-read-bytes input-queue (input-bytes 0)
  (notice "") error geometry-contributions contributions decorations pane-notes
  component-state hook-context hooks (layout-state (make-layout-state))
  layout-placements (camera-x 0) (camera-y 0) motions tweens glows ghosts slides redecorated popup-ghost animating
  initialized extension-initialized initialization-failure quarantined)
(defstruct command-origin daemon view epoch target-pane-id peer config-generation layout)
(defstruct command-request origin name event snapshot)
(defstruct hook-request origin owner snapshot)
(defstruct pending-split pane origin owner actions)

(defun daemon-store-entries (daemon)
  (store-state-entries (daemon-store daemon)))
(defun (setf daemon-store-entries) (entries daemon)
  (setf (store-state-entries (daemon-store daemon)) entries))
(defun pane-by-id (daemon id)
  (find id (daemon-panes daemon) :key #'pane-id))
(defun view-panes (view)
  (daemon-panes (view-daemon view)))
(defun pane-state (view id &optional (create t))
  (or (gethash id (view-pane-states view))
      (let ((pane (pane-by-id (view-daemon view) id)))
        (unless pane (error "Unknown pane: ~S" id))
        (let ((state (make-pane-view :pane-id id)))
          (when create (setf (gethash id (view-pane-states view)) state))
          state))))
(defun focused-pane (view)
  (pane-by-id (view-daemon view) (view-focus-id view)))
(defun capture-origin (view &optional peer target)
  (make-command-origin :daemon (view-daemon view) :view view :epoch (view-epoch view)
                       :target-pane-id (or target (view-focus-id view)) :peer peer
                       :config-generation (daemon-config-generation (view-daemon view))
                       :layout (copy-tree (daemon-tree (view-daemon view)))))
(defun origin-current-p (origin)
  (let ((view (command-origin-view origin)) (daemon (command-origin-daemon origin)))
    (and (eq daemon (view-daemon view)) (member view (daemon-views daemon))
         (= (command-origin-epoch origin) (view-epoch view))
         (= (command-origin-config-generation origin) (daemon-config-generation daemon))
         (not (daemon-stopping daemon)))))
(defun origin-target (origin)
  (or (pane-by-id (command-origin-daemon origin) (command-origin-target-pane-id origin))
      (error "Command target pane no longer exists")))
(defun initialize-view (view)
  (let ((daemon (view-daemon view)))
    (pushnew view (daemon-views daemon))
    (unless (daemon-home-view daemon) (setf (daemon-home-view daemon) view))
    (unless (view-focus-id view)
      (setf (view-focus-id view) (and (first (view-panes view)) (pane-id (first (view-panes view))))))
    (dolist (pane (view-panes view)) (pane-state view (pane-id pane)))
    (unless (view-initialized view)
      (setf (view-mode view) (option daemon :initial-keymap)
            (view-initialized view) t)
      (pushnew view (daemon-initialization-queue daemon)))
    (layout view))
  view)
(defun reset-view-input (view &optional preserve-queue)
  (setf (view-prefix view) nil (view-drag view) nil (view-window-drag view) nil
        (view-window-hover view) nil (view-chrome-press view) nil (view-popup view) nil
        (view-transition view) nil (view-paste-target view) nil (view-paste-fallback view) nil
        (view-paste-buffer view) nil (view-paste-overflow view) nil
        (view-key-fragment view) nil (view-key-fragment-mode view) nil
        (view-input-read-framed view) nil (view-input-read-bytes view) nil)
  (unless preserve-queue (setf (view-input-queue view) nil (view-input-bytes view) 0)))
(defun attach-view (daemon wire id cols rows cw ch &optional existing-view)
  (let* ((home (daemon-home-view daemon))
         (view (or existing-view home
                   (make-view :daemon daemon :id id :cols cols :rows rows :cw cw :ch ch))))
    (unless (eq daemon (view-daemon view)) (error "View belongs to another daemon"))
    ;; One attached client at a time: an attach takes over the session view.
    ;; A wired view's client drains to a clean disconnect rather than an
    ;; error, which its attach loop would retry into a steal-back.
    (dolist (other (daemon-views daemon))
      (when (and (not (eq other view)) (view-wire other))
        (setf (wire-closing (view-wire other)) t)
        (detach-view other)))
    (when (view-wire view)
      (setf (wire-closing (view-wire view)) t)
      (detach-view view))
    (setf (view-wire view) wire (wire-view wire) view (view-host-focused view) t)
    (initialize-view view)
    (resize-view view cols rows cw ch)
    (update-application-focus daemon)
    view))
(defun detach-view (view)
  (let ((wire (view-wire view)) (daemon (view-daemon view)))
    (when wire (setf (wire-view wire) nil))
    (setf (view-wire view) nil (view-host-focused view) nil)
    (incf (view-epoch view))
    (reset-view-input view)
    (setf (view-hooks view) nil (view-hook-context view) nil)
    (reconcile-size daemon)
    (update-application-focus daemon)
    ;; The workspace persists across detach: PTYs outlive every client.
    ;; Only `stop', the last pane exiting, or a signal tears it down.
    (trim-detached-views daemon)))
(defun resize-view (view cols rows cw ch)
  (setf (view-cols view) cols (view-rows view) rows (view-cw view) cw (view-ch view) ch)
  (layout view))
(defun view-live-p (view)
  (not (null (view-wire view))))
(defun update-application-focus (daemon)
  (dolist (pane (daemon-panes daemon))
    (let ((focused (not (null (pane-watched-p daemon pane)))))
      (unless (eql focused (pane-application-focused pane))
        (setf (pane-application-focused pane) focused)
        (when focused (change-notifications daemon :read-pane (pane-id pane)))
        (when (gethash 1004 (terminal-modes (pane-vt pane)))
          (pane-input pane (text-bytes (format nil "~C[~A" #\Esc (if focused "I" "O")))))))))
(defun note-pane-output (daemon pane)
  (dolist (view (daemon-views daemon))
    (when (and (member pane (view-panes view)) (not (eql (pane-id pane) (view-focus-id view))))
      (setf (pane-view-unseen-output (pane-state view (pane-id pane))) t))))
(defun pane-watched-p (daemon pane)
  (some (lambda (view) (and (view-live-p view) (view-host-focused view)
                            (eql (view-focus-id view) (pane-id pane))))
        (daemon-views daemon)))
(defun notification-text (text limit)
  (let ((clean (substitute-if #\Space (lambda (c) (or (< (char-code c) 32) (<= 127 (char-code c) 159)))
                              (string-trim '(#\Space) (or text "")))))
    (subseq clean 0 (min limit (length clean)))))
(defun record-notification (pane message)
  "Keep the newest 64 notifications. MESSAGE is (title body), or nil for BEL.
A bell from a pane someone is watching is only a beep; an explicit message
from it is stored already read. Repeated unread bells coalesce."
  (let* ((daemon (pane-daemon pane)) (watched (pane-watched-p daemon pane))
         (live (remove-if-not (lambda (n) (pane-by-id daemon (getf n :pane)))
                              (daemon-notifications daemon)))
         (previous (find (pane-id pane) live :key (lambda (n) (getf n :pane)))))
    (cond ((and (null message) watched))
          ((and (null message) previous (getf previous :bell) (not (getf previous :read)))
           (setf (getf previous :time) (get-universal-time))
           (incf (getf previous :count))
           (setf (daemon-notifications daemon) (cons previous (remove previous live))))
          (t
           (push (list :id (daemon-next-notification-id daemon) :pane (pane-id pane)
                       :time (get-universal-time) :bell (null message) :count 1
                       :title (notification-text (first message) 80)
                       :body (notification-text (second message) 240)
                       :read (not (null watched)))
                 live)
           (incf (daemon-next-notification-id daemon))
           (setf (daemon-notifications daemon) (subseq live 0 (min 64 (length live))))))))
(defun change-notifications (daemon op &optional id)
  "Apply OP (:read, :dismiss, :read-pane, :read-all or :clear) to ID."
  (let ((key (if (eq op :read-pane) :pane :id)))
    (ecase op
      (:clear (setf (daemon-notifications daemon) nil))
      (:dismiss (setf (daemon-notifications daemon)
                      (remove id (daemon-notifications daemon) :key (lambda (n) (getf n :id)))))
      ((:read :read-pane :read-all)
       (dolist (n (daemon-notifications daemon))
         (when (or (eq op :read-all) (eql id (getf n key)))
           (setf (getf n :read) t)))))))
(defun prune-view-pane (view id)
  (unless (or (gethash id (view-pane-states view)) (eql id (view-focus-id view)))
    (return-from prune-view-pane))
  (remhash id (view-pane-states view))
  (setf (view-layout-placements view)
        (remove id (view-layout-placements view) :key (lambda (p) (getf p :pane))))
  (when (eql (view-focus-id view) id)
    (setf (view-focus-id view) (and (first (view-panes view)) (pane-id (first (view-panes view))))))
  (reset-view-input view)
  (expire-pane-notes view)
  (incf (view-revision view)))
#+end_src

** src/server.lisp
The daemon's single-workspace server core and PTY sizing.

#+begin_src lisp :tangle src/server.lisp :mkdirp yes
(in-package #:ekko/runtime)

(defun pty-cell-size (view)
  (if (eq (option (view-daemon view) :pty-pixel-source ':effective) :reported)
      (values (or (view-pty-reported-cw view) 0) (or (view-pty-reported-ch view) 0))
      (values (view-cw view) (view-ch view))))
(defun pty-size-for (view cols rows)
  (multiple-value-bind (cw ch) (pty-cell-size view)
    ;; winsize pixel fields are unsigned 16-bit values in the platform ABI.
    (list cols rows (logand #xffff (* cols cw)) (logand #xffff (* rows ch)))))
(defun paste-policy (view)
  (let ((map (view-mode view)))
    (getf (find map (reverse (getf (daemon-registry (view-daemon view)) :keymaps))
              :key (lambda (entry) (getf entry :name))) :unbound)))
(defun finish-paste-fallback (view)
  (let ((command (view-paste-fallback view))
        (bytes (view-paste-buffer view))
        (overflow (view-paste-overflow view)))
    (setf (view-paste-fallback view) nil (view-paste-buffer view) nil
          (view-paste-overflow view) nil)
    (when (and (stringp command) (not overflow))
      (request-command view command (list :key nil :bytes (coerce bytes 'list) :paste t) nil))))
(defun expire-pane-notes (view &optional (time (now)))
  (let ((remaining (remove-if (lambda (note)
                                (or (<= (getf note :until) time)
                                    (not (pane-by-id (view-daemon view) (getf note :pane)))))
                              (view-pane-notes view))))
    (unless (equal remaining (view-pane-notes view))
      (setf (view-pane-notes view) remaining)
      (incf (view-revision view)))))
(defun pane-note-data (view &optional (registry (daemon-registry (view-daemon view))))
  (loop for component in (getf registry :components)
        append (loop for note in (view-pane-notes view)
                     when (equal (getf component :id) (getf note :owner))
                       collect (list :owner (getf note :owner) :pane (getf note :pane)
                                     :text (copy-seq (getf note :text))
                                     :sgr (copy-list (getf note :sgr))))))
(defun pane-input (pane bytes)
  (when (and (null (pane-status pane)) (pane-io pane))
    (when (> (+ (wire-queued (pane-io pane)) (length bytes)) 65536) (error "Pane input queue full"))
    (queue-bytes (pane-io pane) bytes)))
(defun pane-event (pane kind value)
  (let ((vt (pane-vt pane)) (graphics (pane-graphics pane)))
    (case kind
      (:reply (pane-input pane (text-bytes value)))
      (:graphics (accept-command graphics value vt (lambda (k v) (pane-event pane k v))))
      (:scroll (destructuring-bind (top bottom amount) value
                 (scroll-images graphics (terminal-screen vt) top bottom amount (terminal-cw vt) (terminal-ch vt))))
      (:screen (when (eq value :main) (clear-screen graphics :alternate)))
      (:sync
       ;; Hold scene publication until the application closes its frame; the
       ;; deadline is a safety valve for a frame that never closes.  An expired
       ;; deadline stays expired until the mode clears, so the next frame arms
       ;; a fresh one.
       (setf (pane-sync-until pane) (if value (+ (now) 1) nil)))
      (:notify (record-notification pane value))
      (:reset (clear-screen graphics :main) (clear-screen graphics :alternate)))))
(defun pane-mid-frame-p (pane)
  (let ((until (pane-sync-until pane)))
    (and (numberp until) (< (now) until))))
(defun geometry-value (view key default &optional (registry (daemon-registry (view-daemon view))))
  "Resolve runtime owner geometry in registry order, then static options."
  (loop for component in (reverse (getf registry :components))
        for owner = (getf component :id)
        for entry = (assoc owner (view-geometry-contributions view) :test #'equal)
        when (and entry (member key (rest entry)))
          do (return-from geometry-value (copy-tree (getf (rest entry) key))))
  (copy-tree (getf (getf registry :options) key default)))
(defun geometry-option (view key default length &optional (registry (daemon-registry (view-daemon view))))
  (let ((value (geometry-value view key default registry)))
    (unless (and (listp value) (= (length value) length)
                 (every (lambda (n) (and (integerp n) (<= 0 n 16))) value))
      (error "Invalid geometry option ~S: ~S" key value))
    (copy-list value)))
(defun effective-insets (width height requested)
  "Cap top and left first, then bottom and right, leaving one cell."
  (let* ((top (min (first requested) (max 0 (1- height))))
         (left (min (fourth requested) (max 0 (1- width))))
         (bottom (min (third requested) (max 0 (- height top 1))))
         (right (min (second requested) (max 0 (- width left 1)))))
    (list top right bottom left)))
(defun requested-pane-insets (view x y width height)
  (let ((pane (geometry-option view :pane-insets '(1 0 0 0) 4))
        (boundary (geometry-value view :boundary-insets nil)))
    (when (and boundary x y)
      (multiple-value-bind (viewport ignored gaps vw vh) (workspace-geometry view)
        (declare (ignore ignored gaps))
        (loop for outside in (list (= y (first viewport))
                                   (= (+ x width) (+ (fourth viewport) vw))
                                   (= (+ y height) (+ (first viewport) vh))
                                   (= x (fourth viewport)))
              for offset from 0
              when outside do (setf (nth offset pane) (nth offset boundary)))))
    pane))
(defun content-size (view width height &optional x y)
  (let ((insets (effective-insets width height
                                (requested-pane-insets view x y width height))))
    (values (max 1 (- width (second insets) (fourth insets)))
            (max 1 (- height (first insets) (third insets))))))
(defun pane-minimums (tree pane boundary)
  ;; Tree topology identifies viewport edges before allocating rectangles.
  (labels ((walk (node edges)
             (if (integerp node)
                 (let ((insets (loop for inside in pane for outside in (or boundary pane)
                                     for edge in edges collect (if edge outside inside))))
                   (list (cons node (list (+ 1 (second insets) (fourth insets))
                                          (+ 1 (first insets) (third insets))))))
                 (let ((a (copy-list edges)) (b (copy-list edges)))
                   (if (eq (first node) :columns)
                       (setf (second a) nil (fourth b) nil)
                       (setf (third a) nil (first b) nil))
                   (append (walk (third node) a) (walk (fourth node) b))))))
    (walk tree '(t t t t))))
(defun workspace-geometry (view &optional (registry (daemon-registry (view-daemon view))))
  (let* ((viewport (effective-insets
                   (view-cols view) (view-rows view)
                   (geometry-option view :viewport-insets '(0 0 1 0) 4 registry)))
         (pane (geometry-option view :pane-insets '(1 0 0 0) 4 registry))
         (gaps (geometry-option view :split-gaps '(1 0) 2 registry))
         (width (max 1 (- (view-cols view) (second viewport) (fourth viewport))))
         (height (max 1 (- (view-rows view) (first viewport) (third viewport)))))
    (values viewport pane gaps width height)))
(defun tiled-tree (view &optional (tree (daemon-tree (view-daemon view))))
  (dolist (pane (daemon-panes (view-daemon view)) tree)
    (when (and tree (or (pane-view-minimized (pane-state view (pane-id pane) nil)) (pane-floating pane)))
      (setf tree (ekko/layout:remove-pane tree (pane-id pane))))))
(defun clamp-window-rect (view rect &optional (registry (daemon-registry (view-daemon view))))
  (multiple-value-bind (viewport pane gaps width height) (workspace-geometry view registry)
    (declare (ignore pane gaps))
    (destructuring-bind (x y w h) rect
      (let ((w (min width (max 12 w))) (h (min height (max 4 h))))
        (list (max (fourth viewport) (min x (+ (fourth viewport) width (- w))))
              (max (first viewport) (min y (+ (first viewport) height (- h)))) w h)))))
(defun workspace-rectangles (view &optional (zoom (view-zoom view)) (tree (tiled-tree view))
                                (registry (daemon-registry (view-daemon view))))
  (multiple-value-bind (viewport pane gaps width height) (workspace-geometry view registry)
    (let* ((left (fourth viewport)) (top (first viewport))
           (focus (focused-pane view))
           (minimums (when tree (pane-minimums tree pane (geometry-value view :boundary-insets nil registry)))))
      (when (and zoom (not (pane-view-minimized (pane-state view (pane-id focus) nil))))
        (return-from workspace-rectangles (list (list (pane-id focus) left top width height))))
      (append
        (when tree
          (mapcar (lambda (rect) (list (first rect) (+ left (second rect)) (+ top (third rect))
                                      (fourth rect) (fifth rect)))
                  (ekko/layout:rectangles tree width height
                    (if (and (not (pane-floating focus)) (not (pane-view-minimized (pane-state view (pane-id focus) nil))))
                        (pane-id focus) (first (layout-pane-ids tree))) nil
                    :leaf-min (lambda (id) (rest (assoc id minimums)))
                    :column-gap (first gaps) :row-gap (second gaps))))
        (loop for p in (sort (copy-list (daemon-panes (view-daemon view))) #'< :key (lambda (p) (pane-view-activation-order (pane-state view (pane-id p) nil))))
              when (and (pane-floating p) (not (pane-view-minimized (pane-state view (pane-id p) nil))))
                collect (cons (pane-id p) (clamp-window-rect view (pane-floating p) registry)))))))
(defun pane-on-screen-p (view pane)
  "Whether PANE shows, where it is shown: a window moving off stays until it has gone."
  (multiple-value-bind (viewport insets gaps width height) (workspace-geometry view)
    (declare (ignore insets gaps))
    (destructuring-bind (x y w h) (shown-rect view (pane-id pane))
      (let ((left (fourth viewport)) (top (first viewport)))
        (and (plusp w) (plusp h) (< x (+ left width)) (> (+ x w) left)
             (< y (+ top height)) (> (+ y h) top))))))
(defun visible-panes (view)
  (let ((panes (loop for placement in (view-layout-placements view)
                     for pane = (pane-by-id (view-daemon view) (getf placement :pane))
                     when (and pane (getf placement :visible t) (pane-on-screen-p view pane)) collect pane))
        (drag (view-window-drag view)))
    ;; A window held by a moving drag is lifted above the rest.
    (if (and drag (window-drag-moved drag) (not (window-drag-split drag))
             (member (window-drag-pane drag) panes))
        (append (remove (window-drag-pane drag) panes) (list (window-drag-pane drag)))
        panes)))
(defun commit-layout (view owner placements camera)
  (declare (ignore owner))
  ;; The provider validator has checked IDs, dimensions, and containment before
  ;; any state reaches here. Camera changes projection, never logical PTY size.
  ;; An unchanged response to observed viewport facts must not signal PTYs.
  (unless (and (= (length placements) (length (view-layout-placements view)))
               (loop for new in placements for old in (view-layout-placements view)
                     always (loop for key in '(:pane :x :y :cols :rows :outer :visible)
                                  always (equal (getf new key (eq key :visible))
                                                (getf old key (eq key :visible))))))
    (setf (view-pty-reported-cw view) (view-reported-cw view)
          (view-pty-reported-ch view) (view-reported-ch view)))
  (let ((before (outer-places view t))
        (committed (loop for id being the hash-keys of (view-pane-states view)
                         collect (cons id (committed-rect view id)))))
  (maphash (lambda (id state)
             (declare (ignore id))
             (setf (pane-view-x state) 0 (pane-view-y state) 0
                   (pane-view-cols state) 0 (pane-view-rows state) 0
                   (pane-view-outer-x state) 0 (pane-view-outer-y state) 0
                   (pane-view-outer-cols state) 0 (pane-view-outer-rows state) 0))
           (view-pane-states view))
  (setf (view-layout-placements view) (copy-tree placements)
        (view-camera-x view) (first camera) (view-camera-y view) (second camera))
  (dolist (placement placements)
    (let* ((state (pane-state view (getf placement :pane)))
           (outer (getf placement :outer)))
      (setf (pane-view-x state) (- (getf placement :x) (first camera))
            (pane-view-y state) (- (getf placement :y) (second camera))
            (pane-view-cols state) (getf placement :cols)
            (pane-view-rows state) (getf placement :rows)
            (pane-view-outer-x state) (- (first outer) (first camera))
            (pane-view-outer-y state) (- (second outer) (second camera))
            (pane-view-outer-cols state) (third outer)
            (pane-view-outer-rows state) (fourth outer))))
  (start-window-motions view before committed))
  (reconcile-size (view-daemon view))
  (update-application-focus (view-daemon view))
  (incf (view-revision view)))
(defun clear-layout-provider (view)
  (commit-layout view nil nil '(0 0)))
(defun rectangle-placement (view rectangle)
  (destructuring-bind (id x y width height) rectangle
    (let ((insets (effective-insets width height (requested-pane-insets view x y width height))))
      (multiple-value-bind (cols rows) (content-size view width height x y)
        (list :pane id :x (+ x (fourth insets)) :y (+ y (first insets))
              :cols cols :rows rows :outer (list x y width height))))))
(defun layout (view)
  ;; A physical report is only an observation until a real layout operation.
  (setf (view-pty-reported-cw view) (view-reported-cw view)
        (view-pty-reported-ch view) (view-reported-ch view))
  (setf (view-transition view) nil (view-window-drag view) nil (view-window-hover view) nil)
  (close-popup view)
  (let* ((daemon (view-daemon view))
         (erase-display-history (option daemon :erase-display-history nil)))
    (dolist (pane (daemon-panes daemon))
      (setf (terminal-erase-display-history (pane-vt pane)) erase-display-history))
    ;; With no provider, the bare host can show the focused pane. It does not
    ;; implement a hidden tiled policy. Tests and built-ins install a provider.
    (unless (option daemon :layout-provider)
      (let ((pane (focused-pane view)))
        (commit-layout view nil
          (when pane
            (multiple-value-bind (viewport insets gaps width height) (workspace-geometry view)
              (declare (ignore insets gaps))
              (list (rectangle-placement view
                      (list (pane-id pane) (fourth viewport) (first viewport) width height)))))
          '(0 0))))
    (incf (view-revision view))))
(defun layout-all-views (daemon)
  (incf (daemon-revision daemon))
  (dolist (view (daemon-views daemon)) (layout view))
  (update-application-focus daemon))
(defun view-pane-size (view pane)
  (unless (member pane (view-panes view)) (return-from view-pane-size))
  (let ((state (pane-state view (pane-id pane)))
        (placement (find (pane-id pane) (view-layout-placements view) :key (lambda (p) (getf p :pane)))))
    ;; A provider may keep a logical size demand without displaying the pane.
    (when (and placement (not (pane-view-minimized state)) (not (pane-view-copy-lines state)))
      (list (getf placement :cols) (getf placement :rows)))))
(defun pane-sizing (pane)
  "Return canonical columns, rows and metric owner; NIL owner preserves size."
  (let* ((views (daemon-views (pane-daemon pane)))
         (demands (loop for view in views
                        for size = (and (view-live-p view) (view-pane-size view pane))
                        when size collect (cons view size)))
         (demands (or demands
                      (when (and (null (pane-io pane)) (null (pane-status pane)))
                        (loop for view in views for size = (view-pane-size view pane)
                              when (and size (eq view (daemon-home-view (view-daemon view))))
                                collect (cons view size))))))
    (if demands
        (values (reduce #'min demands :key #'second) (reduce #'min demands :key #'third)
                (first (reduce (lambda (a b) (if (< (view-id (first a)) (view-id (first b))) a b)) demands)))
        (values (terminal-cols (pane-vt pane)) (terminal-rows (pane-vt pane)) nil))))
(defun reconcile-size (daemon)
  (dolist (pane (daemon-panes daemon))
    (multiple-value-bind (cols rows owner) (pane-sizing pane)
      (when owner
        (let* ((vt (pane-vt pane)) (desired (pty-size-for owner cols rows)))
          (unless (equal (list cols rows (view-cw owner) (view-ch owner))
                         (list (terminal-cols vt) (terminal-rows vt) (terminal-cw vt) (terminal-ch vt)))
            (resize-terminal vt cols rows (view-cw owner) (view-ch owner)))
          (unless (equal desired (pane-pty-size pane))
            (when (pane-io pane)
              (checked (resize (wire-fd (pane-io pane)) cols rows (third desired) (fourth desired)) "PTY resize"))
            (setf (pane-pty-size pane) desired)))))))
(defun record-activation (view pane)
  (setf (pane-view-activation-order (pane-state view (pane-id pane)))
        (incf (view-activation-sequence view))))
(defun set-focus (view id &optional relayout)
  (let ((pane (pane-by-id (view-daemon view) id)))
    (unless (and pane (member pane (view-panes view))) (error "Pane is outside this view's workspace"))
    (let ((state (pane-state view id)))
      (setf (pane-view-unseen-output state) nil)
      (when (pane-view-minimized state) (setf (pane-view-minimized state) nil relayout t))
      (when (or relayout (not (eql id (view-focus-id view))))
        (setf (view-focus-id view) id)
        (record-activation view pane)
        (layout view)
        (update-application-focus (view-daemon view))))))
(defun utf8-width (byte)
  (cond ((<= 0 byte 127) 1)
        ((<= #xc2 byte #xdf) 2)
        ((<= #xe0 byte #xef) 3)
        ((<= #xf0 byte #xf4) 4)
        (t nil)))
(defun key-width (bytes)
  "First UTF-8 key width; NIL for a valid incomplete prefix, 1 for invalid input."
  (let* ((lead (and (plusp (length bytes)) (aref bytes 0)))
         (width (and lead (utf8-width lead))))
    (cond
      ((or (null width) (= width 1)) 1)
      ((loop for i from 1 below (min width (length bytes))
             for byte = (aref bytes i)
             thereis (or (not (<= #x80 byte #xbf))
                         (and (= i 1)
                              (or (and (= lead #xe0) (< byte #xa0))
                                  (and (= lead #xed) (> byte #x9f))
                                  (and (= lead #xf0) (< byte #x90))
                                  (and (= lead #xf4) (> byte #x8f)))))) 1)
      ((< (length bytes) width) nil)
      (t width))))
(defun utf8-codepoint (bytes)
  "Return one valid Unicode scalar, never a raw non-ASCII byte."
  (when (and (plusp (length bytes))
             (eql (key-width bytes) (length bytes))
             (eql (utf8-width (aref bytes 0)) (length bytes)))
    (char-code (char (bytes-text bytes) 0))))
(defun close-pane (pane)
  (when (pane-pid pane) (signal-group (pane-pid pane) 1))
  (when (pane-io pane) (close-wire (pane-io pane)) (setf (pane-io pane) nil)))
(defun input-key (view bytes &optional raw-input)
  ;; Client packets may end between UTF-8 bytes. Keep an incomplete modal key
  ;; until its continuation arrives; forwarding uses the original byte slice.
  (when (view-key-fragment view)
    (when (eql (view-mode view) (view-key-fragment-mode view))
      (setf bytes (concatenate '(vector (unsigned-byte 8)) (view-key-fragment view) bytes)))
    (setf (view-key-fragment view) nil (view-key-fragment-mode view) nil))
  ;; A framed read is consumed by its first complete semantic key.  Keep the
  ;; framing bit so later keys explicitly carry NIL read metadata.
  (when (command-pending-p view)
    (defer-input view 2 bytes) (return-from input-key))
  (when (and (null (view-mode view)) (> (length bytes) 1) (/= (aref bytes 0) 27))
    (let ((prefix (position (option (view-daemon view) :prefix 2) bytes)))
      (when (and prefix (plusp prefix) (not (view-prefix view)))
        (input-key view (subseq bytes 0 prefix))
        (return-from input-key (input-key view (subseq bytes prefix))))))
  ;; A client may coalesce ordinary key bytes.  Prefix handling still needs
  ;; to see the first byte as a complete key, then can forward the remainder
  ;; as one ordinary run.  Escape-led sequences are already framed by the
  ;; client and retain their existing CSI/Kitty handling below.
  (when (and (not raw-input) (view-mode view) (plusp (length bytes)) (/= (aref bytes 0) 27))
    (let ((width (key-width bytes)))
      (unless width
        (setf (view-key-fragment view) (copy-seq bytes)
              (view-key-fragment-mode view) (view-mode view))
        (return-from input-key))
      (when (> (length bytes) width)
        (let ((action (input-key view (subseq bytes 0 width)
                                (and (= width 1) (> (aref bytes 0) 127)))))
          (when (or (member action '(:detach :stop)) (command-pending-p view))
            (when (> (length bytes) width) (defer-input view 2 (subseq bytes width)))
            (return-from input-key action))
          (return-from input-key (input-key view (subseq bytes width)))))))
  (when (and (null (view-mode view)) (> (length bytes) 1) (/= (aref bytes 0) 27)
             (or (view-prefix view) (and (pane-view-copy-lines (pane-state view (view-focus-id view)))
                                             (not (pane-view-search-input (pane-state view (view-focus-id view)))))
                 (= (aref bytes 0) (option (view-daemon view) :prefix 2))))
    (let ((action (input-key view (subseq bytes 0 1))))
      (when (or (member action '(:detach :stop)) (command-pending-p view))
        (when (> (length bytes) 1) (defer-input view 2 (subseq bytes 1)))
        (return-from input-key action))
      (return-from input-key (input-key view (subseq bytes 1)))))
  (let* ((read-bytes (when (view-input-read-framed view)
                       (prog1 (view-input-read-bytes view)
                         (setf (view-input-read-bytes view) nil))))
         (text (map 'string #'code-char bytes)) (esc (code-char 27))
         (kitty (and (> (length text) 3) (char= (char text 0) esc) (char= (char text 1) #\[)
                     (char= (char text (1- (length text))) #\u)))
         (params (when kitty (parameters (subseq text 2 (1- (length text))))))
         (code (if kitty (first params)
                   (if (view-mode view) (and (not raw-input) (utf8-codepoint bytes))
                       (and (= (length bytes) 1) (aref bytes 0)))))
         (modifiers (if kitty (1- (max 1 (or (second params) 1))) 0))
         ;; Some hosts report the shifted codepoint as the primary field. When a
         ;; modifier rides along, fold an uppercase ASCII letter down to lowercase
         ;; and mark Shift so it matches the uppercase chord spec. Unmodified keys
         ;; stay untouched: bare capitals, Shift+Enter and Shift+Tab keep their codes.
         (shifted (and code (or (logbitp 2 modifiers) (logbitp 1 modifiers) (logbitp 3 modifiers))
                       (<= 65 code 90)))
         (code (if shifted (+ code 32) code))
         (modifiers (if shifted (logior modifiers 1) modifiers))
         ;; Control folds into the legacy control code for an ASCII letter base only,
         ;; so a plain Ctrl+letter keeps matching its legacy byte; on every other base
         ;; Ctrl stays a distinct bit. Escape's raw 0x1B byte and CSI 27u never fold to
         ;; Ctrl+[, so they remain the plain 27 key.
         (ctrl (and code (logbitp 2 modifiers)))
         (folded (and ctrl (or (<= 65 code 90) (<= 97 code 122))))
         (key (if folded (logand code 31) code))
         ;; Structural modifiers for the binding lookup: Ctrl survives only where it
         ;; did not fold, and the Shift bit rides only when Ctrl, Alt or Super is also
         ;; present, so Shift+Tab and Shift+Enter keep their plain named keys.
         (chord-mods (logior (if (and (logbitp 0 modifiers)
                                      (or (logbitp 1 modifiers) ctrl (logbitp 3 modifiers))) 1 0)
                             (if (logbitp 1 modifiers) 2 0)
                             (if (and ctrl (not folded)) 4 0)
                             (if (logbitp 3 modifiers) 8 0)))
         (chorded (binding-key bytes key chord-mods))
         (pane (focused-pane view)))
    (when (view-window-drag view)
      (when (eql (semantic-key bytes key) 27)
        (setf (view-window-drag view) nil (view-chrome-press view) :dismiss)
        (incf (view-revision view)))
      (return-from input-key))
    (when (popup-key view (semantic-key bytes key)) (return-from input-key))
    (when (or (pane-view-copy-pointer (pane-state view (pane-id pane))) (pane-view-copy-flash-until (pane-state view (pane-id pane))))
      (apply-actions view :pointer '((:copy-exit)) nil nil)
      (when (eql key 27) (return-from input-key)))
    ;; A copy fallback opts into the builtin search editor and copy bindings.
    ;; Other maps (notably locked maps) retain literal application forwarding.
    (when (and (eq (getf (find (view-mode view)
                                    (getf (daemon-registry (view-daemon view)) :keymaps)
                                    :key (lambda (m) (getf m :name))) :unbound) :copy)
               (pane-view-copy-lines (pane-state view (pane-id pane)))
               (not (key-binding view (view-mode view) chorded)))
      (if (pane-view-search-input (pane-state view (pane-id pane)))
          (copy-search-input view
                             (if (and kitty key (<= 0 key #x10ffff))
                                 (text-bytes (string (code-char key))) bytes))
          (dispatch-binding view :copy chorded))
      (return-from input-key))
    (when (view-mode view)
      (let* ((map (view-mode view)) (binding (key-binding view map chorded))
             (policy (find map (reverse (getf (daemon-registry (view-daemon view)) :keymaps))
                           :key (lambda (m) (getf m :name)))))
        (cond ((and binding (getf binding :command))
               (dispatch-binding view map chorded)
               (return-from input-key))
              ((dispatch-keymap-fallback view map (semantic-key bytes key) bytes read-bytes)
               (return-from input-key))
              ((eq (getf policy :unbound) :ignore) (return-from input-key)))))
    (when (pane-view-minimized (pane-state view (pane-id pane))) (return-from input-key))
    (cond
      ((and (null (view-mode view)) (view-prefix view))
       (setf (view-prefix view) nil)
       (if (eql key (option (view-daemon view) :prefix 2))
           (pane-input pane (octets-from-list (list key)))
           (dispatch-binding view :prefix chorded)))
      ((and (null (view-mode view)) (eql key (option (view-daemon view) :prefix 2))) (setf (view-prefix view) t) nil)
      ((and (null (view-mode view)) (pane-view-search-input (pane-state view (pane-id pane))))
       (copy-search-input view (if (and kitty key (<= 0 key #x10ffff) (not (<= #xd800 key #xdfff)))
                                     (text-bytes (string (code-char key))) bytes)))
      ((and (null (view-mode view)) (pane-view-copy-lines (pane-state view (pane-id pane)))) (dispatch-binding view :copy chorded))
      (t
       (cond
         ((and kitty (zerop (first (terminal-keyboards (pane-vt pane)))))
          (when (and key (<= 0 key #x10ffff) (not (<= #xd800 key #xdfff)))
            (pane-input pane (text-bytes (concatenate 'string (if (logbitp 1 modifiers) (string esc) "")
                                                     (string (code-char key)))))))
         ((and (gethash 1 (terminal-modes (pane-vt pane))) (= (length bytes) 3)
               (= (aref bytes 0) 27) (= (aref bytes 1) 91) (find (code-char (aref bytes 2)) "ABCDHF"))
          (pane-input pane (octets-from-list (list 27 79 (aref bytes 2)))))
         (t (pane-input pane bytes))) nil))))
(defun octets-from-list (items) (coerce items '(vector (unsigned-byte 8))))
(defun decoration-at (view x y)
  ;; Hit reserved chrome, or an opaque overlay painted over content, in the
  ;; same component/span order as paint.
  (unless (or (>= x (view-cols view)) (>= y (view-rows view)))
    (let ((hit nil) (owner nil) (content (decoration-content-p view (window-at view x y) x y 1)))
      (dolist (component (getf (daemon-registry (view-daemon view)) :components))
        (dolist (span (rest (assoc (getf component :id) (view-decorations view) :test #'equal)))
          (when (and (span-hit-p span x y) (or (not content) (getf span :overlay))
                     (or (not (getf span :pane))
                         (eql (getf span :pane) (let ((p (window-at view x y))) (and p (pane-id p))))))
            (setf hit span owner (getf component :id)))))
      (values hit owner))))
(defun decoration-action-at (view x y)
  (getf (decoration-at view x y) :action))
(defun input-mouse (view text)
  (let* ((args (parameters (subseq text 3 (1- (length text)))))
