         (button (first args)) (x (second args)) (y (third args))
         (up (char= (char text (1- (length text))) #\m))
         (cw (view-cw view)) (ch (view-ch view)))
    (unless (and (= (length args) 3) (<= 0 button 255) (plusp x) (plusp y)) (return-from input-mouse))
    (let ((col (floor (1- x) cw)) (row (floor (1- y) ch)))
      (update-window-hover view button col row up)
      (when (eq (view-chrome-press view) :dismiss)
        (when up (setf (view-chrome-press view) nil))
        (return-from input-mouse))
      (when (window-drag-mouse view button col row up) (return-from input-mouse))
      (when (popup-mouse view button col row up) (return-from input-mouse))
      (multiple-value-bind (span owner) (decoration-at view col row)
        (let ((pressed (view-chrome-press view)))
          (when pressed
            (when up
              (setf (view-chrome-press view) nil)
              (when (and (= button 0) (equal pressed (list owner span)))
                (activate-span view owner span)))
            (return-from input-mouse)))
        (when (and span (not (view-drag view)))
          (cond ((and (not up) (= button 0) (getf span :drag))
                 (begin-window-drag view owner span col row)
                 (return-from input-mouse))
                ((and (not up) (member button '(64 65)) (getf span :wheel-command))
                 (handler-case
                     (request-command view (getf span :wheel-command)
                                      (list :arguments (getf span :arguments)
                                            :direction (if (= button 64) -1 1)) nil)
                   (error (e) (note-error view e)))
                 (return-from input-mouse))
                ((and (not up) (= button 1) (getf span :middle-command))
                 (handler-case
                     (request-command view (getf span :middle-command)
                                      (list :arguments (getf span :arguments)) nil)
                   (error (e) (note-error view e)))
                 (return-from input-mouse))
                ((and (not up) (= button 2) (getf span :context-command))
                 (setf (view-chrome-press view) :dismiss)
                 (handler-case
                     (request-command view (getf span :context-command)
                                      (list :arguments (getf span :arguments) :x col :y row) nil)
                   (error (e) (note-error view e))))
                ((and (not up) (= button 0) (span-control-p span))
                 (setf (view-chrome-press view) (list owner (copy-tree span)))))
          (when (or (span-control-p span) (getf span :context-command)) (return-from input-mouse)))))
    (let* ((hit (let* ((col (floor (1- x) cw)) (row (floor (1- y) ch)) (p (window-at view col row)))
                        (when (decoration-content-p view p col row 1) p)))
           (pane (or (view-drag view) hit)))
      (when (and pane (not up) (zerop (logand button 96)) (< (logand button 3) 3))
        (set-focus view (pane-id pane)) (setf (view-drag view) pane))
      (when (and pane (pointer-copy-input view pane button x y up))
        (when up (setf (view-drag view) nil))
        (return-from input-mouse))
      (when pane
        (let* ((vt (pane-vt pane)) (modes (terminal-modes vt))
               (px (max 1 (- x (* (pane-view-x (pane-state view (pane-id pane))) cw))))
               (py (max 1 (- y (* (pane-view-y (pane-state view (pane-id pane))) ch)))))
          (when (or (gethash 1003 modes) (and (gethash 1002 modes) (or (view-drag view) (zerop (logand button 32))))
                    (and (gethash 1000 modes) (zerop (logand button 32))))
            ;; Images retain native pixels. Pixel mouse coordinates therefore
            ;; retain local host pixels too; only cell mouse uses host cells.
            (if (gethash 1016 modes)
                (setf px (min px (* (terminal-cols vt) (terminal-cw vt)))
                      py (min py (* (terminal-rows vt) (terminal-ch vt))))
                (setf px (min (terminal-cols vt) (ceiling px cw))
                      py (min (terminal-rows vt) (ceiling py ch))))
            (when (gethash 1006 modes)
              (pane-input pane (text-bytes (format nil "~C[<~D;~D;~D~C" (code-char 27) button px py (if up #\m #\M))))))))
      (when up (setf (view-drag view) nil)))))
(defun cell-runs (cells &optional selected-start selected-end (offset 0) (end (length cells))
                                (selection-sgr '(27 48 5 238)))
  "One styled-row formatter for both live output and frozen selection."
  (let ((runs nil) (start 0) (attr nil) (index 0) (chars (make-string-output-stream)))
    (loop for position from offset below end for cell = (aref cells position) for x from 0
          for text = (if (and (= position (1- end)) (plusp (length (first cell)))
                              (= 2 (ekko/vt:character-width (char (first cell) 0))))
                         " " (first cell))
          for style = (if (and selected-start selected-end
                               (< index selected-end) (> (+ index (length text)) selected-start))
                          (ekko/vt:update-rendition (second cell) selection-sgr)
                          (second cell)) do
      (unless (equal attr style)
        (when attr (push (list start (get-output-stream-string chars) attr) runs))
        (setf start x attr style))
      (write-string text chars)
      (incf index (length text)))
    (when attr (push (list start (get-output-stream-string chars) attr) runs))
    (nreverse runs)))
(defun pane-lines (pane)
  "PANE's rows as styled runs, reusing a row's runs while its cells are unchanged."
  (let* ((vt (pane-vt pane)) (cells (terminal-cells vt)) (cols (terminal-cols vt)) (rows (terminal-rows vt))
         (cache (pane-row-cache pane)))
    (unless (and cache (= (length cache) rows))
      (setf cache (make-array rows :initial-element nil) (pane-row-cache pane) cache))
    (loop for y below rows for row = (svref cells y) for entry = (svref cache y)
          collect (if (and entry (= (length (car entry)) cols)
                           (loop for x below cols always (eq (svref (car entry) x) (svref row x))))
                      (cdr entry)
                      (cdr (setf (svref cache y) (cons (copy-seq row) (cell-runs row nil nil 0 cols))))))))
(defvar *content-rects* nil "Pane content rectangles memoized for one scene build.")
(defun pane-content-rect (view pane)
  "PANE's visible application cells within the viewport, as half-open (x0 y0 x1 y1)."
  (let ((memo (and *content-rects* (gethash pane *content-rects*))))
    (or memo
        (let ((rect (compute-content-rect view pane)))
          (when *content-rects* (setf (gethash pane *content-rects*) rect))
          rect))))
(defun compute-content-rect (view pane)
  (let ((state (pane-state view (pane-id pane))))
    (multiple-value-bind (viewport insets gaps viewport-width viewport-height) (workspace-geometry view)
      (declare (ignore insets gaps))
      (multiple-value-bind (cols rows x y) (shown-content view pane)
        (list (max x (fourth viewport)) (max y (first viewport))
              (min (+ x cols) (+ (fourth viewport) viewport-width))
              (min (+ y rows) (+ (first viewport) viewport-height)))))))
(defun rect-hit-p (rect x y width)
  (destructuring-bind (x0 y0 x1 y1) rect
    (and (< x0 (+ x width)) (< x x1) (<= y0 y) (< y y1))))
(defun decoration-content-p (view pane x y width)
  (when pane (rect-hit-p (pane-content-rect view pane) x y width)))
(defun clip-decoration (view panes span &optional occluders)
  (let* ((x (getf span :x)) (y (getf span :y)) (text (getf span :text))
         (sgr (copy-list (getf span :sgr))) (alpha (getf span :alpha)) (cols (view-cols view))
         (rects (append (mapcar (lambda (pane) (pane-content-rect view pane)) panes)
                        (loop for pane in occluders
                              collect (destructuring-bind (ox oy w h) (shown-rect view (pane-id pane))
                                        (list ox oy (+ ox w) (+ oy h)))))))
    (flet ((free-p (x width)
             (and (<= 0 x) (<= (+ x width) cols) (<= 0 y) (< y (view-rows view))
                  (notany (lambda (rect) (rect-hit-p rect x y width)) rects))))
      (if (and (plusp (length text)) (plusp (ekko/vt:character-width (char text 0)))
               (free-p x (ekko/text:display-width text)))
          (list (list* x y (copy-seq text) sgr (when alpha (list alpha))))
          (let ((cursor x) (run-x nil) (run-width 0) (run nil) (out nil))
            (flet ((flush ()
                     (when run
                       (push (list* run-x y (coerce (nreverse run) 'string) (copy-list sgr) (when alpha (list alpha))) out)
                       (setf run nil run-x nil run-width 0))))
              (loop for char across text
                    for width = (ekko/vt:character-width char) do
                      ;; Combining marks do not claim a new cell. Keep them with an
                      ;; accepted base glyph, and drop leading marks so a clipped
                      ;; span cannot affect an unrelated cell.
                      (cond
                        ((zerop width) (when run (push char run)))
                        ((free-p cursor width)
                         (unless (and run-x (= cursor (+ run-x run-width))) (flush))
                         (unless run-x (setf run-x cursor))
                         (push char run) (incf run-width width))
                        (t (flush)))
                      (incf cursor width))
              (flush))
            (nreverse out))))))
(defun span-pieces (view owner span dx alpha)
  "SPAN's text as clip-ready pieces in its shown style, a :gradient spread
across its cells, carrying ALPHA for the ground to fade against."
  (destructuring-bind (sgr gradient) (if (view-tweens view)
                                         (shown-style view (span-key owner span) (span-style span))
                                         (span-style span))
    (let ((x (+ (getf span :x) dx)) (text (getf span :text)))
      (if (and gradient (> (length text) 1))
          (loop with last = (1- (length text)) for char across text for index from 0
                collect (list :x x :text (string char) :sgr (blend-sgr sgr gradient (/ index last)) :alpha alpha)
                do (incf x (ekko/vt:character-width char)))
          (list (list :x x :text text :sgr sgr :alpha alpha))))))
(defun paint-span (view owner span dx dy alpha pane panes overlay)
  (loop for row from (+ (getf span :y) dy) below (+ (getf span :y) dy (getf span :rows 1))
        append (loop for piece in (span-pieces view owner span dx alpha)
                     append (clip-decoration view (unless overlay (if pane (list pane) panes))
                              (list* :y row piece)
                              (unless overlay (and pane (rest (member pane panes))))))))
(defun redecorated-since-p (view owner time)
  (> (or (rest (assoc owner (view-redecorated view) :test #'equal)) 0) time))
(defun scene-decorations (view &optional overlay)
  (let* ((*content-rects* (make-hash-table :test 'eq))
         (panes (visible-panes view))
         (declared (mapcar (lambda (c) (getf c :id)) (getf (daemon-registry (view-daemon view)) :components)))
         (owners (append declared (loop for entry in (view-decorations view)
                                       unless (member (first entry) declared :test #'equal) collect (first entry)))))
    (append
     ;; A moving window's chrome as it was, wherever its owners have redrawn.
     (loop for (id nil started nil ref . spans) in (view-slides view)
           for pane = (pane-by-id (view-daemon view) id)
           when (member pane panes)
             append (loop with rect = (shown-rect view id)
                          for (owner . span) in spans
                          for fitted = (and (eq (getf span :overlay) overlay) (redecorated-since-p view owner started)
                                            (fit-span span ref rect))
                          when fitted append (paint-span view owner fitted 0 0 nil pane panes overlay)))
     (loop for owner in owners
           append (loop for span in (rest (assoc owner (view-decorations view) :test #'equal))
                        for pane = (pane-by-id (view-daemon view) (getf span :pane))
                        when (and (eq (getf span :overlay) overlay) (or (not (getf span :pane)) (member pane panes)))
                          append (let ((fitted (window-span view owner span)))
                                   (when fitted
                                     (multiple-value-bind (dx dy alpha) (motion-offset view span)
                                       (paint-span view owner fitted dx dy alpha pane panes overlay))))))
     (loop for (owner span dx dy alpha) in (ghost-spans view)
           when (eq (getf span :overlay) overlay)
             append (paint-span view owner span dx dy alpha nil panes overlay)))))
(defun shown-content (view pane)
  "PANE's content where it shows, as (values cols rows x y): a shrinking
window clips it, a growing one shows the ground around it."
  (let ((state (pane-state view (pane-id pane))))
    (multiple-value-bind (cols rows) (pane-display-size view pane)
      (multiple-value-bind (dx dy dcols drows) (pane-shift view (pane-id pane))
        (values (max 0 (min cols (+ cols dcols))) (max 0 (min rows (+ rows drows)))
                (+ (pane-view-x state) dx) (+ (pane-view-y state) dy))))))
(defun pane-display-size (view pane)
  (let ((state (pane-state view (pane-id pane))) (vt (pane-vt pane)))
    (if (pane-view-copy-lines state)
        (values (pane-view-cols state) (pane-view-rows state))
        (values (min (pane-view-cols state) (terminal-cols vt))
                (min (pane-view-rows state) (terminal-rows vt))))))
(defun scene-data (view)
  (list +wire-version+ (view-cols view) (view-rows view) (view-cw view) (view-ch view)
        (or (view-focus-id view) 0)
        (loop for pane in (visible-panes view) for vt = (pane-vt pane)
              for state = (pane-state view (pane-id pane)) collect
          (multiple-value-bind (cols rows x y) (shown-content view pane)
            (list (pane-id pane) x y cols rows
                  (if (pane-view-copy-lines state)
                      (format nil "~A [copy ~D/~D]" (pane-label pane)
                              (1+ (pane-view-copy-cursor state)) (length (pane-view-copy-lines state)))
                      (pane-label pane))
                  (pane-status pane) (min (1- cols) (terminal-x vt)) (terminal-y vt)
                  (and (not (pane-view-copy-lines state)) (terminal-visible vt))
                  (ground-lines view pane (if (pane-view-copy-lines state) (copy-display-lines view pane) (pane-lines pane)))
                  (loop for image being the hash-values of (store-images (pane-graphics pane))
                        when (and (not (pane-view-copy-lines state)) (image-visible image)
                                  (eq (image-screen image) (terminal-screen vt))) collect
                        (list (image-id image) (image-generation image) (image-x image) (image-y image)
                              (image-cols image) (image-rows image)))
                  (shown-rect view (pane-id pane)))))
        (multiple-value-bind (viewport pane gaps width height) (workspace-geometry view)
          (declare (ignore pane width height))
          (list :view-id (view-id view) :status (status-text view)
                :style (option (view-daemon view) :status-style '(0 30 47))
                :viewport-insets viewport :split-gaps gaps
                :decorations (ground-fragments view (append (ground-fill view) (scene-decorations view)))
                :overlays (ground-fragments view
                            (append (scene-decorations view t) (transition-overlays view)
                                    (window-hover-overlays view) (window-drag-overlays view) (popup-overlays view)))
                :exit-text (option (view-daemon view) :viewer-exit-text nil)))))
(defun checked-startup-viewport (viewport)
  (when viewport
    (unless (and (listp viewport) (= (length viewport) 4)
                 (every #'integerp viewport))
      (error "Invalid startup viewport ~S" viewport))
    (destructuring-bind (cols rows cw ch) viewport
      (unless (and (<= 5 cols 500) (<= 4 rows 300)
                   (<= 1 cw 128) (<= 1 ch 256))
        (error "Invalid startup viewport ~S" viewport))
      (list cols rows cw ch))))
(defun workspace-stamp (view)
  (list (daemon-revision (view-daemon view)) (view-revision view)
        (loop for pane in (view-panes view) collect
          (list (pane-id pane) (pane-label pane) (terminal-revision (pane-vt pane))
                (store-revision (pane-graphics pane)) (pane-status pane)))))
(defun scene-mid-frame-p (view)
  "True while a pane the viewer can see is inside an application frame.
Scenes are whole-screen snapshots, so the hold is view-wide and bounded
by the deadline PANE-EVENT arms.  Copy mode presents frozen rows, not the
application's screen, so it is not held."
  (some (lambda (pane)
          (and (not (pane-view-copy-lines (pane-state view (pane-id pane))))
               (gethash 2026 (terminal-modes (pane-vt pane)))
               (pane-mid-frame-p pane)))
        (visible-panes view)))
(defun publish-scene (view wire)
  (when (scene-mid-frame-p view) (return-from publish-scene))
  (let ((stamp (workspace-stamp view)))
    (when (and (null (wire-queue wire)) (null (wire-awaiting-scene wire))
               (not (equal stamp (wire-revision wire))))
      (let ((live (make-hash-table :test 'equal)))
        (dolist (pane (visible-panes view))
          (maphash (lambda (id image)
                     (when (and (not (pane-view-copy-lines (pane-state view (pane-id pane)))) (image-visible image) (eq (image-screen image) (terminal-screen (pane-vt pane))))
                       (setf (gethash (list (pane-id pane) id) live) t)))
                   (store-images (pane-graphics pane))))
        (maphash (lambda (key value) (declare (ignore value))
                   (unless (gethash key live) (remhash key (wire-known wire)))) (wire-known wire)))
      (dolist (pane (visible-panes view))
        (maphash (lambda (id image)
                   (let ((key (list (pane-id pane) id)))
                     (when (and (not (pane-view-copy-lines (pane-state view (pane-id pane)))) (image-visible image) (eq (image-screen image) (terminal-screen (pane-vt pane)))
                                (not (eql (gethash key (wire-known wire)) (image-generation image))))
                       (let ((data (image-data image))
                             (metadata (integers (list (pane-id pane) id (image-generation image)
                                                       (image-width image) (image-height image) (image-format image)))))
                         (send-packet wire (if (local-asset-p data) 13 11)
                           (concatenate '(vector (unsigned-byte 8)) metadata
                             (if (local-asset-p data) (text-bytes (local-asset-path data)) data))))
                       (setf (gethash key (wire-known wire)) (image-generation image)))))
                 (store-images (pane-graphics pane))))
      (dolist (pane (visible-panes view))
        (maphash (lambda (id image) (declare (ignore id))
                   (when (and (not (pane-view-copy-lines (pane-state view (pane-id pane)))) (image-visible image)
                              (eq (image-screen image) (terminal-screen (pane-vt pane)))
                              (local-asset-p (image-data image)))
                     (push (retain-asset (image-data image)) (wire-leases wire))))
                 (store-images (pane-graphics pane))))
      (let ((scene (scene-data view)))
        (setf (first scene) (wire-version wire))
        (send-packet wire 12 (encode-scene scene)))
      (setf (wire-awaiting-scene wire) (now))
      (setf (wire-revision wire) stamp))))
(defun view-status-data (view)
  (list :object :id (view-id view) :epoch (view-epoch view)
        :attached (if (view-wire view) t :false) :focus (view-focus-id view)
        :mode (view-mode view) :cols (view-cols view) :rows (view-rows view)
        :cw (view-cw view) :ch (view-ch view)
        :panes (loop for pane in (view-panes view) for state = (pane-state view (pane-id pane))
                     collect (list :object :id (pane-id pane)
                                   :x (pane-view-x state) :y (pane-view-y state)
                                   :cols (pane-view-cols state) :rows (pane-view-rows state)
                                   :minimized (if (pane-view-minimized state) t :false)
                                   :copy_mode (if (pane-view-copy-lines state) t :false)))))
(defun workspace-status-data (daemon &optional view)
  (list :object :wire_version +wire-version+ :session *instance* :name *instance*
        :daemon_pid (sb-posix:getpid)
        :extension_pid (when (daemon-worker daemon)
                         (sb-ext:process-pid (extension-worker-process (daemon-worker daemon))))
        :allocated_bytes (sb-ext:get-bytes-consed)
        :gc_seconds (coerce (/ sb-ext:*gc-run-time* internal-time-units-per-second) 'double-float)
        :snapshot_bytes ekko/platform:*asset-bytes*
        :attached (if (some #'view-wire (daemon-views daemon)) t :false)
        :focus (when view (view-focus-id view))
        :views (mapcar #'view-status-data (daemon-views daemon))
        :panes (loop for p in (daemon-panes daemon) for vt = (pane-vt p) for g = (pane-graphics p)
                     for state = (when view (pane-state view (pane-id p))) collect
                 (list :object :id (pane-id p) :pid (pane-pid p) :label (pane-label p) :exit_code (pane-status p)
                       :cols (terminal-cols vt) :rows (terminal-rows vt)
                       :x (when state (pane-view-x state)) :y (when state (pane-view-y state))
                       :history_rows (if (and (terminal-history vt) (eq (terminal-screen vt) :main))
                                         (history-count (terminal-history vt)) 0)
                       :copy_mode (if (and state (pane-view-copy-lines state)) t :false)
                       :images (hash-table-count (store-images g)) :frames (store-frames g)
                       :graphics_errors (store-errors g) :image_bytes (store-bytes g)
                       :pty_output_bytes (pane-output-bytes p)
                       :local_images (loop for image being the hash-values of (store-images g)
                                           count (local-asset-p (image-data image)))))))
(defun status-json (daemon &optional view)
  (json-value (workspace-status-data daemon view)))

(defun server-packet (view wire packet)
  "Handle a routed view operation. The daemon owns connection negotiation."
  (let ((daemon (view-daemon view)) (kind (aref packet 0)) (data (subseq packet 1)))
    (when (and (member kind '(2 4 5 6 16)) (command-pending-p view))
      (defer-input view kind data) (return-from server-packet))
    (case kind
      (15
       (unless (and (= (length data) 8) (eq (view-wire view) wire))
         (error "Unexpected reported cell size"))
       (let ((cw (u32 data 0)) (ch (u32 data 4)))
         (unless (and (<= 1 cw 128) (<= 1 ch 256)) (error "Invalid reported cell size"))
         (setf (view-reported-cw view) cw (view-reported-ch view) ch)
         (incf (view-revision view))))
      (2 (input-key view data))
      (4 (setf (view-input-read-bytes view) nil) (input-mouse view (bytes-text data)))
      (16
       (unless (<= 1 (length data) 65536) (error "Invalid input read frame"))
       (when (> (+ (length (view-input-read-bytes view)) (length data)) 65536)
         (error "Input read frame exceeds 64 KiB"))
       (setf (view-input-read-framed view) t
             (view-input-read-bytes view)
             (concatenate '(vector (unsigned-byte 8))
                          (or (view-input-read-bytes view) (octets 0)) data)))
      (5
       (when (view-popup view) (return-from server-packet))
       (setf (view-input-read-bytes view) nil)
       (let* ((begin (equalp data (text-bytes (format nil "~C[200~~" #\Esc))))
              (end (equalp data (text-bytes (format nil "~C[201~~" #\Esc))))
              (pane (or (view-paste-target view) (focused-pane view)))
              (state (pane-state view (pane-id pane)))
              (fallback (or (view-paste-fallback view)
                            (and (view-paste-target view) :forward) (paste-policy view))))
         (when (and begin (or (pane-view-copy-pointer state) (pane-view-copy-flash-until state)))
           (apply-actions view :pointer '((:copy-exit)) nil nil))
         (cond
           ((and begin (or (stringp fallback) (eq fallback :ignore)))
            (setf (view-paste-fallback view) fallback (view-paste-buffer view) (octets 0)
                  (view-paste-overflow view) nil))
           ((view-paste-fallback view)
            (unless (or begin end (eq (view-paste-fallback view) :ignore) (view-paste-overflow view))
              (if (<= (+ (length (view-paste-buffer view)) (length data)) 4096)
                  (setf (view-paste-buffer view)
                        (concatenate '(vector (unsigned-byte 8)) (view-paste-buffer view) data))
                  (progn (setf (view-paste-overflow view) t)
                         (note-error view "Fallback paste exceeds 4096 bytes"))))
            (when end (finish-paste-fallback view)))
           ((stringp fallback)
            (if (<= (length data) 4096)
                (request-command view fallback (list :key nil :bytes (coerce data 'list) :paste t) nil)
                (note-error view "Fallback paste exceeds 4096 bytes")))
           ((or (eq fallback :ignore) (pane-view-minimized state)))
           (t
            (when begin (setf (view-paste-target view) pane))
            (when (or (not (or begin end)) (gethash 2004 (terminal-modes (pane-vt pane))))
              (pane-input pane data))
            (when end (setf (view-paste-target view) nil))))))
      (6
       (unless (or (equalp data (text-bytes (format nil "~C[I" #\Esc)))
                   (equalp data (text-bytes (format nil "~C[O" #\Esc))))
         (error "Invalid host focus report"))
       (setf (view-input-read-bytes view) nil
             (view-host-focused view) (equalp data (text-bytes (format nil "~C[I" #\Esc))))
       (update-application-focus daemon))
      (7 (let ((parts (split-arguments data)))
           (request-command view (first parts) (list :arguments (rest parts)) wire)))
      (3 (unless (equalp data (text-bytes "inspect")) (error "Unknown view control command"))
         (send-packet wire 20 (text-bytes (inspect-json view))))
      (otherwise (error "Unknown IPC message")))))
;; The ground is Ekko's default background: the :ground option shows wherever
;; neither an application nor a decoration chose a background, so the desktop
;; runs through every window. Bands are sampled at each cell's screen row.
(defun ground-at (view row)
  (let ((ground (option (view-daemon view) :ground)))
    (if (or (null ground) (integerp ground) (every #'integerp ground))
        ground
        (let* ((height (max 1 (- (view-rows view) (min 1 (third (workspace-geometry view))))))
               (total (reduce #'+ ground :key #'first)) (top 0))
          (loop for (band . more) on ground
                do (incf top (if more (round (* height (first band)) total) height))
                   (when (or (< row top) (null more)) (return (second band))))))))
(defun sgr-background-p (codes)
  (loop while codes for code = (pop codes)
        do (cond ((member code '(38 58)) (pop codes) (pop codes))
                 ((eql code 48) (return t))
                 ((or (<= 40 code 47) (<= 100 code 107)) (return t)))))
(defun grounded (sgr colour)
  "SGR with COLOUR as its background, unless it already has one."
  (if (or (null colour) (sgr-background-p sgr))
      sgr
      (append sgr (if (integerp colour) (list 48 5 colour) (list* 48 2 colour)))))
(defun window-rects (view)
  "Visible windows' outer rectangles as half-open (x0 y0 x1 y1)."
  (loop for (id x y cols rows) in (outer-places view)
        collect (list x y (+ x cols) (+ y rows))))
(defun ground-fragments (view fragments)
  "Ground every fragment without a background: dimmed like the window's glass
inside a window, full outside. Rows' colours are computed once per frame."
  (if (option (view-daemon view) :ground)
      (let ((rects (window-rects view)) (dim (option (view-daemon view) :ground-dim 0))
            (rows (make-hash-table)))
        (loop for (x y text sgr alpha) in fragments
              for (full . glass) = (or (gethash y rows)
                                       (setf (gethash y rows)
                                             (let ((colour (ground-at view y))) (cons colour (dimmed colour dim)))))
              for colour = (if (some (lambda (r) (rect-hit-p r x y 1)) rects) glass full)
              ;; A fading fragment is seen through: it blends toward what it covers.
              collect (list x y text (grounded (if alpha (faded-sgr sgr alpha colour) sgr) colour))))
      ;; Without a ground there is nothing to blend toward; a fading fragment
      ;; shows while it is mostly opaque.
      (loop for (x y text sgr alpha) in fragments
            when (or (null alpha) (>= alpha 1/2)) collect (list x y text sgr))))
(defun dimmed (colour percent)
  "COLOUR darkened by PERCENT; palette indices have no known RGB and stay as they are."
  (if (or (integerp colour) (zerop percent))
      colour
      (mapcar (lambda (c) (round (* c (- 100 percent)) 100)) colour)))
(defun ground-lines (view pane lines)
  "Pane rows on the ground, darkened by :ground-dim percent so text stays legible."
  (if (option (view-daemon view) :ground)
      ;; Cells share a few style objects, so each row colour caches its grounded styles.
      (loop with dim = (option (view-daemon view) :ground-dim 0) with styles = (make-hash-table :test 'equal)
            for runs in lines for row from (+ (pane-view-y (pane-state view (pane-id pane)))
                                              (nth-value 1 (pane-shift view (pane-id pane))))
            for colour = (dimmed (ground-at view row) dim)
            for cache = (or (gethash colour styles) (setf (gethash colour styles) (make-hash-table :test 'eq)))
            collect (loop for (start text attr) in runs
                          collect (list start text (or (gethash attr cache)
                                                       (setf (gethash attr cache) (grounded attr colour))))))
      lines))
(defun ground-fill (view)
  "Blank ground runs under every decoration: each row minus application content,
split at window edges so window chrome takes the dimmed ground and the desktop
the full one."
  (when (option (view-daemon view) :ground)
    (let ((rects (mapcar (lambda (pane) (compute-content-rect view pane)) (visible-panes view)))
          (edges (loop for rect in (window-rects view) collect (first rect) collect (third rect)))
          (cols (view-cols view)))
      (flet ((blank (x0 x1 row)
               ;; Cut at window edges so each piece is wholly inside or outside a window.
               (loop with cuts = (sort (remove-duplicates (remove-if-not (lambda (e) (< x0 e x1)) edges)) #'<)
                     for start = x0 then end for end in (append cuts (list x1))
                     collect (list start row (make-string (- end start) :initial-element #\Space) (list 0)))))
        (loop for row below (view-rows view)
              append (let ((x 0) (out nil))
                       (dolist (rect (sort (remove-if-not (lambda (r) (and (<= (second r) row) (< row (fourth r))
                                                                           (< (first r) (third r))))
                                                          rects)
                                           #'< :key #'first))
                         (when (< x (first rect)) (push (blank x (first rect) row) out))
                         (setf x (max x (third rect))))
                       (when (< x cols) (push (blank x cols row) out))
                       (reduce #'append (nreverse out))))))))
#+end_src

** src/commands.lisp
Command dispatch and per-daemon option lookup.

#+begin_src lisp :tangle src/commands.lisp :mkdirp yes
(in-package #:ekko/runtime)

(defun option (daemon key &optional default)
  (getf (getf (daemon-registry daemon) :options) key default))
(defun layout-pane-ids (tree)
  "Validate a public layout tree and return its pane leaves."
  (let ((nodes 0))
    (labels ((walk (node)
               ;; Sixteen leaves need at most 31 nodes. Bound traversal before
               ;; descending so an invalid deep tree cannot exhaust the stack.
               (when (> (incf nodes) 255) (error "At most 128 layout leaves are supported"))
               (cond
                 ((and (integerp node) (plusp node)) (list node))
                 ((and (listp node) (= (length node) 4)
                       (member (first node) '(:columns :rows))
                       (typep (second node) '(integer 1 99)))
                  (append (walk (third node)) (walk (fourth node))))
                 (t (error "Invalid layout tree: ~S" tree)))))
      (walk tree))))
(defun validate-layout-tree (daemon tree)
  (let ((ids (layout-pane-ids tree))
        (existing (mapcar #'pane-id (daemon-panes daemon))))
    (unless (and (<= 1 (length ids) (option daemon :pane-budget 16))
                 (= (length ids) (length (remove-duplicates ids)))
                 (equal (sort (copy-list ids) #'<)
                        (sort (copy-list existing) #'<)))
      (error "Layout leaves must name each existing pane exactly once"))
    t))
(defun validate-component-state (value)
  (let ((active (make-hash-table :test #'eq)) (nodes 0) (bytes 0))
    (labels ((walk (x depth)
               (when (> depth 16) (error "Component state is too deep"))
               (when (> (incf nodes) 1024) (error "Component state has too many nodes"))
               (cond
                 ((or (null x) (eq x t) (keywordp x) (integerp x)) nil)
                 ((stringp x) (incf bytes (length (text-bytes x))))
                 ((consp x)
                  (let ((marked nil) (tail x))
                    (loop while (consp tail) do
                      (when (gethash tail active) (error "Cyclic component state"))
                      (setf (gethash tail active) t)
                      (push tail marked)
                      (walk (first tail) (1+ depth))
                      (setf tail (rest tail)))
                    (unless (null tail) (error "Component state needs proper lists"))
                    (dolist (cell marked) (remhash cell active))))
                 (t (error "Invalid component state value")))))
      (walk value 0)
      (when (> bytes 16384) (error "Component state exceeds 16 KiB")))
    t))
(defun component-state-bytes (entries)
  (loop for (owner . value) in entries
        sum (length (text-bytes (prin1-to-string (list owner value))))))
(defun copy-component-state (value)
  (if (consp value)
      (cons (copy-component-state (first value))
            (copy-component-state (rest value)))
      (if (stringp value) (copy-seq value) value)))
(defparameter *store-persist* t
  "NIL while staging a candidate so a rejected reload cannot write durable state.")
(defun store-namespace (owner)
  (unless owner (error "Store actions need a component owner"))
  (ekko/store:store-namespace owner))
(defun store-entries (daemon namespace)
  (rest (assoc namespace (daemon-store-entries daemon) :test #'equal)))
(defun store-set (daemon owner key value)
  "Return namespace and entries for OWNER with KEY set to VALUE, or removed."
  (let* ((namespace (store-namespace owner))
         (entries (remove key (store-entries daemon namespace) :key #'car :test #'equal)))
    (when value (push (cons key (copy-component-state value)) entries))
    (values namespace entries)))
(defun context-data (view &optional (registry (daemon-registry (view-daemon view))))
  "Return detached view state with static options read from REGISTRY."
  (let ((daemon (view-daemon view)) (options (getf registry :options)))
    (labels ((metadata (pane)
               (list :id (pane-id pane) :session *instance*
                     :label (pane-label pane) :name (pane-name pane) :argv (pane-argv pane)
                     :launch-kind (pane-launch-kind pane)
                     :creation-position (pane-creation-position pane)
                     :terminal-title (terminal-title (pane-vt pane))
                     :pid (pane-pid pane) :exit (pane-status pane))))
      (multiple-value-bind (insets pane-insets gaps width height) (workspace-geometry view registry)
        (declare (ignore width height))
        (let ((visible (visible-panes view))
              (tiled (workspace-rectangles view nil (tiled-tree view) registry)))
          (list :session *instance*
                :view (list :id (view-id view) :epoch (view-epoch view))
                :all-panes (mapcar #'metadata (daemon-panes daemon))
                :workspace (list :camera (list (view-camera-x view) (view-camera-y view)))
                :mode (view-mode view) :time (get-universal-time)
                :focus (view-focus-id view) :zoom (view-zoom view)
                :viewport (list :cols (view-cols view) :rows (view-rows view)
                                :cell-width (view-cw view) :cell-height (view-ch view)
                                :reported-cell-width (view-reported-cw view)
                                :reported-cell-height (view-reported-ch view)
                                :insets insets :gaps gaps)
                :geometry (list :pane-insets pane-insets
                                :boundary-insets (geometry-value view :boundary-insets nil registry)
                                :viewport-insets insets :split-gaps gaps)
                :chrome-status (list :text (status-text view registry)
                                     :style (getf options :status-style '(0 30 47)))
                :pane-notes (pane-note-data view registry)
                :notifications (mapcar #'copy-list (remove-if-not (lambda (n) (pane-by-id daemon (getf n :pane)))
                                                             (daemon-notifications daemon)))
                :component-state (view-component-state view)
                :store (daemon-store-entries daemon) :layout (daemon-tree daemon)
                :panes (loop for pane in (view-panes view)
                             for vt = (pane-vt pane)
                             for state = (pane-state view (pane-id pane) nil)
                             collect
                         (append (metadata pane)
                           (list :minimized (pane-view-minimized state)
                                 :floating (pane-floating pane)
                                 :activity (pane-view-unseen-output state)
                                 :activation-order (pane-view-activation-order state)
                                 :layout-rect (rest (assoc (pane-id pane) tiled))
                                 :display-label
                                 (if (pane-view-copy-lines state)
                                     (format nil "~A [copy ~D/~D]" (pane-label pane)
                                             (1+ (pane-view-copy-cursor state))
                                             (length (pane-view-copy-lines state)))
                                     (pane-label pane))
                                 :cols (terminal-cols vt) :rows (terminal-rows vt)
                                 :view-cols (pane-view-cols state) :view-rows (pane-view-rows state)
                                 :pty-size (pane-pty-size pane)
                                 :x (pane-view-x state) :y (pane-view-y state)
                                 :outer-rect (list (pane-view-outer-x state) (pane-view-outer-y state)
                                                   (pane-view-outer-cols state) (pane-view-outer-rows state))
                                 :visible (not (null (find pane visible)))
                                 :history-rows (if (and (terminal-history vt)
                                                        (eq (terminal-screen vt) :main))
                                                   (history-count (terminal-history vt)) 0))))))))))

(defun declared-context (context reads)
  "Detach CONTEXT to the read keys a callback is allowed to consume."
  (loop for key in reads append (list key (ekko/extensions:copy-data (getf context key)))))
(defun command-reads (registry name)
  (let* ((entry (find name (getf registry :commands)
                      :key (lambda (c) (getf c :name)) :test #'equal))
         (component (and entry (find (getf entry :owner) (getf registry :components)
                                     :key (lambda (c) (getf c :id)) :test #'equal))))
    (and component (getf component :reads))))
(defun layout-context (view)
  "Do not feed committed pane geometry back into its layout provider."
  (let ((context (context-data view)))
    (setf (getf context :panes)
          (loop for pane in (getf context :panes) collect
            (loop for (key value) on pane by #'cddr
                  unless (member key '(:x :y :outer-rect :layout-rect :visible
                                       :cols :rows :pty-size :view-cols :view-rows))
                    append (list key value))))
    context))
(defun validate-registry (registry)
  (unless (and (= (getf registry :api-version 0) 1)
               (<= (length (getf registry :components)) 64)
               (<= (length (getf registry :commands)) 256)
               (<= (length (getf registry :bindings)) 256)
               (<= (length (getf registry :keymaps)) 256))
    (error "Invalid extension registry"))
  (flet ((known (name) (find name (getf registry :keymaps) :key (lambda (m) (getf m :name)))))
    (when (and (getf (getf registry :options) :initial-keymap)
               (not (known (getf (getf registry :options) :initial-keymap))))
      (error "Initial keymap is not registered"))
    (dolist (binding (getf registry :bindings))
      (unless (or (member (getf binding :map) '(:prefix :copy)) (known (getf binding :map)))
        (error "Binding references unknown keymap"))))
  (dolist (binding (getf registry :bindings))
    (unless (or (null (getf binding :command))
                (find (getf binding :command) (getf registry :commands) :key (lambda (c) (getf c :name)) :test #'equal))
      (error "Binding references unknown command: ~A" (getf binding :command))))
  (dolist (keymap (getf registry :keymaps))
    (let ((fallback (getf keymap :unbound)))
      (when (and (stringp fallback)
                 (not (find fallback (getf registry :commands)
                            :key (lambda (c) (getf c :name)) :test #'equal)))
        (error "Keymap fallback references unknown command: ~A" fallback))))
  (validate-layout-registry registry)
  t)
(defun retained-owner-entries (entries registry)
  (remove-if-not (lambda (entry)
                   (find (first entry) (getf registry :components)
                         :key (lambda (component) (getf component :id)) :test #'equal)) entries))
(defun install-registry (daemon registry &optional preserve-quarantine)
  (validate-registry registry)
  (setf (daemon-registry daemon) registry (daemon-config-error daemon) nil)
  (incf (daemon-config-generation daemon))
  (let ((owners (getf registry :components)))
    (flet ((mounted (entry)
             (find (first entry) owners :key (lambda (c) (getf c :id)) :test #'equal)))
      (setf (daemon-hook-failures daemon) (remove-if-not #'mounted (daemon-hook-failures daemon)))
      (dolist (view (remove-if (lambda (view) (and preserve-quarantine (view-quarantined view)))
                                     (daemon-views daemon)))
        (when (equal (view-notice view) (view-error view)) (setf (view-notice view) ""))
        (setf (view-error view) nil (view-quarantined view) nil)
        (unless (find (view-mode view) (getf registry :keymaps) :key (lambda (m) (getf m :name)))
          (setf (view-mode view) (getf (getf registry :options) :initial-keymap)))
        (setf (view-component-state view) (remove-if-not #'mounted (view-component-state view))
              (view-geometry-contributions view) (remove-if-not #'mounted (view-geometry-contributions view))
              (view-contributions view) nil (view-decorations view) nil (view-pane-notes view) nil
              (view-hook-context view) nil (view-hooks view) nil)
        (let* ((panes (view-panes view)) (ids (mapcar #'pane-id panes)))
          (unless (member (view-focus-id view) ids)
            (setf (view-focus-id view) (first ids) (view-zoom view) nil))
          (dolist (id (loop for id being the hash-keys of (view-pane-states view)
                           unless (member id ids) collect id))
            (remhash id (view-pane-states view))))
        (reset-view-input view t)
        (reset-layout-state (view-layout-state view) registry)
        ;; Same-owner reload also reconstructs placement/camera effects.
        (setf (layout-state-requested-context (view-layout-state view)) nil
              (layout-state-requested-token (view-layout-state view)) nil
              (layout-state-committed-context (view-layout-state view)) nil
              (layout-state-committed-token (view-layout-state view)) nil)
        (clear-layout-provider view)
        (layout view))))
  (update-application-focus daemon)
  (incf (daemon-revision daemon)))
(defun command-pending-p (view)
  (let* ((daemon (view-daemon view)) (worker (daemon-worker daemon))
         (request (and worker (extension-worker-request worker))))
    (or (some (lambda (pending) (eq view (command-origin-view (pending-split-origin pending))))
              (daemon-pending-splits daemon))
        (member view (daemon-initialization-queue daemon))
        (and (consp request) (eq (first request) :initialize) (eq (second request) view))
        (and (command-request-p request) (eq (command-origin-view (command-request-origin request)) view))
        (some (lambda (request) (eq (command-origin-view (command-request-origin request)) view))
              (daemon-command-queue daemon))
        (and (daemon-candidate daemon)
             (or (null worker) (daemon-candidate-contexts daemon))))))
(defun defer-input (view kind data)
  (when (> (+ (view-input-bytes view) (length data)) 65536)
    (error "Pending command input exceeds 64 KiB"))
  (push (list (view-wire view) (view-epoch view) kind (copy-seq data)) (view-input-queue view))
  (incf (view-input-bytes view) (length data)))
(defun replay-input (view)
  (let ((pending (nreverse (view-input-queue view))))
    (setf (view-input-queue view) nil (view-input-bytes view) 0)
    (dolist (item pending)
      (destructuring-bind (wire epoch kind data) item
        (when (and wire (eq wire (view-wire view)) (= epoch (view-epoch view)))
          (server-packet view wire
            (concatenate '(vector (unsigned-byte 8)) (octets-from-list (list kind)) data)))))))
(defun replay-all-input (daemon)
  (dolist (view (daemon-views daemon)) (replay-input view)))
(defun semantic-key (bytes key)
  (or (rest (assoc (map 'string #'code-char bytes)
                 (loop for (suffix . value) in '(("[A" . :up) ("[B" . :down) ("[C" . :right) ("[D" . :left)
                                                 ("OA" . :up) ("OB" . :down) ("OC" . :right) ("OD" . :left)
                                                 ("[5~" . :page-up)
                                                 ("[6~" . :page-down) ("[H" . :home) ("[F" . :end))
                       collect (cons (concatenate 'string (string #\Esc) suffix) value)) :test #'equal))
      (rest (assoc key '((57350 . :left) (57351 . :right) (57352 . :up) (57353 . :down) (57354 . :page-up) (57355 . :page-down)
                        (57356 . :home) (57357 . :end)))) key))
(defun key-binding (view map key)
  (find-if (lambda (b) (and (eq map (getf b :map)) (equal key (getf b :key))))
                          (reverse (getf (daemon-registry (view-daemon view)) :bindings))))
(defun binding-key (bytes key modifiers)
  "Lookup key for a keymap binding. The caller passes only structural input
modifiers (bit 1 Shift, bit 2 Alt, bit 4 Ctrl, bit 8 Super); any of them yields a
(MODS . KEY) chord with value bit 1 for Alt, bit 2 for Super, bit 4 for Shift and
bit 8 for Ctrl. With no structural modifier the plain integer key is returned, so
legacy control bytes still match a folded C- chord."
  (let ((semantic (semantic-key bytes key))
        (shift (logbitp 0 modifiers)) (alt (logbitp 1 modifiers))
        (ctrl (logbitp 2 modifiers)) (super (logbitp 3 modifiers)))
    (if (or shift alt ctrl super)
        (cons (logior (if alt 1 0) (if super 2 0) (if shift 4 0) (if ctrl 8 0)) semantic)
        semantic)))
(defun dispatch-binding (view map key)
  (let ((binding (key-binding view map key)))
    (when (and binding (getf binding :command))
      (handler-case (request-command view (getf binding :command)
                                     (list :arguments (getf binding :arguments)) nil)
        (error (e) (note-error view e))))))
(defun dispatch-keymap-fallback (view map key bytes &optional read-bytes)
  "Dispatch a named keymap fallback through the ordinary worker command path."
  (let* ((policy (find map (reverse (getf (daemon-registry (view-daemon view)) :keymaps))
                       :key (lambda (m) (getf m :name))))
         (fallback (and policy (getf policy :unbound))))
    (when (stringp fallback)
      (handler-case
          (request-command view fallback
                           (append (list :key (semantic-key bytes key)
                                         :bytes (coerce bytes 'list))
                                   (when (view-input-read-framed view)
                                     (list :read-bytes (and read-bytes (coerce read-bytes 'list))))) nil)
        (error (e) (note-error view e)))
      t)))
(defun dispatch-command-request (request)
  (let* ((origin (command-request-origin request)) (daemon (command-origin-daemon origin))
         (view (command-origin-view origin)) (worker (daemon-worker daemon)))
    (unless (origin-current-p origin) (error "Command view changed before dispatch"))
    (when (view-quarantined view) (error "View callbacks quarantined until a successful config reload"))
    (origin-target origin)
    (extension-send (extension-worker-output worker)
      (list :dispatch :command (command-request-name request)
            (command-request-snapshot request) (command-request-event request)))
    (setf (view-notice view) "" (extension-worker-request worker) request
          (extension-worker-deadline worker) (+ (now) 1/20))))
(defun request-command (view name event peer)
  (when (view-quarantined view)
    (error "View callbacks quarantined until a successful config reload"))
  (let* ((daemon (view-daemon view)) (worker (daemon-worker daemon))
         (origin (capture-origin view peer))
         (request (make-command-request :origin origin :name name
                                        :event (copy-component-state event)
                                        :snapshot (declared-context (context-data view)
                                                                    (command-reads (daemon-registry daemon) name)))))
    (unless (find name (getf (daemon-registry daemon) :commands)
                  :key (lambda (c) (getf c :name)) :test #'equal)
      (error "Unknown command: ~A" name))
    (unless worker (error "Extension worker is restarting; use config reload if recovery fails"))
    (when (>= (length (daemon-command-queue daemon)) 32) (error "Command queue full"))
    (setf (daemon-command-queue daemon) (append (daemon-command-queue daemon) (list request)))
    (schedule-work daemon)))
(defun command-reply (peer message &optional errorp)
  (when (and peer (>= (wire-fd peer) 0))
    (handler-case
        (progn (send-packet peer (if errorp 21 20) (text-bytes message)) (flush-wire peer))
      (error () (close-wire peer)))))
(defun error-text (error)
  (let ((message (princ-to-string error))) (subseq message 0 (min 1000 (length message)))))
(defun note-error (view error)
  (setf (view-error view) (error-text error)
        (view-notice view) (view-error view))
  (incf (view-revision view))
  (format *error-output* "view ~A: ~A~%" (view-id view) (view-notice view)))
(defun note-workspace-error (daemon error)
  (setf (daemon-config-error daemon) (error-text error))
  (incf (daemon-revision daemon))
  (format *error-output* "configuration ~A: ~A~%" *instance* (daemon-config-error daemon)))
(defun start-reload (daemon peer &optional recover source-worker executable)
  "Load a candidate worker. EXECUTABLE, named by the reloading client, supplies the
extension code; otherwise the active worker's binary does, so recovery keeps it."
  (when (daemon-candidate daemon) (error "A configuration reload is already running"))
  (when recover
    (unless source-worker (setf (daemon-recovery-budget daemon) (1+ (length (daemon-views daemon)))))
    (when (minusp (decf (daemon-recovery-budget daemon)))
      (error "Recovery budget exhausted; reload configuration explicitly")))
  (let* ((active (or source-worker (daemon-worker daemon)))
         (path (or (daemon-config-path daemon) (and active (extension-worker-path active))
                   (namestring (merge-pathnames "__ekko-empty-init__.lisp"
                                 (uiop:ensure-directory-pathname (or (daemon-launch-directory daemon) "."))))))
         (source (if (and recover active) (extension-worker-source active)
                     (config-file-source (daemon-config-path daemon)))))
    (setf (daemon-candidate daemon)
          (start-worker source path (format nil "~A.extensions.log" (socket-path))
                        :directory (daemon-launch-directory daemon)
                        :environment (daemon-launch-environment daemon)
                        ;; A collected store path falls back to the daemon's own
                        ;; binary, whose revision always matches.
                        :executable (let ((chosen (or executable (and active (extension-worker-executable active)))))
                                      (if (and chosen (probe-file chosen)) chosen "/proc/self/exe")))
          (daemon-reload-peer daemon) peer
          (daemon-candidate-views daemon) nil (daemon-candidate-results daemon) nil
          (daemon-candidate-contexts daemon) nil (daemon-candidate-layouts daemon) nil
          (daemon-candidate-stage daemon) :load (daemon-candidate-members daemon) nil)
    (setf (extension-worker-recovery (daemon-candidate daemon)) recover)))
(defun hook-context-current-p (view request)
  (let* ((daemon (view-daemon view))
         (component (find (hook-request-owner request) (getf (daemon-registry daemon) :components)
                          :key (lambda (c) (getf c :id)) :test #'equal))
         (current (context-data view)))
    (and (origin-current-p (hook-request-origin request)) component
         (every (lambda (key) (equal (getf (hook-request-snapshot request) key) (getf current key)))
                (getf component :reads)))))
(defun initialization-components (registry)
  (remove-if-not (lambda (c) (getf c :initialize)) (getf registry :components)))
(defun initialization-context (view registry)
  (let ((context (context-data view registry)))
    (unless (find (getf context :mode) (getf registry :keymaps) :key (lambda (m) (getf m :name)))
      (setf (getf context :mode) (getf (getf registry :options) :initial-keymap)))
    (unless (find (getf context :focus) (getf context :panes) :key (lambda (pane) (getf pane :id)))
      (setf (getf context :focus) (getf (first (getf context :panes)) :id)
            (getf context :zoom) nil))
    (setf (getf context :component-state)
          (remove-if-not (lambda (entry)
                           (find (first entry) (getf registry :components)
                                 :key (lambda (c) (getf c :id)) :test #'equal))
                         (getf context :component-state)))
    context))
(defun initialization-current-p (view worker &optional before)
  (let* ((registry (extension-worker-registry worker))
         (before (or before (extension-worker-initialization-context worker)))
         (current (initialization-context view registry))
         (reads (append '(:component-state :mode)
                        (loop for c in (initialization-components registry) append (getf c :reads)))))
    (every (lambda (key) (equal (getf before key) (getf current key))) reads)))
(defun begin-initialization (view worker reason)
  (let* ((registry (extension-worker-registry worker))
         ;; Keep the implicit staleness keys even when no initializer reads them.
         (context (declared-context
                    (initialization-context view registry)
                    (append '(:component-state :mode)
                            (loop for c in (initialization-components registry)
                                  append (getf c :reads))))))
    (setf (extension-worker-initialization-context worker) context
          (extension-worker-request worker) (list :initialize view context (capture-origin view))
          (extension-worker-deadline worker) (+ (now) 1/20))
    (extension-send (extension-worker-output worker)
                    (list :initialize context (list :type :initialize :reason reason)))))
(defun stage-initialization (view registry results)
  (unless (equal (mapcar (lambda (entry) (getf entry :owner)) results)
                 (mapcar (lambda (c) (getf c :id)) (initialization-components registry)))
    (error "Invalid initialization owners"))
  ;; Validation is pure. No shallow daemon/view copy can accidentally expose
  ;; shared pane mutations or durable writes during a rejected reload.
  (let ((state (copy-component-state (retained-owner-entries (view-component-state view) registry))))
    (dolist (result results)
      (let ((actions (getf result :actions)) (owner (getf result :owner)))
        (dolist (action actions)
          (unless (member (first action) '(:set-state :store-set :set-keymap :status :decorate))
            (error "Initialization may only set state, store, keymap, status or decorations")))
        (validate-actions view actions nil owner registry t)
        (let ((action (find :set-state actions :key #'first)))
          (when action
            (setf state (remove owner state :key #'car :test #'equal))
            (when (getf (rest action) :value)
              (push (cons owner (getf (rest action) :value)) state)))))
      ;; Commit validates each owner's replacement against earlier results,
      ;; not only the final state after later owners remove their entries.
      (when (> (component-state-bytes state) 16384) (error "Aggregate component state exceeds 16 KiB"))))
  (stage-store-actions (daemon-store-entries (view-daemon view)) results)
  (let ((spans (loop for result in results append
                (copy-list (getf (rest (find :decorate (getf result :actions) :key #'first :from-end t)) :spans)))))
    (when (> (length spans) 1024) (error "At most 1024 retained decoration spans are allowed"))
    (when (> (loop for span in spans sum (* (getf span :rows 1) (length (getf span :text)))) 16000)
      (error "Retained decoration text exceeds 16000 characters")))
  results)
(defun apply-initialization (view results)
  (dolist (result results)
    (apply-actions view (getf result :owner) (getf result :actions) nil nil))
  (setf (view-extension-initialized view) t (view-initialization-failure view) nil
        (daemon-initialization-queue (view-daemon view))
        (remove view (daemon-initialization-queue (view-daemon view)))))
(defun request-view (request)
  (typecase request
    (command-request (command-origin-view (command-request-origin request)))
    (hook-request (command-origin-view (hook-request-origin request)))
    (layout-request (first (layout-request-token request)))
    (cons (when (eq (first request) :initialize) (second request)))))
(defun layout-token (view)
  (list view (view-epoch view) (daemon-config-generation (view-daemon view))))
(defun fail-command (request error)
  (let* ((origin (command-request-origin request)) (view (command-origin-view origin)))
    (when (origin-current-p origin) (note-error view error))
    (command-reply (command-origin-peer origin) (princ-to-string error) t)))
(defun stage-store-actions (store results)
  "Validate aggregate writes against a detached store in commit order."
  (let ((store (copy-component-state store)))
    (dolist (result results store)
      (dolist (action (getf result :actions))
        (when (eq (first action) :store-set)
          (let* ((args (rest action)) (namespace (store-namespace (getf result :owner)))
                 (entries (remove (getf args :key) (rest (assoc namespace store :test #'equal))
                                  :key #'car :test #'equal)))
            (when (getf args :value)
              (push (cons (getf args :key) (copy-component-state (getf args :value))) entries))
            (when (> (component-state-bytes (list (cons namespace entries))) 16384)
              (error "Store namespace exceeds 16 KiB"))
            (setf store (acons namespace entries (remove namespace store :key #'car :test #'equal)))))))))
(defun candidate-view-context (view registry results)
  "Apply allowed initializer outputs only to a detached candidate snapshot."
  (let ((context (initialization-context view registry)) (contributions nil))
    (dolist (result results)
      (let ((owner (getf result :owner)))
        (dolist (action (getf result :actions))
          (let ((args (rest action)))
            (case (first action)
              (:set-state
               (setf (getf context :component-state)
                     (remove owner (getf context :component-state) :key #'car :test #'equal))
               (when (getf args :value)
                 (push (cons owner (copy-component-state (getf args :value))) (getf context :component-state))))
              (:set-keymap (setf (getf context :mode) (getf args :name)))
              (:status (setf contributions (acons owner (getf args :text)
                                                  (remove owner contributions :key #'car :test #'equal)))))))))
    (setf (getf context :store)
          (stage-store-actions (getf context :store)
            (loop for entry in (daemon-candidate-results (view-daemon view)) append (rest entry)))
          (getf context :pane-notes) nil
          (getf (getf context :chrome-status) :text) (status-text view registry contributions))
    context))
(define-condition candidate-stale (simple-error) ())
(defun check-candidate-members (daemon)
  (when (set-exclusive-or (daemon-candidate-members daemon) (daemon-views daemon) :test #'eq)
    (error 'candidate-stale :format-control "View membership changed; retry configuration reload")))
(defun candidate-participants (daemon candidate)
  (remove-if (lambda (view) (and (extension-worker-recovery candidate) (view-quarantined view)))
             (daemon-candidate-members daemon)))
(defun quarantine-view (view error)
  "An unchanged view callback cannot repeatedly kill its peers' shared worker."
  (let* ((daemon (view-daemon view))
         (message (format nil "~A; view callbacks quarantined until a successful config reload" (error-text error))))
    (setf (view-quarantined view) t (view-hooks view) nil
          (daemon-initialization-queue daemon) (remove view (daemon-initialization-queue daemon)))
    (reset-view-input view)
    (dolist (request (daemon-command-queue daemon))
      (when (eq view (command-origin-view (command-request-origin request))) (fail-command request message)))
    (setf (daemon-command-queue daemon)
          (remove-if (lambda (request) (eq view (command-origin-view (command-request-origin request))))
                     (daemon-command-queue daemon)))
    (note-error view message)))
(defun reject-candidate (daemon candidate error)
  (note-workspace-error daemon error)
  (command-reply (daemon-reload-peer daemon) (princ-to-string error) t)
  (stop-worker candidate)
  (setf (daemon-candidate daemon) nil (daemon-reload-peer daemon) nil
        (daemon-candidate-views daemon) nil (daemon-candidate-results daemon) nil
        (daemon-candidate-contexts daemon) nil (daemon-candidate-layouts daemon) nil
        (daemon-candidate-stage daemon) nil (daemon-candidate-members daemon) nil))
(defun commit-candidate (daemon candidate)
  (check-candidate-members daemon)
  (let ((registry (extension-worker-registry candidate)))
    (stage-store-actions (daemon-store-entries daemon)
      (loop for entry in (daemon-candidate-results daemon) append (rest entry)))
    (dolist (entry (daemon-candidate-contexts daemon))
      (unless (and (origin-current-p (third entry))
                   (initialization-current-p (first entry) candidate (second entry)))
        (error 'candidate-stale :format-control "Initialization dependencies changed; retry configuration reload")))
    (dolist (entry (daemon-candidate-layouts daemon))
      (destructuring-bind (view state request) entry
        (declare (ignore state))
        (when request
          (unless (layout-request-current-p request registry
                    (candidate-view-context view registry (rest (assoc view (daemon-candidate-results daemon))))
                    (layout-token view))
            (error 'candidate-stale :format-control "Layout dependencies changed; retry configuration reload")))))
    ;; Every candidate callback has passed before registrations or live output
    ;; change. Application content is outside this reconstruction boundary.
    (stop-worker (daemon-worker daemon))
    (setf (daemon-worker daemon) candidate (daemon-candidate daemon) nil)
    (install-registry daemon registry (extension-worker-recovery candidate))
    (dolist (entry (daemon-candidate-results daemon)) (apply-initialization (first entry) (rest entry)))
    (dolist (entry (daemon-candidate-layouts daemon))
      (destructuring-bind (view state request) entry
        (when request
          (setf (view-layout-state view) state
                (layout-state-requested-token state) (layout-token view)
                (layout-state-committed-token state) (layout-token view))
          (apply-actions view (layout-state-owner state)
                         (list (list :place-panes :version 1 :placements (layout-state-placements state)
                                     :camera (layout-state-camera state))) :layout nil))))
    (setf (daemon-candidate-results daemon) nil (daemon-candidate-contexts daemon) nil
          (daemon-candidate-layouts daemon) nil (daemon-candidate-stage daemon) nil
          (daemon-candidate-members daemon) nil
          (extension-worker-initialization-context candidate) nil)
    (unless (extension-worker-recovery candidate)
      (setf (daemon-disabled-hooks daemon) nil (daemon-hook-failures daemon) nil))
    (command-reply (daemon-reload-peer daemon) "")
    (setf (daemon-reload-peer daemon) nil)
    (replay-all-input daemon)))
(defun service-candidate (daemon buffer)
  (let ((candidate (daemon-candidate daemon)) (failed-request nil) (callback-failed nil))
    (when candidate
      (handler-case
          (progn
            (unless (eq (daemon-candidate-stage daemon) :load) (check-candidate-members daemon))
            (when (extension-worker-request candidate)
              (let* ((request (extension-worker-request candidate))
                     (response (progn
                                 (setf failed-request request)
                                 (handler-case (poll-worker candidate buffer)
                                   (error (e) (setf callback-failed t) (error e))))))
                (when response
                  (when (eq (first response) :error)
                    (setf callback-failed t) (error "~A" (second response)))
                  (setf (extension-worker-request candidate) nil)
                  (cond
                    ((eq request :load)
                     (validate-registry (ready-registry response))
                     ;; Quarantined views keep their last valid owner output.
                     ;; Do not reuse it with silently changed registrations.
                     (when (and (extension-worker-recovery candidate)
                                (some #'view-quarantined (daemon-views daemon))
                                (not (equal (second response) (daemon-registry daemon))))
                       (error "Recovery registry changed; reload configuration explicitly"))
                     (setf (extension-worker-registry candidate) (second response)
                           (daemon-candidate-stage daemon) :initialize
                           (daemon-candidate-members daemon) (copy-list (daemon-views daemon))
                           (daemon-candidate-views daemon) (candidate-participants daemon candidate)))
                    ((layout-request-p request)
                     (let* ((view (first (layout-request-token request)))
                            (entry (assoc view (daemon-candidate-layouts daemon)))
                            (registry (extension-worker-registry candidate))
                            (context (candidate-view-context view registry (rest (assoc view (daemon-candidate-results daemon))))))
                       (unless (eq :committed (complete-layout (second entry) request registry context (layout-token view) response))
                         (error 'candidate-stale :format-control "Layout dependencies changed; retry configuration reload"))))
                    (t
                     (let ((view (second request)))
                       (unless (and (origin-current-p (fourth request))
                                    (initialization-current-p view candidate (third request)))
                         (error 'candidate-stale :format-control "Initialization dependencies changed; retry configuration reload"))
                       (unless (eq (first response) :initialized) (error "Invalid initialization reply"))
                       (stage-initialization view (extension-worker-registry candidate) (second response))
                       (push (cons view (second response)) (daemon-candidate-results daemon))
                       (push (list view (third request) (fourth request)) (daemon-candidate-contexts daemon))))))))
            (when (and (null (extension-worker-request candidate))
                       (or (null (daemon-worker daemon)) (null (extension-worker-request (daemon-worker daemon)))))
              (check-candidate-members daemon)
              (when (and (eq (daemon-candidate-stage daemon) :initialize) (null (daemon-candidate-views daemon)))
                (setf (daemon-candidate-stage daemon) :layout
                      (daemon-candidate-views daemon) (candidate-participants daemon candidate)))
              (case (daemon-candidate-stage daemon)
                (:initialize
                 (begin-initialization (pop (daemon-candidate-views daemon)) candidate
                                       (if (extension-worker-recovery candidate) :recovery :reload)))
                (:layout
                 (if (daemon-candidate-views daemon)
                     (let* ((view (pop (daemon-candidate-views daemon))) (state (make-layout-state))
                            (registry (extension-worker-registry candidate))
                            (context (candidate-view-context view registry (rest (assoc view (daemon-candidate-results daemon)))))
                            (request (schedule-layout candidate state registry context (layout-token view))))
                       (push (list view state request) (daemon-candidate-layouts daemon)))
                     (commit-candidate daemon candidate))))))
        (error (e)
          (let* ((view (and failed-request (request-view failed-request)))
                 (retry (and (extension-worker-recovery candidate)
                             (or (typep e 'candidate-stale)
                                 (and callback-failed view
                                      (or (layout-request-p failed-request)
                                          (and (consp failed-request) (eq (first failed-request) :initialize))))))))
            (when (and retry callback-failed view) (quarantine-view view e))
            (reject-candidate daemon candidate e)
            (when retry
              (handler-case (start-reload daemon nil t candidate)
                (error (recovery-error) (note-workspace-error daemon recovery-error))))
            (when (and (null (daemon-worker daemon)) (null (daemon-candidate daemon)))
              (dolist (request (daemon-command-queue daemon)) (fail-command request e))
              (setf (daemon-command-queue daemon) nil))
            (replay-all-input daemon)))))))

(defun service-extensions (daemon buffer)
  (service-candidate daemon buffer)
  (let ((worker (daemon-worker daemon)))
    (when worker
      (handler-case
          (let ((response (poll-worker worker buffer)) (request (extension-worker-request worker)))
            (when response
              (setf (extension-worker-request worker) nil)
              (handler-case
                  (let ((view (request-view request)))
                    (when (eq (first response) :error) (error "~A" (second response)))
                    (cond
                      ((layout-request-p request)
                       (when (eq :committed (complete-layout (view-layout-state view) request
                                              (daemon-registry daemon) (context-data view)
                                              (layout-token view) response))
                         (apply-actions view (layout-request-owner request)
                                        (getf (second response) :actions) :layout nil)))
                      ((and (consp request) (eq (first request) :initialize))
                       (unless (and (origin-current-p (fourth request))
                                    (initialization-current-p view worker (third request)))
                         (error "View changed during initialization"))
                       (unless (eq (first response) :initialized) (error "Invalid initialization reply"))
                       (stage-initialization view (daemon-registry daemon) (second response))
                       (apply-initialization view (second response))
                       (setf (extension-worker-initialization-context worker) nil))
                      (t
                       (unless (eq (first response) :result) (error "Invalid extension result"))
                       (cond
                         ((command-request-p request)
                          (let ((origin (command-request-origin request)))
                            (unless (origin-current-p origin) (error "Command view changed before completion"))
                            (let ((result (apply-actions view (getf (second response) :owner)
                                                         (getf (second response) :actions) nil origin)))
                              (unless (or (eq result :pending)
                                          (eq (command-origin-peer origin) (daemon-reload-peer daemon)))
                                (command-reply (command-origin-peer origin) "")))))
                         ((hook-request-p request)
                          (note-hook-answered daemon (hook-request-owner request))
                          (when (hook-context-current-p view request)
                            (apply-actions view (getf (second response) :owner) (getf (second response) :actions)
                                           t (hook-request-origin request))))))))
                (error (e)
                  (if (command-request-p request) (fail-command request e)
                      (progn
                        (when (layout-request-p request)
                          (fail-layout (view-layout-state (request-view request)) request e))
                        (when (and (consp request) (eq (first request) :initialize))
                          (setf (view-initialization-failure (request-view request))
                                (list (fourth request) (third request))))
                        (when (request-view request) (note-error (request-view request) e))))))
              ;; Hand a newly free active-worker slot to a loaded candidate
              ;; before input replay can queue another continuously eligible hook.
              (service-candidate daemon buffer)
              (replay-all-input daemon)))
        (error (e)
          (let* ((request (extension-worker-request worker)) (view (request-view request))
                 (view-callback (and view (or (layout-request-p request)
                                              (and (consp request) (eq (first request) :initialize))))))
            (if view-callback (quarantine-view view e) (note-workspace-error daemon e))
            (when (command-request-p request) (fail-command request e))
            (when (hook-request-p request) (note-hook-timeout daemon (hook-request-owner request)))
            (when (layout-request-p request)
              (fail-layout (view-layout-state view) request e))
            ;; A failed active worker cannot be the fallback for a rejected
            ;; user candidate. Reject that transaction, then recover its last
            ;; accepted source rather than the newly edited file.
            (when (daemon-candidate daemon)
              (reject-candidate daemon (daemon-candidate daemon)
                                "Active callback failed during reload; retry configuration reload"))
            (handler-case (start-reload daemon nil t)
              (error (recovery-error) (note-workspace-error daemon recovery-error)))
            (stop-worker worker) (setf (daemon-worker daemon) nil))))))
  (complete-pending-splits daemon)
  (schedule-hooks daemon)
  ;; A view without initializers can become ready without another response.
  ;; Replay after scheduling, not inside it, so enqueueing input cannot recurse
  ;; through synchronous initialization. The saved wire/epoch keeps framing.
  (dolist (view (daemon-views daemon))
    (when (and (view-input-queue view) (not (command-pending-p view)))
      (handler-case (replay-input view)
        (error (e) (note-error view e))))))
(defconstant +hook-deadline+ 1/2)
(defconstant +hook-timeout-limit+ 3)
(defun note-hook-timeout (daemon owner)
  (let ((entry (assoc owner (daemon-hook-failures daemon) :test #'equal)))
    (if entry (incf (rest entry)) (push (cons owner 1) (daemon-hook-failures daemon)))
    (when (>= (rest (assoc owner (daemon-hook-failures daemon) :test #'equal)) +hook-timeout-limit+)
      (pushnew owner (daemon-disabled-hooks daemon) :test #'equal)
      (dolist (view (daemon-views daemon))
        (setf (view-hooks view) (remove owner (view-hooks view) :test #'equal))))))
(defun note-hook-answered (daemon owner)
  (setf (daemon-hook-failures daemon) (remove owner (daemon-hook-failures daemon) :key #'car :test #'equal)))
(defun views-after (views previous)
  (let ((tail (member previous views)))
    (if tail (append (rest tail) (ldiff views (rest tail))) views)))
