(defun schedule-view-work (daemon worker kind)
  (let ((registry (daemon-registry daemon)))
    (dolist (view (views-after (daemon-views daemon)
                              (rest (assoc kind (daemon-dispatch-cursors daemon)))))
      (when (and (not (view-quarantined view))
                 (case kind
                   (:initialize (member view (daemon-initialization-queue daemon)))
                   (:layout (view-extension-initialized view))
                   (:hook (and (view-extension-initialized view) (view-hooks view)))))
        (let ((layoutp (member kind '(:initialize :layout)))
              (id (when (eq kind :hook) (pop (view-hooks view)))))
          (handler-case
              (let ((context (context-data view)) (state (view-layout-state view))
                    (token (layout-token view)))
                (case kind
                  (:initialize
                   ;; Retry a failed initializer only after its declared reads
                   ;; or origin changes, not because unrelated time advanced.
                   (let ((failure (view-initialization-failure view)))
                     (unless (and failure (origin-current-p (first failure))
                                  (initialization-current-p view worker (second failure)))
                       ;; Failed geometry blocks only this view's initializer.
                       (if (layout-state-current-p state registry context token)
                           (progn
                             (setf layoutp nil)
                             (if (initialization-components registry)
                                 (begin-initialization view worker :attach)
                                 (apply-initialization view nil)))
                           (schedule-layout worker state registry context token)))))
                  (:layout (schedule-layout worker state registry context token))
                  (:hook
                   (let* ((snapshot (declared-context
                                      context
                                      (getf (find id (getf registry :components)
                                                  :key (lambda (c) (getf c :id)) :test #'equal)
                                            :reads)))
                          (request (make-hook-request :origin (capture-origin view)
                                                      :owner id :snapshot snapshot)))
                     (extension-send (extension-worker-output worker)
                                     (list :dispatch :hook id snapshot (list :type :change)))
                     (setf (extension-worker-request worker) request
                           (extension-worker-deadline worker) (+ (now) +hook-deadline+))))))
            (error (e)
              (when layoutp (setf (layout-state-error (view-layout-state view)) (error-text e)))
              ;; BEGIN-INITIALIZATION installs its request before sending it.
              ;; Failed sends cannot leave that view owning the shared worker.
              (when (eq kind :initialize)
                (let ((request (extension-worker-request worker)))
                  (when (and (not layoutp) request)
                    ;; Retain the exact origin and detached pre-send snapshot
                    ;; already captured by BEGIN-INITIALIZATION.
                    (setf (view-initialization-failure view)
                          (list (fourth request) (third request)))))
                (setf (extension-worker-request worker) nil
                      (extension-worker-initialization-context worker) nil))
              (note-error view e)))
          (when (extension-worker-request worker)
            (let ((cursor (assoc kind (daemon-dispatch-cursors daemon))))
              (if cursor (setf (rest cursor) view)
                  (push (cons kind view) (daemon-dispatch-cursors daemon))))
            (return (extension-worker-request worker))))))))
(defun schedule-work (daemon)
  (let ((worker (daemon-worker daemon)))
    (when (and worker (null (extension-worker-request worker)))
      ;; At most four kinds and one pass over each view; never drain a busy
      ;; client's work before checking its peers. Only one callback is queued.
      (loop for offset below 4
            for turn = (mod (+ (daemon-work-turn daemon) offset) 4)
            for kind = (nth turn '(:initialize :command :layout :hook)) do
        (if (eq kind :command)
            (let ((request
                    (find-if (lambda (request)
                               (let ((origin (command-request-origin request)))
                                 (or (not (origin-current-p origin))
                                     (not (member (command-origin-view origin)
                                                  (daemon-initialization-queue daemon))))))
                             (daemon-command-queue daemon))))
              (when request
                (setf (daemon-command-queue daemon) (remove request (daemon-command-queue daemon) :count 1))
                (let ((origin (command-request-origin request)))
                  (handler-case (dispatch-command-request request)
                    (error (e)
                      (fail-command request e)
                      (when (origin-current-p origin)
                        (handler-case (replay-input (command-origin-view origin))
                          (error (input-error) (note-error (command-origin-view origin) input-error)))))))))
            (schedule-view-work daemon worker kind))
        (when (extension-worker-request worker)
          (setf (daemon-work-turn daemon) (mod (1+ turn) 4))
          (return (extension-worker-request worker)))))))
(defun schedule-hooks (daemon)
  ;; Collect detached snapshots even while another callback owns the worker.
  ;; Pending owners remain FIFO when dependencies change again before dispatch.
  (dolist (view (daemon-views daemon))
    (when (and (view-extension-initialized view) (not (view-quarantined view)))
      (handler-case
          (let ((context (context-data view)) (previous (view-hook-context view)))
            (unless (equal context previous)
              (let ((changed (loop for key in ekko/extensions:*context-keys*
                                   unless (and previous (equal (getf context key) (getf previous key))) collect key)))
                (dolist (component (getf (daemon-registry daemon) :components))
                  (let ((id (getf component :id)))
                    (when (and (getf component :hook)
                               (not (member id (daemon-disabled-hooks daemon) :test #'equal))
                               (or (null previous) (intersection changed (getf component :reads)))
                               (not (member id (view-hooks view) :test #'equal)))
                      (setf (view-hooks view) (append (view-hooks view) (list id)))))))
              (setf (view-hook-context view) context)))
        (error (e) (note-error view e)))))
  (schedule-work daemon))

(defun valid-decoration-text-p (text)
  (and (stringp text)
       (every (lambda (char)
                (let ((code (char-code char)))
                  (and (>= code 32) (/= code 127) (not (<= #x80 code #x9f))))) text)))
(defun validate-decoration-spans (spans &optional menu-p)
  (unless (and (listp spans) (<= (length spans) 1024))
    (error "At most 1024 decoration spans are allowed"))
  (let ((total 0))
    (dolist (span spans)
      (unless (and (listp span) (evenp (length span))) (error "Malformed decoration span: ~S" span))
      (let ((keys nil))
        (loop for (key value) on span by #'cddr do
          (unless (member key '(:x :y :text :sgr :rows :overlay :action :context-command :command :arguments :hover-sgr :gradient :follows :wheel-command :middle-command :pane :drag :enter :key)) (error "Unknown decoration field: ~S" key))
          (when (member key keys) (error "Duplicate decoration field: ~S" key))
          (push key keys))
        (let ((x (getf span :x)) (y (getf span :y)) (text (getf span :text))
              (sgr (getf span :sgr)) (rows (getf span :rows 1)))
          (unless (and (integerp x) (<= 0 x 499) (integerp y) (<= 0 y 299)
                       (member (getf span :overlay) '(nil t))
                       (integerp rows) (<= 1 rows 300)
                       (valid-decoration-text-p text)
                       (listp sgr) (<= (length sgr) 16)
                       (every (lambda (n) (and (integerp n) (<= 0 n 255))) sgr))
            (error "Invalid decoration span: ~S" span))
          (let ((enter (getf span :enter)) (key (getf span :key)))
            (unless (or (null enter)
                        (and (listp enter) (= (length enter) 3) (stringp (first enter))
                             (<= 1 (length (first enter)) 64)
                             (typep (second enter) '(integer -500 500)) (typep (third enter) '(integer -300 300))))
              (error "Entrance must be (id dx dy): ~S" enter))
            (unless (or (null key) (and menu-p (stringp key) (= (length key) 1) (graphic-char-p (char key 0))))
              (error "A menu key must be one printable character: ~S" key)))
          (when (or (getf span :pane) (getf span :drag))
            (unless (and (not menu-p) (typep (getf span :pane) '(integer 1 *))
                         (member (getf span :drag) '(nil :move :left :right :top :bottom :top-left :top-right :bottom-left :bottom-right)))
              (error "Invalid window handle")))
          (let ((action (getf span :action)) (command (getf span :command))
                (context (getf span :context-command)) (hover (getf span :hover-sgr))
                (wheel (getf span :wheel-command)) (middle (getf span :middle-command)))
            (when action
              (unless (or (and (listp action) (= (length action) 3)
                               (member (first action) '(:focus :minimize :restore :zoom :close :float :tile))
                               (eq (second action) :pane) (typep (third action) '(integer 1 *)))
                          (and (listp action) (= (length action) 5) (eq (first action) :split)
                               (eq (second action) :pane) (typep (third action) '(integer 1 *))
                               (eq (fourth action) :axis) (member (fifth action) '(:rows :columns))))
                (error "Invalid decoration action: ~S" action)))
            (when (or command context wheel middle)
              (unless (and (or (not command) (and (not action) (stringp command) (<= 1 (length command) 80)))
                           (or (not context) (and (not menu-p) (stringp context) (<= 1 (length context) 80)))
                           (or (not wheel) (and (stringp wheel) (<= 1 (length wheel) 80)))
                           (or (not middle) (and (stringp middle) (<= 1 (length middle) 80)))
                           (listp (getf span :arguments)) (<= (length (getf span :arguments)) 8)
                           (every (lambda (arg) (and (stringp arg) (<= (length arg) 512)))
                                  (getf span :arguments)))
                (error "Invalid decoration command")))
            (when hover
              (unless (and (listp hover) (<= (length hover) 16)
                           (every (lambda (n) (typep n '(integer 0 255))) hover))
                (error "Invalid hover style")))
            (unless (typep (getf span :follows) '(or null (integer 1 *)))
              (error "A span follows a window by its pane id"))
            (let ((gradient (getf span :gradient)))
              (unless (or (null gradient)
                          (and (listp gradient) (<= (length gradient) 16)
                               (every (lambda (n) (typep n '(integer 0 255))) gradient)))
                (error "Invalid gradient style")))
            )
          (incf total (* rows (length text))))))
    (when (> total 16000) (error "Decoration text exceeds 16000 characters")))
  t)
(defun bounded-geometry-p (value length)
  (and (listp value) (= (length value) length)
       (every (lambda (n) (and (integerp n) (<= 0 n 16))) value)))
(defun validate-geometry-value (value)
  (unless (or (null value) (and (listp value) (evenp (length value))
                                (<= (length value) 8)))
    (error "Geometry value must be a proper property list"))
  (loop with seen = nil
        for (key item) on value by #'cddr do
    (unless (member key '(:pane-insets :boundary-insets :viewport-insets :split-gaps))
      (error "Unknown geometry field: ~S" key))
    (when (member key seen)
      (error "Duplicate geometry field: ~S" key))
    (push key seen)
    (unless (or (and (member key '(:pane-insets :boundary-insets :viewport-insets))
                     (bounded-geometry-p item 4))
                (and (eq key :split-gaps) (bounded-geometry-p item 2)))
      (error "Invalid geometry field: ~S" key)))
  (copy-tree value))
(defun validate-actions (view actions hook &optional owner
                              (registry (daemon-registry (view-daemon view))) staging)
  (when (eq hook :layout)
    (validate-layout-actions actions (getf (context-data view) :panes))
    (return-from validate-actions t))
  (unless (and (listp actions) (<= (length actions) 32)) (error "At most 32 actions are allowed"))
  (let ((primary-count 0) (mode-count 0) (state-count 0) (mode-before-primary nil) (primary-seen nil))
    (dolist (a actions)
      (unless (and (listp a) (oddp (length a))) (error "Malformed action: ~S" a))
      (let* ((op (first a)) (args (rest a)) (pane (getf args :pane))
             (keys (case op
                     (:pane-note '(:pane :text :sgr :duration))
                     (:notifications '(:op :id))
                     (:send-input '(:pane :bytes)) (:set-keymap '(:name)) (:set-layout '(:tree)) (:set-state '(:value)) (:store-set '(:key :value)) (:set-geometry '(:value)) (:decorate '(:spans)) (:show-menu '(:spans :x :y)) (:split '(:pane :axis :argv)) (:focus '(:pane)) (:rename '(:pane :text)) (:resize '(:pane :delta))
                     (:stop '(:force))
                     (:copy-point '(:pane :x :y :start)) (:copy-scroll '(:pane :delta))
                     (:close '(:pane :focus :force)) ((:minimize :restore :zoom :tile) '(:pane)) (:float '(:pane :rect)) (:place-window '(:pane :target :edge)) (:copy-move '(:pane :delta)) (:copy-edge '(:pane :edge)) (:status '(:text))
                     (:copy-selection '(:pane))
                     ((:focus-next :swap :copy-mode :copy-mark
                       :copy-exit :copy-search :copy-search-next :paste-buffer :help) '(:pane))
                     ((:detach :reload) nil)
                     (otherwise (error "Unknown action: ~S" op)))))
        (loop for (key value) on args by #'cddr do (unless (member key keys) (error "Unknown action argument: ~S" key)))
        (unless (= (length (loop for (key value) on args by #'cddr collect key))
                   (length (remove-duplicates (loop for (key value) on args by #'cddr collect key))))
          (error "Duplicate action argument"))
        (when (and hook (not (member op '(:status :decorate))))
          (error "Change hooks may only contribute status or decorations"))
        (when (and pane (not (member pane (view-panes view) :key #'pane-id)))
          (error "Pane is outside this view's workspace: ~S" pane))
        (when (and (member :force args) (not (member (getf args :force) '(nil t))))
          (error "Force must be a boolean"))
        (case op
          (:set-keymap
           (incf mode-count)
           (when (> mode-count 1) (error "A batch may contain at most one keymap transition"))
           (unless primary-seen (setf mode-before-primary t))
           (unless (find (getf args :name) (getf registry :keymaps)
                         :key (lambda (m) (getf m :name)))
             (error "Unknown keymap")))
          ((:status :decorate :show-menu :pane-note :notifications :set-state :store-set)
           (when (member op '(:set-state :store-set))
             (incf state-count)
             (when (> state-count 1) (error "A batch may contain at most one state update"))
             (unless (find owner (getf registry :components)
                           :key (lambda (component) (getf component :id)) :test #'equal)
               (error "State needs a registered component owner")))
           (cond
             ((eq op :set-state) (validate-component-state (getf args :value)))
             ((eq op :store-set)
              (let ((key (getf args :key)))
                (unless (or (and (stringp key) (<= 1 (length key) 128))
                            (and (keywordp key) (<= (length (symbol-name key)) 128)))
                  (error "Invalid store key")))
              (validate-component-state (getf args :value))
              (multiple-value-bind (namespace entries)
                  (store-set (view-daemon view) owner (getf args :key) (getf args :value))
                (when (> (component-state-bytes (list (cons namespace entries))) 16384)
                  (error "Store namespace exceeds 16 KiB"))))))
          (:set-geometry
           (incf primary-count) (setf primary-seen t)
           (unless (find owner (getf registry :components)
                         :key (lambda (component) (getf component :id)) :test #'equal)
             (error "Geometry needs a registered component owner"))
           (validate-geometry-value (getf args :value)))
          (otherwise (incf primary-count) (setf primary-seen t)))
        (case op
          ((:float :tile :place-window)
           (when (and (eq op :float) (getf args :rect)
                      (not (and (listp (getf args :rect)) (= (length (getf args :rect)) 4)
                                (every (lambda (n) (typep n '(integer 0 500))) (getf args :rect)))))
             (error "Invalid window rectangle"))
           (when (eq op :place-window)
             (let ((target (pane-by-id (view-daemon view) (getf args :target))))
               (unless (and target (not (eq target (or (pane-by-id (view-daemon view) pane) (focused-pane view))))
                            (not (pane-floating target)) (not (pane-view-minimized (pane-state view (pane-id target))))
                            (member (getf args :edge) '(:left :right :top :bottom :center)))
                 (error "Invalid snap target")))))
          (:copy-point
           (unless (and (typep (getf args :x) '(integer 0 499))
                        (typep (getf args :y) '(integer 0 299))
                        (member (getf args :start) '(nil t)))
             (error "Invalid copy point")))
          (:copy-scroll
           (unless (typep (getf args :delta) '(integer -300 300)) (error "Invalid copy scroll")))
          (:send-input
           (unless (and (listp (getf args :bytes)) (<= (length (getf args :bytes)) 4096)
                        (every (lambda (b) (typep b '(unsigned-byte 8))) (getf args :bytes)))
             (error "Invalid input bytes")))

          (:stop (unless (member (getf args :force) '(nil t)) (error "Force must be boolean")))
          (:focus (unless pane (error "Focus needs a pane ID")))
          (:close
           (unless (member (getf args :force) '(nil t)) (error "Force must be boolean"))
           (when (member :focus args)
             (let ((replacement (pane-by-id (view-daemon view) (getf args :focus))))
               (unless (and replacement (not (eq replacement (focused-pane view))))
                 (error "Close focus must name a surviving pane")))))
          ((:status :rename) (unless (and (stringp (getf args :text)) (<= (length (getf args :text)) 512))
                              (error "Text must contain at most 512 characters")))
          (:show-menu
           (unless (and (find owner (getf registry :components)
                              :key (lambda (c) (getf c :id)) :test #'equal)
                        (typep (getf args :x) '(integer 0 499))
                        (typep (getf args :y) '(integer 0 299))
                        (<= 1 (length (getf args :spans)) 256))
             (error "Invalid menu owner or geometry"))
           (validate-decoration-spans (getf args :spans) t))
          (:decorate (validate-decoration-spans (getf args :spans)))
          (:notifications
           (unless (if (member (getf args :op) '(:read :dismiss))
                       (typep (getf args :id) '(integer 1))
                       (and (member (getf args :op) '(:read-all :clear)) (null (getf args :id))))
             (error "Invalid notification change")))
          (:pane-note
           (unless (and pane (valid-decoration-text-p (getf args :text))
                        (<= (length (getf args :text)) 512)
                        (typep (getf args :duration) '(integer 1 60000))
                        (listp (getf args :sgr)) (<= (length (getf args :sgr)) 16)
                        (every (lambda (n) (typep n '(integer 0 255))) (getf args :sgr)))
             (error "Invalid pane note")))
          ((:resize :copy-move) (unless (and (integerp (getf args :delta)) (<= -10000 (getf args :delta) 10000))
                                 (error "Invalid movement")))
          (:copy-edge (unless (member (getf args :edge) '(:start :end)) (error "Invalid edge")))
          (:set-layout (validate-layout-tree (view-daemon view) (getf args :tree)))
          (:split
           (unless (member (getf args :axis) '(:columns :rows)) (error "Invalid split axis"))
           (when (>= (length (daemon-panes (view-daemon view))) (option (view-daemon view) :pane-budget 16))
             (error "Workspace pane budget is exhausted"))
           (let ((argv (getf args :argv (option (view-daemon view) :shell '("/bin/sh" "-i")))))
             (unless (and (listp argv) (<= 1 (length argv) 64)
                          (every (lambda (s) (and (stringp s) (<= (length s) 4096) (not (find #\Null s)))) argv)
                          (plusp (length (first argv)))) (error "Invalid executable argument vector"))))))
    (when (> primary-count 1)
      (error "A batch may contain one view action plus status contributions"))
    (when (and (plusp primary-count) mode-before-primary)
      (error "A keymap transition must follow the view action"))
      (let ((state-action (find :set-state actions :key #'first)))
      (when (and state-action (not staging))
        (let* ((value (getf (rest state-action) :value))
               (entries (remove owner (retained-owner-entries (view-component-state view) registry)
                                :key #'car :test #'equal)))
          (when value
            (push (cons owner value) entries))
          (when (> (component-state-bytes entries) 16384)
            (error "Aggregate component state exceeds 16 KiB")))))
    ;; Contributions are bounded across the retained owner set as well as per
    ;; action. The invoking owner's replacement is staged in this calculation,
    ;; so a rejected batch cannot evict its previous contribution.
    (let ((replacement nil))
      (dolist (a actions)
        (when (eq (first a) :decorate)
          (setf replacement (copy-tree (getf (rest a) :spans)))))
      (when replacement
        (let ((entries (remove owner (unless staging (retained-owner-entries (view-decorations view) registry))
                              :key #'car :test #'equal)))
          (push (cons owner replacement) entries)
          (let ((count 0) (text-length 0))
            (dolist (entry entries)
              (incf count (length (rest entry)))
              (dolist (span (rest entry))
                (let ((rows (getf span :rows 1)))
                  (incf text-length (* rows (length (getf span :text)))))))
            (when (> count 1024) (error "At most 1024 retained decoration spans are allowed"))
            (when (> text-length 16000) (error "Retained decoration text exceeds 16000 characters")))))))))
(defun apply-actions (view owner actions hook origin)
  (let ((origin (or origin (capture-origin view))))
    (unless (and (eq view (command-origin-view origin)) (origin-current-p origin))
      (error "Action origin is no longer current"))
    (validate-actions view actions hook owner)
    (when (eq hook :layout)
      (commit-layout view owner (getf (rest (first actions)) :placements) (getf (rest (first actions)) :camera))
      (return-from apply-actions))
  ;; Complete a mode transition only after the primary action succeeds. This
  ;; keeps mode and status contributions out of a batch whose primary action
  ;; raises after partially updating its own state.
  (let ((primary (find-if (lambda (a) (and (not (member (first a) '(:status :decorate :show-menu :pane-note :notifications :set-state :store-set)))
                                           (not (eq (first a) :set-keymap)))) actions))
        (mode-transition (find-if (lambda (a) (eq (first a) :set-keymap)) actions)))
    (when primary
      (let* ((transition (capture-transition view primary))
             (result (apply-primary-action view (first primary) (rest primary) origin)))
        (when (pending-split-p result)
          (setf (pending-split-owner result) owner
                (pending-split-actions result) (remove primary actions :count 1))
          (return-from apply-actions :pending))
        (when (origin-current-p origin) (start-transition view transition))))
    (when mode-transition
      (apply-primary-action view (first mode-transition) (rest mode-transition) origin)))
  (let ((changed (notevery (lambda (a) (member (first a) '(:status :decorate))) actions)))
    (dolist (a actions)
      (case (first a)
        (:notifications (change-notifications (view-daemon view) (getf (rest a) :op) (getf (rest a) :id)))
        (:show-menu (open-popup view owner (getf (rest a) :spans)
                                (getf (rest a) :x) (getf (rest a) :y)))
        (:pane-note
         (let* ((args (rest a))
                (existing (find-if (lambda (note)
                                     (and (equal owner (getf note :owner))
                                          (eql (getf args :pane) (getf note :pane))))
                                   (view-pane-notes view))))
           ;; Repeating an identical active note leaves its deadline intact.
           (unless (and existing (> (getf existing :until) (now))
                        (equal (getf args :text) (getf existing :text))
                        (equal (getf args :sgr) (getf existing :sgr)))
             (setf (view-pane-notes view)
                   (cons (list :owner owner :pane (getf args :pane)
                               :text (copy-seq (getf args :text)) :sgr (copy-list (getf args :sgr))
                               :until (+ (now) (/ (getf args :duration) 1000)))
                         (remove existing (view-pane-notes view)))))))
        (:status
         (let ((text (getf (rest a) :text)) (entry (assoc owner (view-contributions view) :test #'equal)))
           (unless (and entry (equal (rest entry) text))
             (setf changed t
                   (view-contributions view)
                   (acons owner text (remove owner (view-contributions view) :key #'car :test #'equal))))))
        (:decorate
         (let ((spans (getf (rest a) :spans)) (entry (assoc owner (view-decorations view) :test #'equal)))
           (unless (and entry (equal (rest entry) spans))
             (setf changed t)
             (note-decorations view owner (rest entry) spans)
             (setf (view-decorations view)
                   (acons owner (copy-tree spans) (remove owner (view-decorations view) :key #'car :test #'equal))))))
        (:set-state
         (let ((value (getf (rest a) :value)))
           (setf (view-component-state view)
                 (remove owner (view-component-state view) :key #'car :test #'equal))
           (when value
             (push (cons owner (copy-component-state value))
                   (view-component-state view)))))
        (:store-set
         (multiple-value-bind (namespace entries)
             (store-set (view-daemon view) owner (getf (rest a) :key) (getf (rest a) :value))
           (setf (daemon-store-entries (view-daemon view))
                 (acons namespace entries
                        (remove namespace (daemon-store-entries (view-daemon view)) :key #'car :test #'equal)))
           (when *store-persist*
             (handler-case (ekko/store:store-write namespace entries)
               (error (e) (note-error view (format nil "store write failed: ~A" e)))))))
        (:set-geometry
         (let ((value (validate-geometry-value (getf (rest a) :value))))
           (setf (view-geometry-contributions view)
                 (remove owner (view-geometry-contributions view) :key #'car :test #'equal))
           (when value
             (push (cons owner value) (view-geometry-contributions view)))
           (layout view)))))
    (when changed
      (incf (view-revision view))
      (reconcile-size (view-daemon view))))))
(defun ensure-pane-close (view pane force)
  (unless force
    (when (some (lambda (other)
                  (and (not (eq other view)) (view-live-p other) (member pane (visible-panes other))))
                (daemon-views (view-daemon view)))
      (error "Pane is shown by another view; use force to close it"))))
(defun apply-primary-action (view op args origin)
  (let* ((daemon (view-daemon view))
         (pane (unless (member op '(:set-keymap :set-layout :set-geometry :detach :stop :reload))
                 (if (getf args :pane)
                     (or (pane-by-id daemon (getf args :pane)) (error "Unknown pane"))
                     (origin-target origin))))
         (id (and pane (pane-id pane)))
         (state (and pane (pane-state view id))))
    (case op
      (:set-keymap
       (setf (view-mode view) (getf args :name) (view-prefix view) nil
             (view-key-fragment view) nil (view-key-fragment-mode view) nil))
      (:set-layout
       (unless (equal (command-origin-layout origin) (daemon-tree daemon))
         (error "Layout changed before the command completed"))
       (setf (daemon-tree daemon) (copy-tree (getf args :tree)))
       (layout-all-views daemon))
      (:split
       (when (>= (length (daemon-panes daemon)) (option daemon :pane-budget 16))
         (error "Workspace pane budget is exhausted"))
       (let* ((argv (copy-list (getf args :argv (option daemon :shell '("/bin/sh" "-i")))))
              (new-id (allocate-pane-id daemon))
              (new (make-pane :id new-id :daemon daemon :argv argv
                              :label (file-namestring (first argv))
                              :vt (make-terminal :cols (terminal-cols (pane-vt pane))
                                                 :rows (terminal-rows (pane-vt pane))
                                                 :cw (view-cw view) :ch (view-ch view))
                              :launch-kind (if (member :argv args) :command :shell)
                              :creation-position (1+ (length (daemon-panes daemon)))))
              (pending (make-pending-split :pane new :origin origin)))
         (setf (daemon-tree daemon)
               (ekko/layout:split-pane (daemon-tree daemon) id new-id (getf args :axis))
               (daemon-panes daemon) (append (daemon-panes daemon) (list new))
               (view-zoom view) nil)
         (push pending (daemon-pending-splits daemon))
         (layout-all-views daemon)
         (set-focus view new-id t)
         pending))
      (:close
       (ensure-pane-close view pane (getf args :force))
       (if (= (length (daemon-panes daemon)) 1)
           (request-stop daemon view (getf args :force))
           (progn
             (close-pane pane)
             (clear-screen (pane-graphics pane) :main) (clear-screen (pane-graphics pane) :alternate)
             (when (pane-pid pane) (push (cons pane (+ (now) 1)) (daemon-retired daemon)))
             (setf (daemon-panes daemon) (remove pane (daemon-panes daemon))
                   (daemon-tree daemon) (ekko/layout:remove-pane (daemon-tree daemon) id))
             (dolist (other (daemon-views daemon)) (prune-view-pane other id))
             (when (getf args :focus) (set-focus view (getf args :focus)))
             (layout-all-views daemon))))
      (:rename
       (setf (pane-label pane) (getf args :text) (pane-name pane) (getf args :text))
       (incf (daemon-revision daemon)))
      (:focus (set-focus view id))
      (:focus-next
       (let* ((panes (view-panes view)) (at (or (position pane panes) 0)))
         (set-focus view (pane-id (nth (mod (1+ at) (length panes)) panes)))))
      (:float
       (setf (pane-floating pane)
             (clamp-window-rect view (or (getf args :rect) (pane-floating pane)
               (list (+ (pane-view-outer-x state) 3) (+ (pane-view-outer-y state) 2)
                     (max 20 (floor (* (pane-view-outer-cols state) 3) 4))
                     (max 8 (floor (* (pane-view-outer-rows state) 3) 4)))))
             (pane-view-minimized state) nil (view-zoom view) nil)
       (set-focus view id) (record-activation view pane) (layout-all-views daemon))
      (:tile
       (setf (pane-floating pane) nil (pane-view-minimized state) nil (view-zoom view) nil)
       (set-focus view id) (layout-all-views daemon))
      (:place-window
       (let* ((target (getf args :target)) (edge (getf args :edge)))
         (unless (pane-by-id daemon target) (error "Unknown pane"))
         (setf (daemon-tree daemon)
           (if (eq edge :center) (ekko/layout:swap-panes (daemon-tree daemon) id target)
               (labels ((insert-at (node)
                          (if (integerp node)
                              (if (= node target)
                                  (list (if (member edge '(:left :right)) :columns :rows) 50
                                        (if (member edge '(:left :top)) id target)
                                        (if (member edge '(:left :top)) target id)) node)
                              (list (first node) (second node) (insert-at (third node)) (insert-at (fourth node))))))
                 (insert-at (ekko/layout:remove-pane (daemon-tree daemon) id)))))
         (setf (pane-floating pane) nil (pane-view-minimized state) nil (view-zoom view) nil)
         (set-focus view id) (layout-all-views daemon)))
      (:minimize
       (setf (pane-view-minimized state) t (view-zoom view) nil
             (view-drag view) nil (view-paste-target view) nil)
       (let ((next (find-if-not (lambda (p) (pane-view-minimized (pane-state view (pane-id p)))) (view-panes view))))
         (when (and next (eql id (view-focus-id view))) (set-focus view (pane-id next))))
       (layout view))
      (:restore (setf (pane-view-minimized state) nil (view-zoom view) nil) (set-focus view id) (layout view))
      (:zoom
       (let ((restore (and (view-zoom view) (eql id (view-focus-id view)))))
         (setf (pane-view-minimized state) nil)
         (set-focus view id) (setf (view-zoom view) (not restore)))
       (layout view))
      (:swap
       (let ((panes (daemon-panes daemon)))
         (when (> (length panes) 1)
           (setf (daemon-tree daemon)
                 (ekko/layout:swap-panes (daemon-tree daemon) id
                   (pane-id (nth (mod (1+ (position pane panes)) (length panes)) panes))))
           (layout-all-views daemon))))
      (:resize
       (setf (daemon-tree daemon) (ekko/layout:resize-split (daemon-tree daemon) id (getf args :delta)))
       (layout-all-views daemon))
      (:detach
       (when (view-wire view) (setf (wire-closing (view-wire view)) t))
       (detach-view view))
      (:stop (request-stop daemon view (getf args :force)))
      (:reload (start-reload daemon (command-origin-peer origin)))
      (:help
       (enter-copy view pane
         (coerce (cons "Registered prefix bindings (q exits)"
                       (loop for b in (reverse (getf (daemon-registry daemon) :bindings))
                             when (eq (getf b :map) :prefix) collect
                             (format nil "~A -> ~A (~A)" (getf b :key) (getf b :command) (getf b :owner)))) 'vector)))
      (:send-input (pane-input pane (octets-from-list (getf args :bytes))))
      (:copy-point (point-copy view pane (getf args :x) (getf args :y) (getf args :start)))
      (:copy-scroll (scroll-copy view pane (getf args :delta)))
      (:copy-mode (enter-copy view pane))
      (:copy-exit (leave-copy view pane))
      (:copy-move (when (pane-view-copy-lines state) (move-copy view pane (+ (pane-view-copy-cursor state) (getf args :delta)))))
      (:copy-edge (when (pane-view-copy-lines state)
                    (move-copy view pane (if (eq (getf args :edge) :start) 0 (1- (length (pane-view-copy-lines state)))))))
      (:copy-mark (when (pane-view-copy-lines state) (setf (pane-view-copy-mark state) (pane-view-copy-cursor state))))
      (:copy-selection
       (when (pane-view-copy-lines state)
         (publish-copy view (selected-copy-text view pane))
         (setf (pane-view-copy-flash-until state) (+ (now) 1/5))))
      (:copy-search (when (pane-view-copy-lines state) (setf (pane-view-search-input state) (octets 0))))
      (:copy-search-next (search-copy view pane))
      (:paste-buffer
       (let* ((text (daemon-clipboard daemon)) (bytes (text-bytes text))
              (bracketed (gethash 2004 (terminal-modes (pane-vt pane)))))
         (when (> (length bytes) 60000) (error "Paste exceeds pane input capacity; export with ekko buffer"))
         (pane-input pane (if bracketed
                              (concatenate '(vector (unsigned-byte 8)) #(27 91 50 48 48 126) bytes #(27 91 50 48 49 126)) bytes)))))))
(defun pending-layout-ready-p (pending)
  (let* ((pane (pending-split-pane pending)) (daemon (pane-daemon pane)) (ready t))
    ;; Wait for every participating live/home view. A quarantined view keeps
    ;; old output but cannot supply a layout for this new pane.
    (dolist (view (daemon-views daemon) ready)
      (when (and (not (view-quarantined view)) (member pane (view-panes view))
                 (or (view-live-p view) (eq view (daemon-home-view (view-daemon view)))))
        (let ((state (view-layout-state view)))
          ;; Visit all participants even if an earlier layout is still pending.
          ;; A failed peer must reject startup rather than block it indefinitely.
          (when (layout-state-error state)
            (error "View ~A layout failed before pane startup: ~A" (view-id view) (layout-state-error state)))
          (unless (layout-state-current-p state (daemon-registry (view-daemon view))
                                          (context-data view) (layout-token view))
            (setf ready nil)))))))

(defun discard-pending-pane (daemon pane)
  (when (member pane (daemon-panes daemon))
    (setf (daemon-panes daemon) (remove pane (daemon-panes daemon))
          (daemon-tree daemon) (ekko/layout:remove-pane (daemon-tree daemon) (pane-id pane)))
    (dolist (view (daemon-views daemon)) (prune-view-pane view (pane-id pane)))
    (layout-all-views daemon)))
(defun complete-pending-splits (daemon)
  (dolist (pending (copy-list (daemon-pending-splits daemon)))
    (let* ((origin (pending-split-origin pending)) (view (command-origin-view origin))
           (pane (pending-split-pane pending)) (state (view-layout-state view)))
      (handler-case
          (progn
            (unless (origin-current-p origin) (error "View changed before pane startup"))
            (when (layout-state-error state) (error "Layout failed before pane startup: ~A" (layout-state-error state)))
            (when (pending-layout-ready-p pending)
              (spawn-pane pane)
              (setf (daemon-pending-splits daemon) (remove pending (daemon-pending-splits daemon)))
              (apply-actions view (pending-split-owner pending) (pending-split-actions pending) nil origin)
              (command-reply (command-origin-peer origin) "")
              (replay-input view)))
        (error (e)
          (setf (daemon-pending-splits daemon) (remove pending (daemon-pending-splits daemon)))
          (close-pane pane)
          (when (pane-pid pane) (push (cons pane (+ (now) 1)) (daemon-retired daemon)))
          (discard-pending-pane daemon pane)
          (when (origin-current-p origin) (note-error view e))
          (command-reply (command-origin-peer origin) (princ-to-string e) t)
          (replay-input view))))))

(defun enter-copy (view pane &optional lines)
  (leave-copy view pane)
  (unless lines
    (setf (pane-view-copy-cells (pane-state view (pane-id pane))) (history-cells (pane-vt pane))
          lines (map 'vector #'ekko/vt:row-text (pane-view-copy-cells (pane-state view (pane-id pane))))))
  (setf (pane-view-copy-lines (pane-state view (pane-id pane))) lines (pane-view-copy-mark (pane-state view (pane-id pane))) nil (pane-view-search-input (pane-state view (pane-id pane))) nil)
  (move-copy view pane (1- (length lines))))
(defun move-copy (view pane cursor)
  (setf (pane-view-copy-cursor (pane-state view (pane-id pane))) (max 0 (min (1- (length (pane-view-copy-lines (pane-state view (pane-id pane))))) cursor))
        (pane-view-copy-top (pane-state view (pane-id pane))) (max 0 (min (pane-view-copy-top (pane-state view (pane-id pane))) (pane-view-copy-cursor (pane-state view (pane-id pane))))))
  (when (>= (pane-view-copy-cursor (pane-state view (pane-id pane))) (+ (pane-view-copy-top (pane-state view (pane-id pane))) (pane-view-rows (pane-state view (pane-id pane)))))
    (setf (pane-view-copy-top (pane-state view (pane-id pane))) (1+ (- (pane-view-copy-cursor (pane-state view (pane-id pane))) (pane-view-rows (pane-state view (pane-id pane))))))))
(defun clip-copy-text (text cols)
  (let ((width 0) (end 0))
    (loop for c across text for n = (ekko/vt:character-width c) do
      (when (> (+ width n) cols) (return))
      (incf width n) (incf end))
    (subseq text 0 end)))
(defun search-copy (view pane)
  (when (and (pane-view-copy-lines (pane-state view (pane-id pane))) (plusp (length (pane-view-search-text (pane-state view (pane-id pane))))))
    (let* ((lines (pane-view-copy-lines (pane-state view (pane-id pane))))
           (match (loop for step from 1 to (length lines) for i = (mod (+ (pane-view-copy-cursor (pane-state view (pane-id pane))) step) (length lines))
                        when (search (pane-view-search-text (pane-state view (pane-id pane))) (aref lines i) :test #'char-equal) return i)))
      (when match (move-copy view pane match)))))
(defun search-input-text (bytes)
  ;; Reads and the bounded input buffer can end inside a UTF-8 character.
  (loop for end from (length bytes) downto (max 0 (- (length bytes) 4))
        for text = (handler-case (bytes-text (subseq bytes 0 end)) (error () nil))
        when text return text finally (return "")))
(defun copy-search-input (view bytes)
  (let ((pane (focused-pane view)))
    (loop for byte across bytes do
      (case byte
        (27 (setf (pane-view-search-input (pane-state view (pane-id pane))) nil) (return))
        ((10 13) (setf (pane-view-search-text (pane-state view (pane-id pane))) (search-input-text (pane-view-search-input (pane-state view (pane-id pane)))) (pane-view-search-input (pane-state view (pane-id pane))) nil)
                 (search-copy view pane) (return))
        ((8 127) (let* ((input (pane-view-search-input (pane-state view (pane-id pane)))) (end (1- (length input))))
                   (when (>= end 0)
                     (loop while (and (plusp end) (= (logand (aref input end) #xc0) #x80)) do (decf end))
                     (setf (pane-view-search-input (pane-state view (pane-id pane))) (subseq input 0 end)))))
        (t (when (< (length (pane-view-search-input (pane-state view (pane-id pane)))) 256)
             (setf (pane-view-search-input (pane-state view (pane-id pane))) (concatenate '(vector (unsigned-byte 8)) (pane-view-search-input (pane-state view (pane-id pane))) (octets-from-list (list byte))))))))
    (incf (view-revision view))))
(defun status-text (view &optional (registry (daemon-registry (view-daemon view)))
                                  (contributions (view-contributions view)))
  (let* ((panes (view-panes view))
         (pane (or (find (view-focus-id view) panes :key #'pane-id) (first panes)))
         (state (and pane (pane-state view (pane-id pane) nil))))
    (cond ((and state (pane-view-search-input state))
           (format nil "Search: ~A" (search-input-text (pane-view-search-input state))))
          ((and state (pane-view-copy-lines state))
           "Copy: j/k move  b/f page  Space mark  Enter copy  / search  n next  q exit")
          ((plusp (length (view-notice view))) (view-notice view))
          (t (or (loop for component in (reverse (getf registry :components))
                       for contribution = (assoc (getf component :id) contributions :test #'equal)
                       when contribution return (rest contribution))
                 (getf (getf registry :options) :status-text ""))))))
(defun binding-key-label (key)
  "Render a binding key (plain key or (MODS . KEY) chord) as plain text."
  (cond ((consp key)
         (let* ((code (rest key))
                (named (cdr (assoc code '((9 . "Tab") (13 . "Enter") (27 . "Escape") (127 . "Backspace")))))
                ;; A chord whose key part folded to a control code prints as Ctrl-<char>,
                ;; lowercase to match the shifted letter it stands for.
                (control (and (integerp code) (<= 1 code 26)
                              (not (member code '(9 10 13 27)))))
                (name (cond ((keywordp code) (string-downcase (symbol-name code)))
                            (control (string (char-downcase (code-char (+ 64 code)))))
                            (named named)
                            ((and (integerp code) (<= 32 code 126)) (string (code-char code)))
                            (t (princ-to-string code)))))
           (concatenate 'string (if (logtest 1 (first key)) "M-" "")
                        (if (logtest 2 (first key)) "Super-" "")
                        (if (logtest 8 (first key)) "Ctrl-" "")
                        (if control "Ctrl-" "")
                        (if (logtest 4 (first key)) "Shift-" "") name)))
        ((and (integerp key) (<= 1 key 26) (not (member key '(9 10 13 27))))
         (format nil "C-~C" (char-downcase (code-char (+ 64 key)))))
        ((and (integerp key) (<= 32 key 126)) (string (code-char key)))
        ((keywordp key) (string-downcase (symbol-name key)))
        (t (princ-to-string key))))
(defun json-string (string)
  (with-output-to-string (out)
    (write-char #\" out)
    (loop for c across string do
      (case c (#\" (write-string "\\\"" out)) (#\\ (write-string "\\\\" out))
            (otherwise (if (< (char-code c) 32) (format out "\\u~4,'0X" (char-code c)) (write-char c out)))))
    (write-char #\" out)))
(defun json-value (value)
  (cond ((null value) "null") ((eq value :false) "false") ((eq value t) "true") ((stringp value) (json-string value))
        ((keywordp value) (json-string (string-downcase (symbol-name value))))
        ((floatp value) (format nil "~F" value)) ((integerp value) (princ-to-string value))
        ((and (consp value) (eq (first value) :object))
         (format nil "{~{~A~^,~}}" (loop for (key val) on (rest value) by #'cddr
                                        collect (format nil "~A:~A" (json-value key) (json-value val)))))
        ((listp value) (format nil "[~{~A~^,~}]" (mapcar #'json-value value)))
        ((vectorp value) (format nil "[~{~A~^,~}]" (map 'list #'json-value value)))
        (t (error "Not JSON data"))))
(defun component-state-json-value (value)
  ;; Plain state must not be interpreted as the inspector's :object/:false
  ;; representation. Preserve keyword values as strings and lists as arrays.
  (cond ((consp value) (map 'vector #'component-state-json-value value))
        ((keywordp value) (string-downcase (symbol-name value)))
        (t value)))
(defun inspect-json (view)
  (let* ((daemon (view-daemon view))
         (registry (daemon-registry daemon))
         (context (context-data view)))
    (labels ((pane-json-data (pane)
               (cons :object
                     (loop for (key value) on pane by #'cddr append
                       (list (case key
                               (:launch-kind :launch_kind)
                               (:creation-position :creation_position)
                               (:terminal-title :terminal_title)
                               (:activation-order :activation_order)
                               (:layout-rect :layout_rect)
                               (:pty-size :pty_size)
                               (:view-cols :view_cols) (:view-rows :view_rows)
                               (:outer-rect :outer_rect) (:history-rows :history_rows)
                               (otherwise key))
                             (if (member key '(:minimized :activity :visible))
                                 (if value t :false) value))))))
      (json-value
        (list :object :generation (daemon-config-generation daemon)
              :error (or (view-error view) (daemon-config-error daemon))
              :quarantined (if (view-quarantined view) t :false)
              :session (getf context :session) :view_id (view-id view) :epoch (view-epoch view)
              :all-panes (mapcar #'pane-json-data (getf context :all-panes))
              :workspace (cons :object (getf context :workspace))
              :focus (getf context :focus) :mode (getf context :mode)
              :popup (when (view-popup view)
                       (let ((popup (view-popup view)))
                         (list :object :owner (popup-owner popup) :x (popup-x popup) :y (popup-y popup)
                               :selected (popup-selected popup))))
              :zoom (if (getf context :zoom) t :false)
              :viewport (cons :object (getf context :viewport))
              :chrome-status (cons :object (getf context :chrome-status))
              :panes (mapcar #'pane-json-data (getf context :panes))
              :pane-notes (mapcar (lambda (note) (cons :object note)) (getf context :pane-notes))
              :notifications (mapcar (lambda (n)
                                       (list :object :id (getf n :id) :pane (getf n :pane) :time (getf n :time)
                                             :title (getf n :title) :body (getf n :body) :count (getf n :count)
                                             :bell (if (getf n :bell) t :false) :read (if (getf n :read) t :false)))
                                     (getf context :notifications))
              :geometry (list* :object :contributions
                               (loop for (owner . value) in (view-geometry-contributions view)
                                     collect (list :object :owner owner :value (copy-component-state value)))
                               (getf context :geometry))
              :keymaps (mapcar (lambda (m) (cons :object m)) (getf registry :keymaps))
              :disabled-hooks (daemon-disabled-hooks daemon)
              :decorations (loop for (owner . spans) in (view-decorations view)
                                 collect (list :object :owner owner
                                               :spans (loop for span in spans collect
                                                        (list :object :x (getf span :x)
                                                              :y (getf span :y)
                                                              :text (getf span :text)
                                                              :action (getf span :action)
                                                              :command (getf span :command)
                                                              :has_menu (if (getf span :context-command) t :false)
                                                              :sgr (getf span :sgr)
                                                              :rows (getf span :rows 1)
                                                              :overlay (if (getf span :overlay) t :false)))))
              :contributions (loop for (owner . text) in (view-contributions view)
                                   collect (list :object :owner owner :text text))
              :component-state (loop for (owner . value) in (getf context :component-state)
                                    collect (list :object :owner owner :value (component-state-json-value value)))
              :options (cons :object (getf registry :options)) :layout (getf context :layout)
              :components (mapcar (lambda (c) (cons :object c)) (getf registry :components))
              :commands (mapcar (lambda (c) (cons :object c)) (getf registry :commands))
              :bindings (mapcar (lambda (entry)
                                  (cons :object
                                        (loop for (key value) on entry by #'cddr
                                              append (list key (if (eq key :key)
                                                                   (binding-key-label value) value)))))
                                (getf registry :bindings)))))))
(defun split-arguments (bytes)
  (loop for start = 0 then (1+ end) for end = (position 0 bytes :start start)
        collect (bytes-text (subseq bytes start end)) while end))
#+end_src

** src/menus.lisp
Attachment-local popups, placed and routed by the host.

#+begin_src lisp :tangle src/menus.lisp :mkdirp yes
(in-package #:ekko/runtime)

;; A popup is attachment-local interaction state. Its spans, styles and actions
;; come from an ordinary decoration owner; the host only places and routes them.
(defstruct popup owner spans x y width height (scroll 0) selected pressed)
(defun span-width (span)
  (loop for c across (getf span :text) sum (ekko/vt:character-width c)))
(defun span-hit-p (span x y)
  (and (<= (getf span :x) x) (< x (+ (getf span :x) (span-width span)))
       (<= (getf span :y) y) (< y (+ (getf span :y) (getf span :rows 1)))))
(defun span-control-p (span)
  (or (getf span :action) (getf span :command)))
(defun activate-span (view owner span)
  (handler-case
      (if (getf span :command)
          (request-command view (getf span :command)
                           (list :arguments (getf span :arguments)) nil)
          (when (getf span :action)
            (apply-actions view owner (list (getf span :action)) nil nil)))
    (error (e) (note-error view e))))
(defun forget-popup-glows (view)
  (setf (view-glows view) (remove :popup (view-glows view) :key #'caar)))
(defun close-popup (view)
  (when (view-popup view)
    ;; A closed menu fades out where it was, without taking input.
    (when (plusp (animation-seconds view))
      (setf (view-popup-ghost view) (list (view-popup view) (now))))
    (setf (view-popup view) nil)
    (forget-popup-glows view)
    (incf (view-revision view))))
(defun open-popup (view owner spans x y)
  (let* ((width (min (view-cols view)
                     (loop for span in spans maximize (+ (getf span :x) (span-width span)))))
         (height (min (view-rows view)
                      (loop for span in spans maximize (+ (getf span :y) (getf span :rows 1))))))
    (setf (view-popup view)
          (make-popup :owner owner :spans (copy-tree spans)
                      :x (max 0 (min x (- (view-cols view) width)))
                      :y (max 0 (min y (- (view-rows view) height)))
                      :width width :height height)
          (view-drag view) nil (view-transition view) nil)
    (forget-popup-glows view)
    (incf (view-revision view))))
(defun popup-select (view index)
  (let* ((popup (view-popup view)) (span (and index (nth index (popup-spans popup)))))
    (unless (eql index (popup-selected popup))
      (when (popup-selected popup) (set-glow view (list :popup (popup-selected popup)) nil))
      (when index (set-glow view (list :popup index) t))
      (setf (popup-selected popup) index)
      (when span
        (setf (popup-scroll popup)
              (max 0 (min (getf span :y)
                          (max (popup-scroll popup) (- (1+ (getf span :y)) (popup-height popup)))))))
      (incf (view-revision view)))))
(defun popup-cycle (view delta)
  (let* ((popup (view-popup view))
         (indices (loop for span in (popup-spans popup) for index from 0
                        when (span-control-p span) collect index))
         (at (position (popup-selected popup) indices)))
    (when indices
      (popup-select view (nth (mod (+ (or at (if (plusp delta) -1 0)) delta)
                                      (length indices)) indices)))))
(defun popup-key (view key)
  (when (view-popup view)
    ;; Up/Down, Tab and the vim keys j/k move the picker selection; a span's
    ;; own :key activates it directly.
    (let* ((popup (view-popup view))
           (keyed (and (integerp key)
                       (find (string (code-char key)) (popup-spans popup)
                             :key (lambda (span) (getf span :key)) :test #'equal))))
      (cond ((member key '(27 7)) (close-popup view))
            (keyed (close-popup view) (activate-span view (popup-owner popup) keyed))
            ((member key '(:down 9 106)) (popup-cycle view 1))
            ((member key '(:up 107)) (popup-cycle view -1))
            ((eql key 13)
             (let ((span (and (popup-selected popup) (nth (popup-selected popup) (popup-spans popup)))))
               (close-popup view)
               (when span (activate-span view (popup-owner popup) span))))))
    t))
(defun popup-mouse (view button x y up)
  (let ((popup (view-popup view)))
    (when popup
      (let* ((inside (and (<= (popup-x popup) x) (< x (+ (popup-x popup) (popup-width popup)))
                          (<= (popup-y popup) y) (< y (+ (popup-y popup) (popup-height popup)))))
             (index (when inside
                      (position-if (lambda (span)
                                     (and (span-control-p span)
                                          (span-hit-p span (- x (popup-x popup))
                                                      (+ (- y (popup-y popup)) (popup-scroll popup)))))
                                   (popup-spans popup)))))
        (cond ((and (not up) (member button '(64 65))) (popup-cycle view (if (= button 64) -1 1)))
              ((and (not up) (< button 3) (not inside))
               (close-popup view) (setf (view-chrome-press view) :dismiss))
              ((= button 0)
               (if up
                   (let ((pressed (popup-pressed popup)))
                     (setf (popup-pressed popup) nil)
                     (when (and pressed (eql pressed index))
                       (let ((span (nth index (popup-spans popup))))
                         (close-popup view)
                         (activate-span view (popup-owner popup) span))))
                   (setf (popup-pressed popup) index))
               (when (view-popup view) (popup-select view index)))
              ((logbitp 5 button) (popup-select view index))))
      t)))
(defun popup-pieces (view popup &optional fading)
  "POPUP's cells: entering, or FADING out at that opacity. The selection's
highlight glows in and out rather than jumping between rows."
  (loop for span in (popup-spans popup) for index from 0
        for row = (- (getf span :y) (popup-scroll popup))
        when (<= 0 row (1- (popup-height popup)))
          append (multiple-value-bind (dx dy entering) (if fading (values 0 0 fading) (motion-offset view span))
                   (let ((level (if fading
                                    (if (eql index (popup-selected popup)) 1 0)
                                    (glow-at view (list :popup index)))))
                     (clip-decoration view nil
                       (list :x (+ (popup-x popup) (getf span :x) dx) :y (+ (popup-y popup) row dy)
                             :text (getf span :text)
                             :sgr (blend-sgr (getf span :sgr) (getf span :hover-sgr (getf span :sgr)) level)
                             :alpha entering))))))
(defun popup-overlays (view)
  (append
   (destructuring-bind (&optional ghost started) (view-popup-ghost view)
     (when ghost
       (popup-pieces view ghost (max 1/100 (- 1 (ease-out (animation-progress started (animation-seconds view 3/4))))))))
   (when (view-popup view) (popup-pieces view (view-popup view)))))
#+end_src

** src/animations.lisp
Motion: continuous fades, glows, entrances, exits, window slides and transitions.

#+begin_src lisp :tangle src/animations.lisp :mkdirp yes
(in-package #:ekko/runtime)

;; Motion is presentation. Logical layout commits once; every effect here is a
;; function of committed state and the clock, and ticks never touch
;; application VT or PTY geometry. Progress is continuous: while anything
;; moves the daemon publishes at display rate, and the client's row diff sends
;; only the rows that changed. Cells cannot move by less than a cell, but
;; truecolour can change by any amount, so every effect that can be a fade is.
(defparameter +frame-seconds+ 1/60)
(defun ease-out (p) (- 1 (expt (- 1 (max 0 (min 1 p))) 3)))
(defun animation-seconds (view &optional (scale 1))
  (* scale (/ (option (view-daemon view) :window-animation-ms 0) 1000)))
(defun animation-progress (started seconds)
  (if (plusp seconds) (min 1 (/ (- (now) started) seconds)) 1))

;; Colour arithmetic on SGR parameter lists.
(defparameter +system-colours+
  '((0 0 0) (205 0 0) (0 205 0) (205 205 0) (0 0 238) (205 0 205) (0 205 205) (229 229 229)
    (127 127 127) (255 0 0) (0 255 0) (255 255 0) (92 92 255) (255 0 255) (0 255 255) (255 255 255)))
(defun palette-rgb (index)
  "An xterm 256-colour INDEX as (r g b); the 16 system colours take xterm's defaults."
  (cond ((< index 16) (nth index +system-colours+))
        ((< index 232) (let ((i (- index 16)))
                         (mapcar (lambda (c) (if (zerop c) 0 (+ 55 (* 40 c))))
                                 (list (floor i 36) (mod (floor i 6) 6) (mod i 6)))))
        (t (let ((v (+ 8 (* 10 (- index 232))))) (list v v v)))))
(defun rgb (colour) (if (integerp colour) (palette-rgb colour) colour))
(defun split-sgr (sgr)
  "SGR as (values fg bg rest): colours as (r g b) or nil for the default, REST
the remaining codes in order."
  (let (fg bg rest)
    (loop while sgr for code = (pop sgr) do
      (case code
        ((38 48 58)
         (let* ((kind (pop sgr))
                (colour (case kind
                          (5 (let ((index (pop sgr))) (and index (palette-rgb index))))
                          (2 (let ((c (list (pop sgr) (pop sgr) (pop sgr)))) (and (every #'integerp c) c))))))
           (case code (38 (setf fg colour)) (48 (setf bg colour)))))
        (39 (setf fg nil)) (49 (setf bg nil))
        (t (cond ((<= 30 code 37) (setf fg (palette-rgb (- code 30))))
                 ((<= 90 code 97) (setf fg (palette-rgb (- code 82))))
                 ((<= 40 code 47) (setf bg (palette-rgb (- code 40))))
                 ((<= 100 code 107) (setf bg (palette-rgb (- code 92))))
                 (t (push code rest))))))
    (values fg bg (nreverse rest))))
(defun join-sgr (fg bg rest)
  (append rest (when fg (list* 38 2 fg)) (when bg (list* 48 2 bg))))
(defun mix (a b p)
  (mapcar (lambda (x y) (round (+ x (* (- y x) p)))) a b))
(defun blend-sgr (from to p)
  "Style TO with its colours P of the way from FROM's. A colour that one side
leaves to the terminal default cannot be interpolated; it switches halfway."
  (if (or (>= p 1) (equal from to))
      to
      (multiple-value-bind (f1 b1 r1) (split-sgr from)
        (multiple-value-bind (f2 b2 r2) (split-sgr to)
          (flet ((pick (a b) (cond ((and a b) (mix a b p)) ((< p 1/2) a) (t b))))
            (join-sgr (pick f1 f2) (pick b1 b2) (if (< p 1/2) r1 r2)))))))
(defun faded-sgr (sgr alpha ground)
  "SGR seen at opacity ALPHA over GROUND."
  (let ((ground (rgb ground)))
    (multiple-value-bind (fg bg rest) (split-sgr sgr)
      (join-sgr (and fg (mix ground fg alpha)) (mix ground (or bg ground) alpha) rest))))

;; Restyle crossfades. A decoration span that keeps its place and text but
;; changes style (focus moving between title bars, a taskbar entry becoming
;; active) blends from the style it showed to the new one. Keyed by owner,
;; position and text, so anything that moves or rewrites itself just switches.
(defun span-key (owner span)
  (list owner (getf span :x) (getf span :y) (getf span :rows 1) (getf span :text)))
(defun span-style (span) (list (getf span :sgr) (getf span :gradient)))
(defun shown-style (view key style)
  (let ((tween (and (view-tweens view) (gethash key (view-tweens view)))))
    (if (and tween (equal (second tween) style))
        (destructuring-bind (from to started) tween
          (let ((p (ease-out (animation-progress started (animation-seconds view)))))
            (list (blend-sgr (first from) (first to) p)
                  (and (second to) (blend-sgr (or (second from) (first from)) (second to) p)))))
        style)))
(defun note-decorations (view owner old new)
  "Start crossfades, exit ghosts and slide bookkeeping as OWNER's spans change."
  (let ((seconds (animation-seconds view)))
    (push (cons owner (now)) (view-redecorated view))
    (setf (view-redecorated view) (remove-duplicates (view-redecorated view) :key #'car :test #'equal :from-end t))
    (when (and (plusp seconds) old)
      (let ((before (make-hash-table :test 'equal)))
        (dolist (span old)
          (let ((key (span-key owner span)))
            (setf (gethash key before) (shown-style view key (span-style span)))))
        (dolist (span new)
          (let* ((key (span-key owner span)) (from (gethash key before)) (to (span-style span)))
            (when (and from (not (equal from to)))
              (unless (view-tweens view) (setf (view-tweens view) (make-hash-table :test 'equal)))
              (setf (gethash key (view-tweens view)) (list from to (now)))))))
      ;; Entrance groups that disappear leave through the way they came.
      (let ((kept (loop for span in new when (getf span :enter) collect (first (getf span :enter)))))
        (loop for span in old for enter = (getf span :enter)
              when (and enter (not (member (first enter) kept :test #'equal)))
                do (let ((ghost (copy-list span)))
                     (remf ghost :pane)
                     (push (list owner ghost (now)) (view-ghosts view))))))))
(defun ghost-spans (view)
  "Departed entrance spans, as (owner span dx dy alpha)."
  (loop with seconds = (animation-seconds view 3/4)
        for (owner span started) in (view-ghosts view)
        for p = (ease-out (animation-progress started seconds))
        when (< p 1)
          collect (destructuring-bind (id dx dy) (getf span :enter)
                    (declare (ignore id))
                    (list owner span (round (* dx p 1/2)) (round (* dy p 1/2)) (- 1 p)))))

;; Glows: pointer and keyboard highlight that arrives quickly and leaves
;; slowly, so a sweep across a taskbar or a menu trails light behind it.
;; Each glow is (key level started target span owner).
(defun glow-level (view glow)
  (destructuring-bind (key level started target &rest more) glow
    (declare (ignore key more))
    (let ((seconds (animation-seconds view (if target 1/3 3/2))))
      (if (plusp seconds)
          (let ((step (/ (- (now) started) seconds)))
            (if target (min 1 (+ level step)) (max 0 (- level step))))
          (if target 1 0)))))
(defun set-glow (view key target &optional span owner)
  (let ((old (find key (view-glows view) :key #'first :test #'equal)))
    (unless (and old (eq (fourth old) target))
      (setf (view-glows view)
            (cons (list key (if old (glow-level view old) 0) (now) target span owner)
                  (remove old (view-glows view))))
      (incf (view-revision view)))))
(defun glow-at (view key)
  (let ((glow (find key (view-glows view) :key #'first :test #'equal)))
    (if glow (glow-level view glow) 0)))

;; Entrance motion: spans that declare (:enter (id dx dy)) start offset by
;; (dx dy) and transparent, and ease into place over :window-animation-ms.
;; Each motion is (id started). A group re-enters after it disappears.
(defun motion-offset (view span)
  "SPAN's current (values dx dy alpha), starting its group's motion on first sight."
  (let ((enter (getf span :enter)))
    (if (null enter)
        (values 0 0 nil)
        (destructuring-bind (id dx dy) enter
          (let* ((motion (or (assoc id (view-motions view) :test #'equal)
                             (first (push (list id (now)) (view-motions view)))))
                 (p (ease-out (animation-progress (second motion) (animation-seconds view)))))
            (values (round (* dx (- 1 p))) (round (* dy (- 1 p))) (and (< p 1) p)))))))

;; Window motion. A window shows at its target: the rectangle a drag holds it
;; at, else its committed place. A motion carries it from where it showed to
;; its target, sliding, and growing or shrinking by clipping content that is
;; already at its new size. Chrome follows by nine-slice: rows that span the
;; window stretch in their longest run of one character, spans nearer an
;; edge keep their distance to it, borders that span its height grow with it.
;; A motion is (id from started seconds ref . spans): the rectangle it starts
;; from, and the window's chrome as it was, drawn for rectangle REF, which
;; carries the window until its owners redraw and covers any part of the
;; frame that was off screen on one side of the move.
(defun lerp-rect (a b p) (mapcar (lambda (x y) (round (+ x (* (- y x) p)))) a b))
(defun committed-rect (view id)
  (let ((state (gethash id (view-pane-states view))))
    (and state (list (pane-view-outer-x state) (pane-view-outer-y state)
                     (pane-view-outer-cols state) (pane-view-outer-rows state)))))
(defun drag-rects (view)
  "The rectangles an active drag holds windows at, as (id . rect)."
  (let ((drag (view-window-drag view)))
    (when (and drag (window-drag-moved drag))
      (if (window-drag-split drag)
          (window-drag-preview drag)
          (list (cons (pane-id (window-drag-pane drag)) (window-drag-free drag)))))))
(defun target-rect (view id)
  (or (rest (assoc id (drag-rects view))) (committed-rect view id)))
(defun shown-rect (view id)
  (let ((target (target-rect view id)) (motion (assoc id (view-slides view))))
    (if (and motion target)
        (destructuring-bind (from started seconds &rest more) (rest motion)
          (declare (ignore more))
          (lerp-rect from target (ease-out (animation-progress started seconds))))
        target)))
(defun window-moving-p (view) (or (view-slides view) (view-window-drag view)))
(defun pane-shift (view id)
  "Pane ID shown against its committed place, as (values dx dy dcols drows)."
  (let ((committed (and (window-moving-p view) (committed-rect view id))))
    (if committed
        (values-list (mapcar #'- (shown-rect view id) committed))
        (values 0 0 0 0))))
(defun outer-places (view &optional placed)
  "Shown outer rectangles as (id x y cols rows): of the visible windows, or
with PLACED of every window the layout places."
  (loop for pane in (if placed
                        (loop for placement in (view-layout-placements view)
                              for pane = (pane-by-id (view-daemon view) (getf placement :pane))
                              when (and pane (getf placement :visible t)) collect pane)
                        (visible-panes view))
        collect (cons (pane-id pane) (shown-rect view (pane-id pane)))))
(defun pane-chrome (view id)
  "Every owner's spans that belong to window ID, as (owner . span)."
  (loop for (owner . spans) in (view-decorations view)
        append (loop for span in spans
                     when (eql (or (getf span :pane) (getf span :follows)) id) collect (cons owner span))))
(defun move-window (view id from seconds &optional ref spans)
  (setf (view-slides view)
        (cons (list* id from (now) seconds ref spans) (remove id (view-slides view) :key #'first))))
(defun grown-from (rect before)
  "Where a new window at RECT grows from: the far edge of the window it split."
  (destructuring-bind (x y w h) rect
    (loop for (nil bx by bw bh) in before
          when (and (< bx (+ x w)) (< x (+ bx bw)) (< by (+ y h)) (< y (+ by bh)))
            return (cond ((> x bx) (list (+ bx bw) y 0 h))
                         ((> y by) (list x (+ by bh) w 0))
                         ((< (+ x w) (+ bx bw)) (list x y 0 h))
                         (t (list x y w 0))))))
(defun start-window-motions (view before committed)
  "Move each window from where BEFORE showed it; COMMITTED holds the places
the last layout gave, and a window whose place is unchanged keeps its motion."
  (setf (view-slides view)
        (when (and (plusp (animation-seconds view)) (view-wire view))
          (loop for placement in (view-layout-placements view)
                for id = (getf placement :pane)
                for new = (committed-rect view id)
                for shown = (rest (assoc id before))
                for old = (rest (assoc id committed))
                for motion = (assoc id (view-slides view))
                when (and new (getf placement :visible t))
                  append (cond ((and shown (equal old new)) (and motion (list motion)))
                               (shown (unless (equal shown new)
                                        (list (list* id shown (now) (animation-seconds view 3/2)
                                                     old (pane-chrome view id)))))
                               (t (let ((from (grown-from new before)))
                                    (and from (list (list* id from (now) (animation-seconds view 3/2) nil))))))))))
(defun stretch-text (text delta)
  "TEXT DELTA cells wider or narrower, changed inside its longest run of one character."
  (if (or (zerop delta) (zerop (length text)))
      text
      (let ((best 0) (best-length 0) (start 0))
        (loop for i from 1 to (length text)
              when (or (= i (length text)) (char/= (char text i) (char text start)))
                do (when (> (- i start) best-length) (setf best start best-length (- i start)))
                   (setf start i))
        (if (plusp delta)
            (concatenate 'string (subseq text 0 best) (make-string delta :initial-element (char text best))
                         (subseq text best))
            (let* ((cut (min best-length (- delta)))
                   (shrunk (concatenate 'string (subseq text 0 best) (subseq text (+ best cut)))))
              (subseq shrunk 0 (max 0 (- (length shrunk) (- (- delta) cut)))))))))
(defun fit-span (span ref rect)
  "SPAN, drawn for window rectangle REF, fitted by nine-slice to RECT; nil when nothing is left."
  (if (or (null ref) (equal ref rect))
      span
      (destructuring-bind (rx ry rw rh) ref
        (destructuring-bind (tx ty tw th) rect
          (let* ((x (getf span :x)) (y (getf span :y)) (rows (getf span :rows 1))
                 (width (span-width span)) (text (getf span :text))
                 (left (- x rx)) (right (- (+ rx rw) (+ x width)))
                 (top (- y ry)) (bottom (- (+ ry rh) (+ y rows)))
                 (wide (and (> width 2) (>= width (- rw 2))))
                 (tall (and (> rows 1) (>= rows (- rh 2))))
                 (text (if wide (stretch-text text (- tw rw)) text))
                 (rows (if tall (+ rows (- th rh)) rows)))
            (when (and (plusp (length text)) (plusp rows))
              (let ((fitted (copy-list span)))
                (setf (getf fitted :x) (if (or wide (<= left right)) (+ tx left) (- (+ tx tw) right width))
                      (getf fitted :y) (if (or tall (<= top bottom)) (+ ty top) (- (+ ty th) bottom rows))
                      (getf fitted :text) text)
                (when (or tall (getf span :rows)) (setf (getf fitted :rows) rows))
                fitted)))))))
(defun window-span (view owner span)
  "OWNER's live SPAN where its window shows now; nil when it has no room."
  (let ((id (and (window-moving-p view) (or (getf span :pane) (getf span :follows)))))
    (if id
        (let* ((motion (assoc id (view-slides view)))
               (ref (if (and motion (fifth motion) (not (redecorated-since-p view owner (third motion))))
                        (fifth motion)
                        (committed-rect view id))))
          (fit-span span ref (shown-rect view id)))
        span)))

;; Short window transitions use the overlay channel: a minimize or restore
;; flies the window's title bar between the window and its taskbar entry,
;; fading as it lands, and other geometry changes sweep an outline.
(defstruct transition style from to sgr started duration)
(defun pane-outer-rect (view pane)
  (let ((state (pane-state view (pane-id pane))))
    (list (pane-view-outer-x state) (pane-view-outer-y state)
          (pane-view-outer-cols state) (pane-view-outer-rows state))))
(defun capture-transition (view action)
  (let* ((pane (if (getf (rest action) :pane)
                   (pane-by-id (view-daemon view) (getf (rest action) :pane))
                   (focused-pane view)))
         (state (when pane (pane-state view (pane-id pane))))
         (op (first action)))
    (when (and pane (plusp (animation-seconds view)) (view-wire view)
               (or (member op '(:minimize :restore :zoom :float :tile :place-window))
                   (and (eq op :focus) (pane-view-minimized state))))
      (let* ((spans (loop for entry in (view-decorations view) append (rest entry)))
             (dock (find-if (lambda (span)
                              (and (= (getf span :y) (1- (view-rows view)))
                                   (eql (getf (rest (getf span :action)) :pane) (pane-id pane)))) spans))
             (header (find-if (lambda (span)
                                (and (= (getf span :x) (pane-view-outer-x state))
                                     (= (getf span :y) (pane-view-outer-y state))
                                     (eql (getf (rest (getf span :action)) :pane) (pane-id pane)))) spans))
             (dock-rect (if dock (list (getf dock :x) (getf dock :y) (max 1 (span-width dock)) 1)
                            (list (floor (view-cols view) 2) (1- (view-rows view)) 1 1)))
             (sgr (copy-list (getf (or header dock) :sgr '(0 38 5 245))))
             (caption (and (member op '(:minimize :restore :focus)) (nth-value 1 (split-sgr sgr)))))
        (list pane op (if caption :caption :outline)
              (if (pane-view-minimized state) dock-rect (pane-outer-rect view pane)) dock-rect
              (if caption (join-sgr nil caption nil) sgr))))))
(defun start-transition (view captured)
  (when captured
    (destructuring-bind (pane op style from dock sgr) captured
      (let ((to (if (eq op :minimize) dock (pane-outer-rect view pane))))
        (unless (equal from to)
          (setf (view-transition view)
                (make-transition :style style :from from :to to :sgr sgr :started (now)
                                 :duration (animation-seconds view (if (eq style :caption) 3/2 1)))))))))
(defun transition-overlays (view)
  (let ((transition (view-transition view)))
    (when transition
      (let* ((p (ease-out (animation-progress (transition-started transition)
                                              (transition-duration transition))))
             (rect (mapcar (lambda (a b) (round (+ a (* (- b a) p))))
                           (transition-from transition) (transition-to transition)))
             (sgr (transition-sgr transition)))
